        js="""
        export default function(component) {
          const { parentElement, data } = component;
          const root = parentElement.querySelector('.adv-root');
          const rows = Array.isArray(data?.rows) ? data.rows : [];
          const summary = data?.summary || {};
          const fmt = (v,d=2) => { const n=Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n.toLocaleString(undefined,{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d}) : '-'; };
          const esc = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>\"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','\"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));

          const cards = [
            ['전체 수리부속', `${fmt(summary.total_parts,0)}종`],
            ['장기조달', `${fmt(summary.long_lead_count,0)}종`],
            ['분석기간 예상 고장수요', `${fmt(summary.expected_demand_total,1)}건`],
            ['평균 리드타임', `${fmt(summary.avg_lead_time_h,1)} h`],
          ];
          root.querySelector('.adv-cards').innerHTML = cards.map(([k,v])=>`<div class="adv-card"><div class="adv-card-label">${k}</div><div class="adv-card-value">${v}</div></div>`).join('');

          root.querySelectorAll('.adv-tab').forEach(btn => btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            root.querySelectorAll('.adv-tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===btn));
            const key=btn.dataset.tab;
            root.querySelectorAll('.adv-pane').forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle('active',p.dataset.pane===key));
          }));

          function drawScatter() {
            const svg=root.querySelector('.adv-scatter'); svg.innerHTML='';
            const NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', W=Math.max(svg.clientWidth||620,420), H=310, ml=55,mr=18,mt=12,mb=42;
            svg.setAttribute('viewBox',`0 0 ${W} ${H}`);
            const xs=rows.map(r=>Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(r.failure_probability)||0)));
            const ys=rows.map(r=>Math.max(0,Number(r.lead_time_h)||0));
            const xmax=Math.max(.01,...xs), ymax=Math.max(1,...ys);
            const sx=x=>ml+x/xmax*(W-ml-mr), sy=y=>mt+(1-y/ymax)*(H-mt-mb);
            for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){
              const gx=ml+i*(W-ml-mr)/4, gy=mt+i*(H-mt-mb)/4;
              const vl=document.createElementNS(NS,'line'); vl.setAttribute('x1',gx);vl.setAttribute('x2',gx);vl.setAttribute('y1',mt);vl.setAttribute('y2',H-mb);vl.setAttribute('class','adv-gridline');svg.appendChild(vl);
              const hl=document.createElementNS(NS,'line'); hl.setAttribute('x1',ml);hl.setAttribute('x2',W-mr);hl.setAttribute('y1',gy);hl.setAttribute('y2',gy);hl.setAttribute('class','adv-gridline');svg.appendChild(hl);
              const xt=document.createElementNS(NS,'text'); xt.setAttribute('x',gx);xt.setAttribute('y',H-mb+17);xt.setAttribute('text-anchor','middle');xt.setAttribute('class','adv-tick');xt.textContent=`${fmt(xmax*i/4*100,0)}%`;svg.appendChild(xt);
              const yt=document.createElementNS(NS,'text'); yt.setAttribute('x',ml-8);yt.setAttribute('y',gy+3);yt.setAttribute('text-anchor','end');yt.setAttribute('class','adv-tick');yt.textContent=fmt(ymax*(4-i)/4,0);svg.appendChild(yt);
            }
            rows.forEach((r,idx)=>{
              const c=document.createElementNS(NS,'circle'); c.setAttribute('cx',sx(Number(r.failure_probability)||0));c.setAttribute('cy',sy(Number(r.lead_time_h)||0));c.setAttribute('r',r.long_lead?4.5:3.2);c.setAttribute('class','adv-dot');
              c.addEventListener('click',()=>{
                svg.querySelectorAll('.adv-dot').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('selected')); c.classList.add('selected');
                root.querySelector('.adv-selected').innerHTML=`<b>${esc(r.part_id)}</b>${r.item_name?` · ${esc(r.item_name)}`:''} &nbsp;|&nbsp; 고장 가능성 ${fmt(Number(r.failure_probability)*100,2)}% &nbsp;|&nbsp; 리드타임 ${fmt(r.lead_time_h,1)}h &nbsp;|&nbsp; 예상수요 ${fmt(r.expected_demand,2)}건`;
              }); svg.appendChild(c);
            });
            const xl=document.createElementNS(NS,'text');xl.setAttribute('x',(ml+W-mr)/2);xl.setAttribute('y',H-8);xl.setAttribute('text-anchor','middle');xl.setAttribute('class','adv-tick');xl.textContent='기간 내 고장 가능성';svg.appendChild(xl);
            const yl=document.createElementNS(NS,'text');yl.setAttribute('x',13);yl.setAttribute('y',(mt+H-mb)/2);yl.setAttribute('text-anchor','middle');yl.setAttribute('transform',`rotate(-90 13 ${(mt+H-mb)/2})`);yl.setAttribute('class','adv-tick');yl.textContent='리드타임(h)';svg.appendChild(yl);
          }

          function renderDemand() {
            const top=[...rows].sort((a,b)=>Number(b.expected_demand)-Number(a.expected_demand)).slice(0,12);
            const mx=Math.max(1,...top.map(r=>Number(r.expected_demand)||0));
            root.querySelector('.adv-demand-list').innerHTML=top.map(r=>`<div class="adv-demand-row"><div class="adv-demand-name" title="${esc(r.part_id)} ${esc(r.item_name)}"><b>${esc(r.part_id)}</b>${r.item_name?` · ${esc(r.item_name)}`:''}</div><div class="adv-bar-bg"><div class="adv-bar-fill" style="width:${Math.max(2,(Number(r.expected_demand)||0)/mx*100)}%"></div></div><div class="adv-demand-value">${fmt(r.expected_demand,2)}</div></div>`).join('');
          }

          function renderEchelon() {
            const counts={}; rows.forEach(r=>{ const k=String(r.echelon||'기타'); counts[k]=(counts[k]||0)+1; });
            const arr=Object.entries(counts).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]), mx=Math.max(1,...arr.map(x=>x[1]));
            root.querySelector('.adv-echelon-bars').innerHTML=arr.map(([k,v])=>`<div class="adv-e-row"><div class="adv-e-label">${esc(k)}</div><div class="adv-bar-bg"><div class="adv-bar-fill" style="width:${v/mx*100}%"></div></div><div style="text-align:right;font-weight:800;">${fmt(v,0)}종</div></div>`).join('');
          }

          function renderProcure() {
            const longN=rows.filter(r=>r.long_lead).length, normalN=Math.max(0,rows.length-longN), total=Math.max(1,rows.length), lp=longN/total*100, np=normalN/total*100;
            root.querySelector('.adv-procure').innerHTML=`<div class="adv-procure-row"><div class="adv-procure-long" style="width:${lp}%">${lp>=12?`장기조달 ${fmt(longN,0)}종`:''}</div><div class="adv-procure-normal" style="width:${np}%">${np>=12?`일반 ${fmt(normalN,0)}종`:''}</div></div><div class="adv-procure-notes"><div class="adv-note"><b>장기조달</b><br>${fmt(longN,0)}종 · 전체의 ${fmt(lp,1)}%</div><div class="adv-note"><b>일반</b><br>${fmt(normalN,0)}종 · 전체의 ${fmt(np,1)}%</div></div>`;
          }

          const ef=root.querySelector('.adv-echelon-filter');
          [...new Set(rows.map(r=>String(r.echelon||'기타')))].sort().forEach(v=>{ const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=v;o.textContent=v;ef.appendChild(o); });
          const search=root.querySelector('.adv-search'), lf=root.querySelector('.adv-long-filter'), tbody=root.querySelector('.adv-table tbody'), count=root.querySelector('.adv-count');
          let sortKey='part_id', sortAsc=true;
          function filteredRows(){
            const q=search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
            return rows.filter(r=>(!q || `${r.part_id} ${r.item_name}`.toLowerCase().includes(q)) && (ef.value==='ALL'||String(r.echelon||'기타')===ef.value) && (lf.value==='ALL'||(lf.value==='YES')===Boolean(r.long_lead))).sort((a,b)=>{ const av=a[sortKey], bv=b[sortKey]; const na=Number(av),nb=Number(bv); let cmp=(Number.isFinite(na)&&Number.isFinite(nb))?na-nb:String(av??'').localeCompare(String(bv??'')); return sortAsc?cmp:-cmp; });
          }
          function renderTable(){
            const arr=filteredRows(); count.textContent=`${arr.length.toLocaleString()} / ${rows.length.toLocaleString()}종`;
            tbody.innerHTML=arr.map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(r.part_id)}</td><td>${esc(r.item_name||'-')}</td><td>${esc(r.echelon||'-')}</td><td>${fmt(r.failure_rate,6)}</td><td>${fmt(r.expected_demand,3)}</td><td>${fmt(Number(r.failure_probability)*100,2)}%</td><td>${fmt(r.lead_time_h,1)}</td><td>${fmt(r.unit_price,0)}</td><td>${fmt(r.qpa,2)}</td><td>${r.repairable?'<span class="adv-badge">수리가능</span>':'-'}</td><td>${fmt(r.repair_tat_h,1)}</td><td>${r.long_lead?'<span class="adv-badge">장기조달</span>':'일반'}</td></tr>`).join('');
          }
          [search,ef,lf].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener(el===search?'input':'change',renderTable));
          root.querySelectorAll('.adv-table th[data-sort]').forEach(th=>th.addEventListener('click',()=>{ const k=th.dataset.sort; if(sortKey===k) sortAsc=!sortAsc; else {sortKey=k;sortAsc=true;} renderTable(); }));

          drawScatter(); renderDemand(); renderEchelon(); renderProcure(); renderTable();
          const ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>drawScatter()); ro.observe(root.querySelector('.adv-scatter'));
          return ()=>ro.disconnect();
        }
        """,
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Browser-side Cost-Effectiveness Curve
# ------------------------------------------------------------
_CE_V2_AVAILABLE = bool(
    hasattr(st, "components")
    and hasattr(st.components, "v2")
    and hasattr(st.components.v2, "component")
)

_CE_COMPONENT = None
if _CE_V2_AVAILABLE:
    _CE_COMPONENT = st.components.v2.component(
        name="nsga_ce_curve_frontend_fullaudit_20261012_v2",
        html="""
        <div class="ce-root">
          <div class="ce-threshold-panel">
            <div class="ce-threshold-head">
              <div class="ce-threshold-title">목표 운용가용도</div>
              <div class="ce-threshold-value">0.94</div>
            </div>
            <input class="ce-threshold-range" type="range" min="0.10" max="0.99" step="0.01" value="0.94">
            <div class="ce-threshold-summary"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="ce-top">
            <div class="ce-chart-panel">
              <div class="ce-title">Cost-Effectiveness Curve</div>
              <svg class="ce-svg" role="img" aria-label="Cost-Effectiveness Curve"></svg>
            </div>

        .ce-policy-table tbody tr:hover { background:#f8fbf6; }

          .ce-svg { height:380px; }

        """,
        js="""
        export default function(component) {
          const { parentElement, data, setStateValue } = component;
          const root = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-root");
          const svg = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-svg");
          const metricsEl = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-metrics");
          const selectedLabel = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-selected-label");
          const policyPanel = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-policy-panel");
          const policySummary = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-policy-summary");
          const policyBody = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-policy-table tbody");
          const exportBtn = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-detail-export-btn");
          const compareExportBtn = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-compare-export-btn");
          const comparePanel = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-compare-panel");
          const slotAButton = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-slot-a");
          const slotBButton = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-slot-b");
          const clearABButton = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-clear-ab");
          const cardA = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-a-card");
          const cardB = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-b-card");
          const compareDeltas = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-compare-deltas");
          const abDetailPanel = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-ab-detail-full");
          const abSummary = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-ab-summary");
          const abTableBody = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-ab-table tbody");
          const costUpList = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-cost-up-list");
          const costDownList = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-cost-down-list");
          const thresholdPanel = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-threshold-panel");
          const thresholdRange = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-threshold-range");
          const thresholdValue = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-threshold-value");
          const thresholdSummary = parentElement.querySelector(".ce-threshold-summary");

          const points = Array.isArray(data?.points) ? data.points : [];
          const candidateMeta = Array.isArray(data?.candidate_meta) ? data.candidate_meta : [];
          const solutionVectors = data?.solution_vectors || {};
          const showPolicy = Boolean(data?.show_policy);
          const expanded = Boolean(data?.expanded);
          const thresholdMode = Boolean(data?.threshold_mode);
          const syncSelection = Boolean(data?.sync_selection);
          const riskRunId = String(data?.risk_run_id || "default");
          const riskStorageKey = `nsga_risk_ab_v2_${riskRunId}`;
          let thresholdAo = Number(data?.threshold_default ?? 0.94);
          let compareSlot = "A";

          function readRiskSelection() {
            if (!thresholdMode) return {A:null, B:null};
            try {
              const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(riskStorageKey);
              if (!raw) return {A:null, B:null};
              const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
              return {A: parsed?.A || null, B: parsed?.B || null};
            } catch (_) {
              return {A:null, B:null};
            }
          }

          const storedAB = readRiskSelection();
          let compareAKey = thresholdMode ? String(storedAB?.A?.key || "") : "";
          let compareBKey = thresholdMode ? String(storedAB?.B?.key || "") : "";
          let selectedKey = thresholdMode
            ? (compareAKey || compareBKey || "")
            : (data?.selected_key || (points[0]?.key ?? ""));
          let selectedPolicyRows = [];

          policyPanel.style.display = showPolicy ? "block" : "none";
          thresholdPanel.style.display = thresholdMode ? "block" : "none";
          comparePanel.style.display = thresholdMode ? "block" : "none";
          abDetailPanel.style.display = thresholdMode ? "block" : "none";

          if (thresholdMode) {
            root.querySelector(".ce-detail-title").style.display = "none";
            selectedLabel.style.display = "none";
            metricsEl.style.display = "none";
            exportBtn.style.display = "none";
            compareExportBtn.style.display = "block";
          } else {
            exportBtn.style.display = showPolicy ? "block" : "none";
            compareExportBtn.style.display = "none";
          }
          if (expanded) {
            if (thresholdMode) {
              root.querySelector(".ce-top").style.gridTemplateColumns = "minmax(0,2.35fr) minmax(300px,0.95fr)";
              root.querySelector(".ce-detail-panel").style.marginTop = "0";
              root.classList.add("threshold-compact");
              svg.style.height = "430px";
            } else {
              root.querySelector(".ce-top").style.gridTemplateColumns = "minmax(0,2.35fr) minmax(260px,0.8fr)";
              svg.style.height = "650px";
            }
          }

          const NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
          const colors = ["#4C78A8","#F58518","#54A24B","#E45756","#72B7B2","#B279A2","#9D755D","#2E91E5","#1CA71C","#E15F99","#7A9E2E","#7B61A8"];
          const uniqTargets = [...new Set(points.map(p => Number(p.target_ao)))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
          const colorFor = (t) => colors[Math.max(0, uniqTargets.indexOf(Number(t))) % colors.length];

          const fmt = (v, digits=2) => {
            const n = Number(v);
            if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "-";
            return n.toLocaleString(undefined,{minimumFractionDigits:digits,maximumFractionDigits:digits});
          };
          const esc = (v) => String(v ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => c === "&" ? "&amp;" : c === "<" ? "&lt;" : c === ">" ? "&gt;" : c === '"' ? "&quot;" : "&#39;");

          function policyType(meta, qty, managed) {
            if (meta.prebuy_flag && managed) return "PRE-BUY";
            if (meta.protection_flag && qty > 0) return "PROTECTION";
            if (managed) return "MANAGED";
            return "NORMAL";
          }

          function buildPolicyRows(key) {
            const point = points.find(x => x.key === key);
            const sig = point ? String(point.signature || "") : "";
            const vec = (sig && solutionVectors[sig]) ? solutionVectors[sig] : solutionVectors[key];
            if (!vec || !candidateMeta.length) return [];
            const sO = vec.sO || [], sI = vec.sI || [], sD = vec.sD || [], y = vec.Y || [];
            const rows = [];
            for (let i=0; i<candidateMeta.length; i++) {
              const o = Number(sO[i] || 0), ii = Number(sI[i] || 0), d = Number(sD[i] || 0);
              const qty = o + ii + d;
              if (qty <= 0) continue;
              const m = candidateMeta[i] || {};
              const managed = Number(y[i] || 0) > 0;
              rows.push({
                part_id: m.part_id ?? "",
                item_name: m.item_name || m.category || "",
                category: m.category || m.item_name || "",
                qty, stock_O:o, stock_I:ii, stock_D:d,
                echelon: m.maint_echelon ?? "",
                policy_type: policyType(m, qty, managed),
                lead_time: Number(m.lead_time || 0),
                proc_cost: Number(m.proc_cost || 0),
                failure_rate: Number(m.failure_rate || 0),
                p_need_period: Number(m.p_need_period || 0),
                impact_score: Number(m.impact_score || 0),
                is_long_lead: Boolean(m.is_long_lead)
              });
            }
            rows.sort((a,b) => b.qty-a.qty || b.impact_score-a.impact_score);
            return rows;
          }

          function renderPolicy(key) {
            if (!showPolicy) return;
            selectedPolicyRows = buildPolicyRows(key);
            const totalUnits = selectedPolicyRows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.qty,0);
            policySummary.textContent = `산출 품목 ${selectedPolicyRows.length.toLocaleString()}종 · 총 재고 ${totalUnits.toLocaleString()}개`;
            policyBody.innerHTML = selectedPolicyRows.map(r => `
              <tr>
                <td>${esc(r.part_id)}</td><td>${esc(r.item_name || r.category || "")}</td>
                <td>${fmt(r.qty,0)}</td><td>${esc(r.policy_type)}</td>
                <td>${fmt(r.lead_time,1)}</td><td>${fmt(r.proc_cost,0)}</td>
                <td>${fmt(r.failure_rate,6)}</td><td>${fmt(r.p_need_period,4)}</td><td>${fmt(r.impact_score,6)}</td>
              </tr>`).join("");
          }

          function percentile(sortedVals, p) {
            if (!sortedVals.length) return 0;
            const vals = [...sortedVals].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
            const idx = (vals.length - 1) * p;
            const lo = Math.floor(idx), hi = Math.ceil(idx);
            if (lo === hi) return vals[lo];
            return vals[lo] + (vals[hi]-vals[lo])*(idx-lo);
          }

          function median(vals) {
            return percentile(vals.filter(Number.isFinite), 0.5);
          }

          function calcRiskMetrics(key) {
            const rows = buildPolicyRows(key);
            const types = rows.length;
            const units = rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+Number(r.qty||0),0);
            const values = rows.map(r=>Math.max(0,Number(r.qty||0)*Number(r.proc_cost||0)));
            const totalValue = values.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0);
            const weightedUnitPrice = units > 0 ? totalValue/units : 0;
            const unitsPerType = types > 0 ? units/types : 0;

            let hhi = 0, top10Share = 0, maxValueShare = 0, effectiveTypes = 0;
            if (totalValue > 0 && values.length) {
              const shares = values.map(v=>v/totalValue);
              hhi = shares.reduce((s,x)=>s+x*x,0);
              effectiveTypes = hhi > 0 ? 1/hhi : 0;
              maxValueShare = Math.max(...shares);
              const sortedVals = [...values].sort((a,b)=>b-a);
              const topN = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(sortedVals.length*0.10));
              top10Share = sortedVals.slice(0,topN).reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/totalValue;
            }

            const longLeadValue = rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.is_long_lead ? Number(r.qty||0)*Number(r.proc_cost||0) : 0),0);
            const longLeadValueShare = totalValue > 0 ? longLeadValue/totalValue : 0;

            return {
              key, rows, types, units, unitsPerType, totalValue, weightedUnitPrice,
              hhi, effectiveTypes, top10Share, maxValueShare, longLeadValueShare
            };
          }

          function getVisibleRiskBenchmarks(visiblePoints) {
            const metrics = visiblePoints.map(p=>calcRiskMetrics(p.key));
            return {
              metrics,
              typesMed: median(metrics.map(m=>m.types)),
              unitsPerTypeMed: median(metrics.map(m=>m.unitsPerType)),
              weightedPriceMed: median(metrics.map(m=>m.weightedUnitPrice)),
              hhiMed: median(metrics.map(m=>m.hhi)),
              top10Med: median(metrics.map(m=>m.top10Share)),
              longLeadMed: median(metrics.map(m=>m.longLeadValueShare))
            };
          }

          function ratioBar(value, benchmark) {
            if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return 0;
            if (!Number.isFinite(benchmark) || benchmark <= 0) return Math.min(100, value > 0 ? 100 : 0);
            return Math.max(3, Math.min(100, (value/(benchmark*2))*100));
          }

          function relativeInventoryLabel(thisM, otherM) {
            if (!thisM || !otherM) return "";
            if (thisM.types > otherM.types) return "상대적 다품종";
            if (thisM.types < otherM.types) return "상대적 소품종";
            return "품목수 동일";
          }

          function colorClass(a, b) {
            if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || a === b) return "ce-neutral";
            return a > b ? "ce-higher" : "ce-lower";
          }

          function renderCompareCard(el, label, key, otherKey) {
            if (!key) {
              el.innerHTML = `<div class="ce-compare-card-title">${label}</div><div style="font-size:12px;color:#879388;">C-E Curve에서 해를 선택 필요.</div>`;
              return null;
            }
            const p = points.find(x => x.key === key);
            if (!p) return null;
            const m = calcRiskMetrics(key);

            const otherP = otherKey ? points.find(x => x.key === otherKey) : null;
            const otherM = otherKey ? calcRiskMetrics(otherKey) : null;

            const costCls = otherP ? colorClass(Number(p.total_cost), Number(otherP.total_cost)) : "ce-neutral";
            const aoCls = otherP ? colorClass(Number(p.Ao), Number(otherP.Ao)) : "ce-neutral";
            const qtyCls = otherM ? colorClass(Number(m.units), Number(otherM.units)) : "ce-neutral";
            const typesCls = otherM ? colorClass(Number(m.types), Number(otherM.types)) : "ce-neutral";
            const invLabel = otherM ? relativeInventoryLabel(m, otherM) : "";

            el.innerHTML = `
              <div class="ce-compare-card-title">${label}</div>
              <div class="ce-compare-card-row"><span>Ao</span><b class="${aoCls}">${Number(p.Ao).toFixed(4)}</b></div>
              <div class="ce-compare-card-row"><span>총 비용</span><b class="${costCls}">${fmt(p.total_cost,0)}</b></div>
              <div class="ce-compare-card-row"><span>재고 품목수</span><b class="${typesCls}">${fmt(m.types,0)}종</b></div>
              <div class="ce-compare-card-row"><span>총 재고수량</span><b class="${qtyCls}">${fmt(m.units,0)}개</b></div>
              <div class="ce-compare-card-row"><span>재고 특징</span><b>${invLabel || "-"}</b></div>
            `;
            return {p,m};
          }

          function signedValue(value, digits=0, suffix="") {
            if (!Number.isFinite(value)) return "-";
            const sign = value > 0 ? "+" : "";
            return `${sign}${fmt(value,digits)}${suffix}`;
          }

          function deltaClass(v) {
            if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v === 0) return "ce-neutral";
            return v > 0 ? "ce-higher" : "ce-lower";
          }

          function buildABDiffRows(aKey, bKey) {
            if (!aKey || !bKey) return [];
            const aRows = buildPolicyRows(aKey);
            const bRows = buildPolicyRows(bKey);

            const mapA = new Map(aRows.map(r => [String(r.part_id), r]));
            const mapB = new Map(bRows.map(r => [String(r.part_id), r]));
            const keys = new Set([...mapA.keys(), ...mapB.keys()]);
            const rows = [];

            keys.forEach(k => {
              const a = mapA.get(k) || {};
              const b = mapB.get(k) || {};
              const qtyA = Number(a.qty || 0);
              const qtyB = Number(b.qty || 0);
              const diff = qtyB - qtyA;
              if (diff === 0) return;

              const unitPrice = Number(b.proc_cost || a.proc_cost || 0);
              const costDiff = diff * unitPrice;

              let change = "";
              if (qtyA === 0 && qtyB > 0) change = "B 추가";
              else if (qtyA > 0 && qtyB === 0) change = "B 제외";
              else if (diff > 0) change = "B 증가";
              else change = "B 감소";

              rows.push({
                part_id: b.part_id || a.part_id || k,
                item_name: b.item_name || b.category || a.item_name || a.category || "",
                qtyA, qtyB, diff, unitPrice, costDiff, change
              });
            });

            rows.sort((x,y) => Math.abs(y.costDiff) - Math.abs(x.costDiff));
            return rows;
          }

          function renderABDetails(aKey, bKey) {
            if (!thresholdMode || !aKey || !bKey) {
              abSummary.innerHTML = `
                <div class="ce-ab-summary-card" style="grid-column:1/-1;">
                  <div class="ce-ab-summary-label">품목 구성 차이</div>
                  <div class="ce-ab-summary-value" style="font-size:12px;color:#718075;">
                    A와 B를 선택하면 두 해의 수리부속 구성과 비용 차이를 비교함.
                  </div>
                </div>`;
              abTableBody.innerHTML = "";
              costUpList.innerHTML = "";
              costDownList.innerHTML = "";
              return;
            }

            const rows = buildABDiffRows(aKey, bKey);
            const aPoint = points.find(x => x.key === aKey);
            const bPoint = points.find(x => x.key === bKey);
            if (!aPoint || !bPoint) return;

            const purchaseCostDiff = rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.costDiff,0);
            const totalCostDiff = Number(bPoint.total_cost) - Number(aPoint.total_cost);
            const otherCostDiff = totalCostDiff - purchaseCostDiff;

            abSummary.innerHTML = [
              ["총 비용 차이", totalCostDiff],
              ["재고 구매비용 차이", purchaseCostDiff],
              ["기타 비용 차이", otherCostDiff]
            ].map(([label,value]) => `
              <div class="ce-ab-summary-card">
                <div class="ce-ab-summary-label">${label}</div>
                <div class="ce-ab-summary-value ${value>0?"ce-positive":(value<0?"ce-negative":"")}">
                  ${value>0?"+":""}${fmt(value,0)}
                </div>
              </div>`).join("");

            abTableBody.innerHTML = rows.map(r => `
              <tr>
                <td>${r.part_id ?? ""}</td>
                <td>${r.item_name || r.category || ""}</td>
                <td>${fmt(r.qtyA,0)}</td>
                <td>${fmt(r.qtyB,0)}</td>
                <td class="${r.diff>0?"ce-positive":"ce-negative"}">${r.diff>0?"+":""}${fmt(r.diff,0)}</td>
                <td>${fmt(r.unitPrice,0)}</td>
                <td class="${r.costDiff>0?"ce-positive":"ce-negative"}">${r.costDiff>0?"+":""}${fmt(r.costDiff,0)}</td>
                <td>${r.change}</td>
              </tr>`).join("");

            const up = rows.filter(r=>r.costDiff>0).sort((a,b)=>b.costDiff-a.costDiff).slice(0,5);
            const down = rows.filter(r=>r.costDiff<0).sort((a,b)=>a.costDiff-b.costDiff).slice(0,5);
            const upTotal = up.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.costDiff,0);
            const downTotalAbs = Math.abs(down.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.costDiff,0));

            costUpList.innerHTML = up.length ? up.map(r => `
              <div class="ce-cost-impact-item">
                <div class="ce-cost-impact-name">
                  <div class="ce-cost-impact-part">${r.part_id || "-"}</div>
                  <div class="ce-cost-impact-itemname">${r.item_name || r.category || "-"}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="ce-cost-impact-value ce-positive">+${fmt(r.costDiff,0)}</div>
              </div>`).join("") : '<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:#889488;">증가 항목 없음</div>';

            costDownList.innerHTML = down.length ? down.map(r => `
              <div class="ce-cost-impact-item">
                <div class="ce-cost-impact-name">
                  <div class="ce-cost-impact-part">${r.part_id || "-"}</div>
                  <div class="ce-cost-impact-itemname">${r.item_name || r.category || "-"}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="ce-cost-impact-value ce-negative">${fmt(r.costDiff,0)}</div>
              </div>`).join("") : '<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:#889488;">감소 항목 없음</div>';
          }

          function renderComparison(visiblePoints) {
            if (!thresholdMode) return;
            const a = renderCompareCard(cardA,"A",compareAKey,compareBKey);
            const b = renderCompareCard(cardB,"B",compareBKey,compareAKey);

            if (!a || !b) {
              compareDeltas.innerHTML = "";
              renderABDetails("", "");
              return;
            }

            const costDiff = Number(b.p.total_cost) - Number(a.p.total_cost);
            const aoDiff = Number(b.p.Ao) - Number(a.p.Ao);
            const qtyDiff = Number(b.m.units) - Number(a.m.units);
            const typesDiff = Number(b.m.types) - Number(a.m.types);

            const cards = [
              ["비용 B-A", signedValue(costDiff,0,""), deltaClass(costDiff)],
              ["Ao B-A", `${aoDiff>0?"+":""}${(aoDiff*100).toFixed(2)}%p`, deltaClass(aoDiff)],
              ["재고수량 B-A", signedValue(qtyDiff,0,"개"), deltaClass(qtyDiff)],
              ["품목수 B-A", signedValue(typesDiff,0,"종"), deltaClass(typesDiff)]
            ];

            compareDeltas.innerHTML = cards.map(([k,v,cls])=>`
              <div class="ce-delta-card">
                <div class="ce-delta-label">${k}</div>
                <div class="ce-delta-value ${cls}">${v}</div>
              </div>`).join("");

            renderABDetails(compareAKey, compareBKey);
          }

          function renderDetail(p, visiblePoints) {
            if (!p) return;
            selectedLabel.textContent = `목표 Ao ${Number(p.target_ao).toFixed(2)} · 해 ${p.solution_id}`;
            const rows = [
              ["Ao", Number(p.Ao).toFixed(4)],
              ["총 비용", fmt(p.total_cost,0)],
              ["DT 진단", fmt(p.DT_diag_h,2)+" h"],
              ["DT 대기", fmt(p.DT_wait_h,2)+" h"],
              ["DT 복구", fmt(p.DT_restore_h,2)+" h"],
              ["총 DT", fmt(p.DT_total_h,2)+" h"],
              ["관리 품목", fmt(p.stocked_managed_parts,0)],
              ["총 재고", fmt(p.total_stock_units,0)],
              ["선발주", fmt(p.prebuy_selected,0)],
              ["보호재고", fmt(p.protection_selected,0)]
            ];
            metricsEl.innerHTML = rows.map(([k,v]) =>
              `<div class="ce-metric"><div class="ce-metric-label">${k}</div><div class="ce-metric-value">${v}</div></div>`
            ).join("");
            renderPolicy(p.key);
          }

          function selectPoint(key, visiblePoints=points, emitSelection=false, assignComparison=true) {
            selectedKey = key;
            const p = points.find(x => x.key === key) || points[0];
            if (thresholdMode && assignComparison) {
              if (compareSlot === "A") {
                compareAKey = key;
              } else {
                compareBKey = key;
              }
            }
            root.querySelectorAll(".ce-point").forEach(el => {
              const isSel = el.dataset.key === key;
              el.classList.toggle("selected", isSel);
              const base = el.classList.contains("pareto") ? 6.3 : 4.2;
              el.setAttribute("r", isSel ? 10 : base);
            });
            renderDetail(p, visiblePoints);
            renderComparison(visiblePoints);
            if (emitSelection && syncSelection && setStateValue) {
              setStateValue("selected_key", key);
            }
            if (emitSelection && thresholdMode && assignComparison) {
              persistABSelection();
            }
          }

          function downloadSelectedPolicy() {
            if (!selectedKey) return;
            const p = points.find(x => x.key === selectedKey);
            if (!p) return;
            const rows = buildPolicyRows(selectedKey);
            const summary = [
              ["항목","값"],
              ["운용가용도(Ao)",(Number(p.Ao)*100).toFixed(2)+"%"],
              ["총 비용",String(p.total_cost)],
              ["총 DT(h)",String(p.DT_total_h)],
              ["총 재고",String(p.total_stock_units)]
            ];
            const table = [
              ["품번","품목","총수량","정책구분","리드타임(h)","단가","고장률","기간 고장확률","영향도"],
              ...rows.map(r => [r.part_id,r.item_name,r.qty,r.policy_type,r.lead_time,r.proc_cost,r.failure_rate,r.p_need_period,r.impact_score])
            ];
            const toHtmlRows = arr => arr.map(row => "<tr>"+row.map(v=>"<td>"+esc(v)+"</td>").join("")+"</tr>").join("");
            const html = `<!doctype html><html><meta charset="utf-8"><body>
              <table>${toHtmlRows(summary)}</table><br><table>${toHtmlRows(table)}</table></body></html>`;
            const blob = new Blob(["\\ufeff", html], {type:"application/vnd.ms-excel;charset=utf-8"});
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement("a");
            a.href = url;
            a.download = `NSGA_selected_${(Number(p.Ao)*100).toFixed(2)}.xls`;
            a.click();
            setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
          }

          // Assign one handler per render. Using onclick avoids duplicate
          // downloads when the Bidi component is re-rendered.
          exportBtn.onclick = downloadSelectedPolicy;
          compareExportBtn.onclick = downloadSelectedPolicy;

          function setCompareSlot(slot) {
            compareSlot = slot;
            slotAButton.classList.toggle("active", slot === "A");
            slotBButton.classList.toggle("active", slot === "B");
          }

          function compactSelection(key) {
            if (!key) return null;
            const p = points.find(x => x.key === key);
            if (!p) return null;
            return {
              key: String(p.key),
              target_ao: Number(p.target_ao),
              solution_id: Number(p.solution_id),
              Ao: Number(p.Ao),
              total_cost: Number(p.total_cost),
              stocked_managed_parts: Number(p.stocked_managed_parts || 0),
              total_stock_units: Number(p.total_stock_units || 0)
            };
          }

          function persistABSelection() {
            if (!thresholdMode) return;
            try {
              window.localStorage.setItem(riskStorageKey, JSON.stringify({
                run_id: riskRunId,
                A: compactSelection(compareAKey),
                B: compactSelection(compareBKey),
                updated_at: Date.now()
              }));
            } catch (_) {}
          }

          function clearABSelection() {
            compareAKey = "";
            compareBKey = "";
            selectedKey = "";
            root.querySelectorAll(".ce-point").forEach(el => {
              el.classList.remove("selected");
              const base = el.classList.contains("pareto") ? 6.3 : 4.2;
              el.setAttribute("r", base);
            });
            renderComparison([]);
            compareDeltas.innerHTML = "";
            renderABDetails("", "");
            persistABSelection();
          }

          slotAButton.onclick = () => setCompareSlot("A");
          slotBButton.onclick = () => setCompareSlot("B");
          clearABButton.onclick = clearABSelection;

          if (thresholdMode) {
            thresholdRange.value = thresholdAo.toFixed(2);
            thresholdRange.addEventListener("input", (ev) => {
              thresholdAo = Number(ev.target.value);
              draw();
            });
          }

          function draw() {
            svg.innerHTML = "";
            const visiblePoints = thresholdMode
              ? points.filter(p => Number(p.Ao) >= thresholdAo)
              : points;

            if (thresholdMode) {
              const visibleKeys = new Set(visiblePoints.map(p=>p.key));
              const oldAKey = compareAKey;
              const oldBKey = compareBKey;
              if (compareAKey && !visibleKeys.has(compareAKey)) compareAKey = "";
              if (compareBKey && !visibleKeys.has(compareBKey)) compareBKey = "";
              if (oldAKey !== compareAKey || oldBKey !== compareBKey) persistABSelection();
              thresholdValue.textContent = thresholdAo.toFixed(2);
              thresholdRange.value = thresholdAo.toFixed(2);
              thresholdSummary.textContent =
                `실제 산출 Ao ≥ ${thresholdAo.toFixed(2)}인 해 ${visiblePoints.length.toLocaleString()}개 / 전체 ${points.length.toLocaleString()}개`;
            }

            if (!visiblePoints.length) {
              const t = document.createElementNS(NS,"text");
              t.setAttribute("x","50%"); t.setAttribute("y","50%");
              t.setAttribute("text-anchor","middle"); t.textContent="표시할 분석 해가 없습니다.";
              svg.appendChild(t); return;
            }

            const W = Math.max(svg.clientWidth || 720, 480);
            const H = expanded ? (thresholdMode ? 430 : 650) : 430, ml=68, mr=18, mt=14, mb=48;
            svg.setAttribute("viewBox",`0 0 ${W} ${H}`);

            const costs = visiblePoints.map(p=>Number(p.total_cost)).filter(Number.isFinite);
            const aos = visiblePoints.map(p=>Number(p.Ao)).filter(Number.isFinite);
            let xmin=Math.min(...costs), xmax=Math.max(...costs);
            let ymin=Math.min(...aos), ymax=Math.max(...aos);
            if (xmax<=xmin) xmax=xmin+1;
            if (ymax<=ymin) ymax=ymin+0.01;
            const xpad=(xmax-xmin)*0.04, ypad=(ymax-ymin)*0.08;
            xmin-=xpad; xmax+=xpad; ymin=Math.max(0,ymin-ypad); ymax=Math.min(1,ymax+ypad);

            const sx=x=>ml+(x-xmin)/(xmax-xmin)*(W-ml-mr);
            const sy=y=>mt+(ymax-y)/(ymax-ymin)*(H-mt-mb);

            for (let i=0;i<=5;i++) {
              const gx=ml+i*(W-ml-mr)/5, gy=mt+i*(H-mt-mb)/5;
              const vl=document.createElementNS(NS,"line");
              vl.setAttribute("x1",gx); vl.setAttribute("x2",gx); vl.setAttribute("y1",mt); vl.setAttribute("y2",H-mb);
              vl.setAttribute("class","ce-grid"); svg.appendChild(vl);
              const hl=document.createElementNS(NS,"line");
              hl.setAttribute("x1",ml); hl.setAttribute("x2",W-mr); hl.setAttribute("y1",gy); hl.setAttribute("y2",gy);
              hl.setAttribute("class","ce-grid"); svg.appendChild(hl);
              const xt=document.createElementNS(NS,"text");
              xt.setAttribute("x",gx); xt.setAttribute("y",H-mb+18); xt.setAttribute("text-anchor","middle"); xt.setAttribute("class","ce-tick");
              xt.textContent=fmt(xmin+i*(xmax-xmin)/5,0); svg.appendChild(xt);
              const yt=document.createElementNS(NS,"text");
              yt.setAttribute("x",ml-10); yt.setAttribute("y",gy+4); yt.setAttribute("text-anchor","end"); yt.setAttribute("class","ce-tick");
              yt.textContent=(ymax-i*(ymax-ymin)/5).toFixed(3); svg.appendChild(yt);
            }

            const xAxis=document.createElementNS(NS,"line");
            xAxis.setAttribute("x1",ml); xAxis.setAttribute("x2",W-mr); xAxis.setAttribute("y1",H-mb); xAxis.setAttribute("y2",H-mb); xAxis.setAttribute("class","ce-axis"); svg.appendChild(xAxis);
            const yAxis=document.createElementNS(NS,"line");
            yAxis.setAttribute("x1",ml); yAxis.setAttribute("x2",ml); yAxis.setAttribute("y1",mt); yAxis.setAttribute("y2",H-mb); yAxis.setAttribute("class","ce-axis"); svg.appendChild(yAxis);

            const xlabel=document.createElementNS(NS,"text");
            xlabel.setAttribute("x",(ml+W-mr)/2); xlabel.setAttribute("y",H-12); xlabel.setAttribute("text-anchor","middle"); xlabel.setAttribute("class","ce-axis-label");
            xlabel.textContent="Cost"; svg.appendChild(xlabel);
            const ylabel=document.createElementNS(NS,"text");
            ylabel.setAttribute("x",16); ylabel.setAttribute("y",(mt+H-mb)/2); ylabel.setAttribute("text-anchor","middle"); ylabel.setAttribute("class","ce-axis-label");
            ylabel.setAttribute("transform",`rotate(-90 16 ${(mt+H-mb)/2})`); ylabel.textContent="Ao"; svg.appendChild(ylabel);

            visiblePoints.forEach(p => {
              const c=document.createElementNS(NS,"circle");
              c.setAttribute("cx",sx(Number(p.total_cost))); c.setAttribute("cy",sy(Number(p.Ao)));
              c.setAttribute("r",p.is_pareto_2d ? 6.3 : 4.2); c.setAttribute("fill",colorFor(p.target_ao));
              c.setAttribute("class",`ce-point ${p.is_pareto_2d ? "pareto" : "normal"}`); c.dataset.key=p.key;
              const tt=document.createElementNS(NS,"title");
              tt.textContent=`목표 Ao ${Number(p.target_ao).toFixed(2)} | Ao ${Number(p.Ao).toFixed(4)} | Cost ${fmt(p.total_cost,0)}`;
              c.appendChild(tt);
              c.onclick = () => selectPoint(p.key, visiblePoints, true);
              svg.appendChild(c);
            });
            if (visiblePoints.length) {
              const selectedStillVisible = visiblePoints.some(p => p.key === selectedKey);
              if (!selectedStillVisible) selectedKey = visiblePoints[0].key;
              selectPoint(selectedKey, visiblePoints, false, false);
            }
          }

          draw();
          const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => draw());
          ro.observe(svg);
          return () => ro.disconnect();
        }
        """
    )


def _build_frontend_ce_points(selector_df: pd.DataFrame) -> list[dict]:
    if not isinstance(selector_df, pd.DataFrame) or selector_df.empty:
        return []
    out = selector_df.copy()
    needed = [
        "target_ao", "solution_id", "Ao", "total_cost", "DT_diag_h", "DT_wait_h",
        "DT_restore_h", "DT_total_h", "stocked_managed_parts", "total_stock_units",
        "prebuy_selected", "protection_selected", "is_pareto_2d", "signature"
    ]
    for c in needed:
        if c not in out.columns:
            out[c] = 0
    pts = []
    for _, r in out.iterrows():
        target = float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("target_ao", 0.0), errors="coerce"))
        sid = int(pd.to_numeric(r.get("solution_id", 0), errors="coerce"))
        pts.append({
            "key": build_solution_payload_key(target, sid),
            "target_ao": target,
            "solution_id": sid,
            "Ao": float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("Ao", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "total_cost": float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("total_cost", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "DT_diag_h": float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("DT_diag_h", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "DT_wait_h": float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("DT_wait_h", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "DT_restore_h": float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("DT_restore_h", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "DT_total_h": float(pd.to_numeric(r.get("DT_total_h", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "stocked_managed_parts": int(pd.to_numeric(r.get("stocked_managed_parts", r.get("managed_items", 0)), errors="coerce")),
            "total_stock_units": int(pd.to_numeric(r.get("total_stock_units", 0), errors="coerce")),
            "prebuy_selected": int(pd.to_numeric(r.get("prebuy_selected", 0), errors="coerce")),
            "protection_selected": int(pd.to_numeric(r.get("protection_selected", 0), errors="coerce")),
            "is_pareto_2d": int(pd.to_numeric(r.get("is_pareto_2d", 0), errors="coerce")),
            "signature": str(r.get("signature", "")),
        })
    return pts


def _build_frontend_policy_payload(result: dict) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
    cached_meta = result.get("_frontend_candidate_meta_cache")
    cached_vec = result.get("_frontend_solution_vectors_cache")
    if isinstance(cached_meta, list) and isinstance(cached_vec, dict):
        return cached_meta, cached_vec

    ctx = result.get("_engine_context", {})
    result_map = result.get("result_map", {})
    if not isinstance(ctx, dict) or not isinstance(result_map, dict):
        return [], {}

    part_id = np.asarray(ctx.get("part_id_c", []), dtype=object)
    item_name = np.asarray(ctx.get("item_name_c", np.full(len(part_id), "", dtype=object)), dtype=object)
    echelon = np.asarray(ctx.get("echelon_c", np.full(len(part_id), "", dtype=object)), dtype=object)
    lead = np.asarray(ctx.get("lead_c", np.zeros(len(part_id))), dtype=float)
    proc_cost = np.asarray(ctx.get("proc_cost_c", np.zeros(len(part_id))), dtype=float)
    cm_cost = np.asarray(ctx.get("cm_cost_c", np.zeros(len(part_id))), dtype=float)
    fr = np.asarray(ctx.get("annual_fr_c", np.zeros(len(part_id))), dtype=float)
    pneed = np.asarray(ctx.get("p_need_c", np.zeros(len(part_id))), dtype=float)
    impact = np.asarray(ctx.get("impact_c", np.zeros(len(part_id))), dtype=float)
    prebuy = np.asarray(ctx.get("prebuy_flag_c", np.zeros(len(part_id), dtype=bool)), dtype=bool)
    protection = np.asarray(ctx.get("protection_flag_c", np.zeros(len(part_id), dtype=bool)), dtype=bool)
    is_long = np.asarray(ctx.get("is_long_c", np.zeros(len(part_id), dtype=bool)), dtype=bool)

    meta = []
    for i in range(len(part_id)):
        _name = str(item_name[i]).strip()
        if _name in {"", "nan", "None", "<NA>"}:
            category_arr = np.asarray(ctx.get("category_c", np.full(len(part_id), "", dtype=object)), dtype=object)
            _name = str(category_arr[i]).strip() if i < len(category_arr) else ""
        meta.append({
            "part_id": str(part_id[i]),
            "item_name": _name,
            "category": _name,
            "maint_echelon": str(echelon[i]),
            "lead_time": float(lead[i]),
            "proc_cost": float(proc_cost[i]),
            "cm_cost": float(cm_cost[i]) if i < len(cm_cost) else 0.0,
            "failure_rate": float(fr[i]),
            "p_need_period": float(pneed[i]),
            "impact_score": float(impact[i]),
            "prebuy_flag": bool(prebuy[i]),
            "protection_flag": bool(protection[i]),
            "is_long_lead": bool(is_long[i]),
        })

    vectors = {}
    for target_key, one in result_map.items():
        if not isinstance(one, dict):
            continue
        sim = one.get("sim_pop")
        if not isinstance(sim, dict):
            continue
        ao_arr = np.asarray(sim.get("Ao", []))
        sO = np.asarray(sim.get("sO", []), dtype=int)
        sI = np.asarray(sim.get("sI", []), dtype=int)
        sD = np.asarray(sim.get("sD", []), dtype=int)
        Y = np.asarray(sim.get("Y", []), dtype=int)
        for sid in range(len(ao_arr)):
            key = build_solution_payload_key(float(target_key), sid)
            sig = (
                "Y:" + ",".join(map(str, Y[sid].tolist()))
                + "|O:" + ",".join(map(str, sO[sid].tolist()))
                + "|I:" + ",".join(map(str, sI[sid].tolist()))
                + "|D:" + ",".join(map(str, sD[sid].tolist()))
            )
            vec_payload = {
                "sO": sO[sid].tolist(),
                "sI": sI[sid].tolist(),
                "sD": sD[sid].tolist(),
                "Y": Y[sid].tolist(),
            }
            # Exact solution identity is primary.
            vectors[sig] = vec_payload
            # Keep legacy key only for backward compatibility.
            vectors[key] = vec_payload

    result["_frontend_candidate_meta_cache"] = meta
    result["_frontend_solution_vectors_cache"] = vectors
    return meta, vectors


def render_frontend_ce_component(
    result: dict,
    selector_df: pd.DataFrame,
    component_key: str,
    show_policy: bool = False,
    expanded: bool = False,
    threshold_mode: bool = False,
    threshold_default: float = 0.94,
):
    current_payload = _selected_payload_or_default(result, selector_df)
    current_key = (
        build_solution_payload_key(current_payload["target_ao"], current_payload["solution_id"])
        if isinstance(current_payload, dict) else ""
    )

    if expanded or threshold_mode:
        points = _build_frontend_ce_points(selector_df)
    else:
        points = result.get("_frontend_ce_points_cache")
        if not isinstance(points, list):
            points = _build_frontend_ce_points(selector_df)
            result["_frontend_ce_points_cache"] = points

    candidate_meta, solution_vectors = ([], {})
    if show_policy or threshold_mode:
        candidate_meta, solution_vectors = _build_frontend_policy_payload(result)

    if _CE_V2_AVAILABLE and _CE_COMPONENT is not None:
        sync_selection = False  # all integrated-result C-E tabs are browser-only
        risk_run_id = str(result.get("_risk_run_id", "default")) if isinstance(result, dict) else "default"

        # Threshold/A-B mode stays browser-only. No callback is registered here,
        # so clicking A/B points cannot trigger a Streamlit rerun.
        if threshold_mode:
            _CE_COMPONENT(
                data={
                    "points": points,
                    "selected_key": current_key,
                    "show_policy": bool(show_policy),
                    "candidate_meta": candidate_meta,
                    "solution_vectors": solution_vectors,
                    "expanded": bool(expanded),
                    "threshold_mode": True,
                    "threshold_default": float(threshold_default),
                    "sync_selection": False,
                    "risk_run_id": risk_run_id,
                },
                key=component_key,
                width="stretch",
                height=(980 if expanded else 535),
            )
            return

        # STEP 2-R2: Detail tab is also browser-only.
        # Point clicks update the chart/detail/policy entirely inside the frontend
        # component and NEVER call setStateValue or a Python callback. This keeps
        # 통합결과-상세결과 at zero Streamlit reruns, matching the A/B tab UX.
        _CE_COMPONENT(
            data={
                "points": points,
                "selected_key": current_key,
                "show_policy": bool(show_policy),
                "candidate_meta": candidate_meta,
                "solution_vectors": solution_vectors,
                "expanded": bool(expanded),
                "threshold_mode": False,
                "threshold_default": float(threshold_default),
                "sync_selection": False,
                "risk_run_id": risk_run_id,
            },
            key=component_key,
            width="stretch",
            height=(790 if expanded else (1040 if show_policy else 535)),
        )
        return

    # Older Streamlit fallback.
    _interactive_ce_fragment_body(result, selector_df, component_key + "_fallback", True)

# Browser-side A/B selector mirror and simulation trigger for the Risk Analysis tab.
# A/B browsing remains frontend-only. Python is invoked only when the user explicitly
# presses the simulation button.

        """,
        js="""
        export default function(component) {
          const { parentElement, data, setStateValue } = component;
          const root = parentElement.querySelector('.risk-mirror-root');
          const status = root.querySelector('.risk-status');
          const cardA = root.querySelector('.risk-a');
          const cardB = root.querySelector('.risk-b');
          const runBtn = root.querySelector('.risk-run-btn');
          const resultsEl = root.querySelector('.risk-results');
          const lastResult = data?.mc_result || null;
          const runId = String(data?.risk_run_id || 'default');
          const storageKey = `nsga_risk_ab_v2_${runId}`;
          const xaiStorageKey = `nsga_xai_payload_v1_${runId}`;
          if(lastResult?.xai_payload){
            try { window.localStorage.setItem(xaiStorageKey, JSON.stringify(lastResult.xai_payload)); } catch(_) {}
          }
          let lastRaw = null;
          let currentState = {A:null,B:null};

          const fmt = (v,d=0) => {
            const n=Number(v); if(!Number.isFinite(n)) return '-';
            return n.toLocaleString(undefined,{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d});
          };
          function readState(){
            try { const raw=window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey)||''; if(raw===lastRaw) return null; lastRaw=raw; return raw?JSON.parse(raw):{A:null,B:null}; }
            catch(_) { return {A:null,B:null}; }
          }
          function card(el,label,p){
            if(!p){ el.innerHTML=`<div class="risk-card-title">${label} 대안</div><div class="risk-empty">아직 선택되지 않았습니다.<br>성과지표 달성지점에서 ${label} 대안을 선택해야 합니다.</div>`; return; }
            el.innerHTML=`<div class="risk-card-title">${label} 대안</div>
              <div class="risk-row"><span>Ao</span><b>${Number(p.Ao).toFixed(4)}</b></div>
              <div class="risk-row"><span>총 비용</span><b>${fmt(p.total_cost,0)}</b></div>
              <div class="risk-row"><span>재고 품목수</span><b>${fmt(p.stocked_managed_parts,0)}종</b></div>
              <div class="risk-row"><span>총 재고수량</span><b>${fmt(p.total_stock_units,0)}개</b></div>`;
          }
          function resultCard(label,one){
            return `<div class="risk-result-card"><div class="risk-result-title">${label} 대안</div><div class="risk-result-metrics">
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">필요 재고비용</div><div class="risk-result-value">${one.stock_cost_text||'-'}</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">선택 해 Ao</div><div class="risk-result-value">${Number(one.nominal_Ao||0).toFixed(4)}</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">미래 평균 Ao</div><div class="risk-result-value">${Number(one.mean_Ao||0).toFixed(4)}</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">목표 Ao 달성률</div><div class="risk-result-value">${fmt(Number(one.target_Ao_achievement_rate||0)*100,2)}%</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">평균 부족 발생 횟수</div><div class="risk-result-value">${fmt(one.mean_shortage_events??one.mean_shortage_units,2)}회</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">전체 수요 중 부족 비율</div><div class="risk-result-value">${fmt(Number(one.mean_shortage_rate||0)*100,2)}%</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">중요도 가중 부족률</div><div class="risk-result-value">${fmt(Number(one.mean_weighted_shortage_rate||0)*100,2)}%</div></div>
              <div class="risk-result-metric"><div class="risk-result-label">평균 대기 DT</div><div class="risk-result-value">${fmt(one.mean_DT_wait_h,2)} h</div></div>
            </div></div>`;
          }
          function setupCanvas(canvas){
            const dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1; const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
            const w=Math.max(420,Math.floor(rect.width)); const h=230;
            canvas.width=Math.floor(w*dpr); canvas.height=Math.floor(h*dpr);
            const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'); ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0); return {ctx,w,h};
          }
          function niceRange(vals){
            const f=vals.filter(Number.isFinite); if(!f.length) return [0,1]; let lo=Math.min(...f), hi=Math.max(...f);
            if(hi<=lo){const d=Math.abs(lo)*0.02||1; return [lo-d,hi+d];} const p=(hi-lo)*0.06; return [lo-p,hi+p];
          }
          function drawAxes(ctx,w,h,xLabel,yLabel,ymin,ymax){
            const L=54,R=14,T=12,B=34; ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h); ctx.strokeStyle='#dfe6dd';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.font='10px Malgun Gothic';ctx.fillStyle='#667568';
            for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const y=T+i*(h-T-B)/4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(L,y);ctx.lineTo(w-R,y);ctx.stroke();const v=ymax-i*(ymax-ymin)/4;ctx.fillText(Number(v).toFixed(Math.abs(ymax-ymin)<1?3:1),4,y+3);}
            ctx.strokeStyle='#839287';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(L,T);ctx.lineTo(L,h-B);ctx.lineTo(w-R,h-B);ctx.stroke();
            ctx.fillText('1',L,h-12);ctx.fillText('10,000',w-R-34,h-12);ctx.fillText(xLabel,Math.max(L,(w-ctx.measureText(xLabel).width)/2),h-2);ctx.save();ctx.translate(11,h/2);ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);ctx.fillText(yLabel,-ctx.measureText(yLabel).width/2,0);ctx.restore(); return {L,R,T,B};
          }
          function drawSeries(canvas,a,b,yLabel){
            if(!canvas) return; const {ctx,w,h}=setupCanvas(canvas); const vals=[...a,...b].map(Number); const [ymin,ymax]=niceRange(vals); const m=drawAxes(ctx,w,h,'시뮬레이션 횟수',yLabel,ymin,ymax); const n=Math.min(a.length,b.length); if(!n)return;
            const sx=i=>m.L+i/(Math.max(1,n-1))*(w-m.L-m.R); const sy=v=>m.T+(ymax-v)/(ymax-ymin)*(h-m.T-m.B);
            [[a,'#2f67c7'],[b,'#c53b3b']].forEach(([arr,color])=>{ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=.8;ctx.globalAlpha=.72;ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const v=Number(arr[i]);if(!Number.isFinite(v))continue;const x=sx(i),y=sy(v);if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(x,y);else ctx.lineTo(x,y);}ctx.stroke();});ctx.globalAlpha=1;
          }
          function histogram(vals,lo,hi,bins=40){const out=new Array(bins).fill(0);const span=Math.max(hi-lo,1e-12);vals.forEach(v=>{v=Number(v);if(!Number.isFinite(v))return;let k=Math.floor((v-lo)/span*bins);k=Math.max(0,Math.min(bins-1,k));out[k]++;});return out;}
          function drawHistogram(canvas,a,b,xLabel){
            if(!canvas)return;const {ctx,w,h}=setupCanvas(canvas);const vals=[...a,...b].map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite);if(!vals.length)return;const [lo,hi]=niceRange(vals);const ha=histogram(a,lo,hi),hb=histogram(b,lo,hi);const ymax=Math.max(1,...ha,...hb);const L=50,R=14,T=12,B=34;ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.strokeStyle='#e2e8df';ctx.font='10px Malgun Gothic';ctx.fillStyle='#667568';for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const y=T+i*(h-T-B)/4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(L,y);ctx.lineTo(w-R,y);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillText(String(Math.round(ymax-i*ymax/4)),5,y+3);}ctx.strokeStyle='#839287';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(L,T);ctx.lineTo(L,h-B);ctx.lineTo(w-R,h-B);ctx.stroke();const bw=(w-L-R)/ha.length;ctx.globalAlpha=.42;ha.forEach((v,i)=>{ctx.fillStyle='#2f67c7';ctx.fillRect(L+i*bw,h-B-v/ymax*(h-T-B),Math.max(1,bw),v/ymax*(h-T-B));});hb.forEach((v,i)=>{ctx.fillStyle='#c53b3b';ctx.fillRect(L+i*bw,h-B-v/ymax*(h-T-B),Math.max(1,bw),v/ymax*(h-T-B));});ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.fillStyle='#667568';ctx.fillText(Number(lo).toFixed(Math.abs(hi-lo)<1?3:1),L,h-12);ctx.fillText(Number(hi).toFixed(Math.abs(hi-lo)<1?3:1),w-R-32,h-12);ctx.fillText(xLabel,Math.max(L,(w-ctx.measureText(xLabel).width)/2),h-2);
          }
          function arrStats(arr){
            const a=(arr||[]).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite); if(!a.length) return {mean:0,median:0,std:0,min:0,max:0};
            const mean=a.reduce((s,v)=>s+v,0)/a.length; const sorted=[...a].sort((x,y)=>x-y);
            const mid=Math.floor(sorted.length/2); const median=sorted.length%2?sorted[mid]:(sorted[mid-1]+sorted[mid])/2;
            const std=Math.sqrt(a.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)*(v-mean),0)/a.length);
            return {mean,median,std,min:sorted[0],max:sorted[sorted.length-1]};
          }
          function setGraphInterpretations(sc){
            const aa=arrStats(sc.A_Ao||[]), ba=arrStats(sc.B_Ao||[]), as=arrStats(sc.A_shortage_events||[]), bs=arrStats(sc.B_shortage_events||[]);
            const aoWho=aa.mean>ba.mean?'A':(ba.mean>aa.mean?'B':'A/B'); const aoGap=Math.abs(aa.mean-ba.mean)*100;
            const shortWho=as.mean<bs.mean?'A':(bs.mean<as.mean?'B':'A/B'); const shortGap=Math.abs(as.mean-bs.mean);
            const aoSpreadWho=aa.std<ba.std?'A':(ba.std<aa.std?'B':'A/B');
            const shortSpreadWho=as.std<bs.std?'A':(bs.std<as.std?'B':'A/B');
            const q=(sel,txt)=>{const el=root.querySelector(sel); if(el) el.textContent=txt;};
            q('.risk-interpret-ao-line', aoWho==='A/B' ? `A와 B의 10,000회 평균 운용가용도는 거의 같음. A 평균 ${(aa.mean*100).toFixed(2)}%, B 평균 ${(ba.mean*100).toFixed(2)}%임.` : `${aoWho}가 10,000회 전반에서 평균 운용가용도가 더 높게 나타남. 평균 차이는 약 ${aoGap.toFixed(2)}%p임.`);
            q('.risk-interpret-short-line', shortWho==='A/B' ? `A와 B의 시나리오당 평균 부족 발생 횟수는 거의 같음. A ${as.mean.toFixed(1)}회, B ${bs.mean.toFixed(1)}회임.` : `${shortWho}가 대부분의 시나리오에서 부족 발생 횟수가 더 낮은 쪽에 위치함. 시나리오당 평균 차이는 약 ${shortGap.toFixed(1)}회임.`);
            q('.risk-interpret-ao-hist', aoSpreadWho==='A/B' ? `두 대안의 운용가용도 분포 폭은 비슷함. 중앙값은 A ${(aa.median*100).toFixed(2)}%, B ${(ba.median*100).toFixed(2)}%임.` : `${aoSpreadWho}의 운용가용도 분포가 더 좁아 미래 결과의 변동성이 상대적으로 작음. A 표준편차 ${(aa.std*100).toFixed(2)}%p, B ${(ba.std*100).toFixed(2)}%p임.`);
            q('.risk-interpret-short-hist', shortSpreadWho==='A/B' ? `A와 B의 부족 횟수 분포 폭은 비슷함. 중앙값은 A ${as.median.toFixed(1)}회, B ${bs.median.toFixed(1)}회임.` : `${shortSpreadWho}의 부족 횟수 분포가 더 좁아 부족 발생 수준이 상대적으로 안정적임. 중앙값은 A ${as.median.toFixed(1)}회, B ${bs.median.toFixed(1)}회임.`);
          }
          function drawGraphs(){
            const sc=lastResult?.scenario||null;if(!sc)return;
            drawSeries(root.querySelector('.risk-ao-line'),sc.A_Ao||[],sc.B_Ao||[],'Ao');
            drawSeries(root.querySelector('.risk-short-line'),sc.A_shortage_events||[],sc.B_shortage_events||[],'부족 발생 횟수');
            drawHistogram(root.querySelector('.risk-ao-hist'),sc.A_Ao||[],sc.B_Ao||[],'Ao 분포');
            drawHistogram(root.querySelector('.risk-short-hist'),sc.A_shortage_events||[],sc.B_shortage_events||[],'부족 발생 횟수 분포');
            setGraphInterpretations(sc);
          }
          function graphHtml(){return `<div class="risk-graphs">
            <div class="risk-graph-card"><div class="risk-graph-title">10,000회 운용가용도 결과</div><div class="risk-graph-sub">X축: 시뮬레이션 횟수 · Y축: 운용가용도(Ao)</div><canvas class="risk-canvas risk-ao-line"></canvas><div class="risk-legend"><span class="risk-legend-a">A</span><span class="risk-legend-b">B</span></div><div class="risk-graph-interpret risk-interpret-ao-line"></div></div>
            <div class="risk-graph-card"><div class="risk-graph-title">10,000회 부족 발생 결과</div><div class="risk-graph-sub">X축: 시뮬레이션 횟수 · Y축: 부족 발생 횟수</div><canvas class="risk-canvas risk-short-line"></canvas><div class="risk-legend"><span class="risk-legend-a">A</span><span class="risk-legend-b">B</span></div><div class="risk-graph-interpret risk-interpret-short-line"></div></div>
            <div class="risk-graph-card"><div class="risk-graph-title">운용가용도 분포</div><div class="risk-graph-sub">10,000회 결과가 어느 Ao 구간에 집중되는지 보여줌.</div><canvas class="risk-canvas risk-ao-hist"></canvas><div class="risk-legend"><span class="risk-legend-a">A</span><span class="risk-legend-b">B</span></div><div class="risk-graph-interpret risk-interpret-ao-hist"></div></div>
            <div class="risk-graph-card"><div class="risk-graph-title">부족 발생 횟수 분포</div><div class="risk-graph-sub">10,000회 결과에서 부족 횟수의 분산과 겹침 정도를 보여줌.</div><canvas class="risk-canvas risk-short-hist"></canvas><div class="risk-legend"><span class="risk-legend-a">A</span><span class="risk-legend-b">B</span></div><div class="risk-graph-interpret risk-interpret-short-hist"></div></div>
          </div>`;}
          function renderResults(state){
            if(!lastResult){resultsEl.innerHTML='';return;}const A=state?.A||null,B=state?.B||null;const same=A&&B&&String(A.key||'')===String(lastResult.A_key||'')&&String(B.key||'')===String(lastResult.B_key||'');
            if(!same){resultsEl.innerHTML=`<div class="risk-result-stale">A/B 선택이 변경되었음. 현재 선택으로 다시 시뮬레이션해야 합니다.</div>`;return;}
            resultsEl.innerHTML=`<div class="risk-result-head">시뮬레이션 결과</div><div class="risk-result-note"><b>${lastResult.comparison_sentence||''}</b></div><div class="risk-result-grid">${resultCard('A',lastResult.A||{})}${resultCard('B',lastResult.B||{})}</div>${graphHtml()}`;
            requestAnimationFrame(drawGraphs);
          }
          function render(state){currentState=state||{A:null,B:null};const A=currentState?.A||null,B=currentState?.B||null;const selected=[A?'A':null,B?'B':null].filter(Boolean);status.textContent=A&&B?'A/B 대안이 모두 선택되었음.':`시뮬레이션을 위해 A/B 대안을 선택해야 합니다. 현재 선택: ${selected.length?selected.join(', '):'없음'}`;const ready=!!(A&&B);runBtn.disabled=!ready;const resultMatches=!!(lastResult&&A&&B&&String(lastResult.A_key||'')===String(A.key||'')&&String(lastResult.B_key||'')===String(B.key||''));runBtn.textContent=resultMatches?'시뮬레이션 다시 실행':'시뮬레이션 시작';card(cardA,'A',A);card(cardB,'B',B);renderResults(currentState);}
          runBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{const A=currentState?.A||null,B=currentState?.B||null;if(!(A&&B)||!setStateValue)return;runBtn.disabled=true;runBtn.textContent='시뮬레이션 실행 중';setStateValue('run_request',{A_key:String(A.key||''),B_key:String(B.key||''),token:String(Date.now())});});
          render(readState()||{A:null,B:null}); const timer=window.setInterval(()=>{const state=readState();if(state)render(state);},120); return()=>window.clearInterval(timer);
        }
        """
    )

def _format_krw(value: Any) -> str:
    try:
        v = float(value)
    except Exception:
        return "-"
    sign = "-" if v < 0 else ""
    a = abs(v)
    if a >= 100_000_000:
        return f"{sign}{a / 100_000_000:.2f}억원"
    if a >= 10_000:
        return f"{sign}{a / 10_000:.1f}만원"
    return f"{sign}{a:,.0f}원"


def _build_mc_comparison_sentence(mc_result: dict) -> str:
    A = mc_result.get("A", {}) if isinstance(mc_result, dict) else {}
    B = mc_result.get("B", {}) if isinstance(mc_result, dict) else {}
    a_cost, b_cost = float(A.get("stock_cost", 0.0)), float(B.get("stock_cost", 0.0))
    a_evt, b_evt = float(A.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0)), float(B.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0))
    a_wr, b_wr = float(A.get("mean_weighted_shortage_rate", A.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0))), float(B.get("mean_weighted_shortage_rate", B.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0)))
    a_ao, b_ao = float(A.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)), float(B.get("mean_Ao", 0.0))

    if abs(a_cost - b_cost) < 1e-9:
        cost_phrase = "A와 B의 재고비용은 동일함."
    else:
        high_label, low_label = ("A", "B") if a_cost > b_cost else ("B", "A")
        high, low = (A, B) if high_label == "A" else (B, A)
        diff = abs(a_cost - b_cost)
        high_evt = float(high.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0)); low_evt = float(low.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0))
        high_wr = float(high.get("mean_weighted_shortage_rate", high.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0))); low_wr = float(low.get("mean_weighted_shortage_rate", low.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0)))
        if high_evt < low_evt:
            reduction = (low_evt - high_evt) / max(low_evt, 1e-12) * 100.0
            cost_phrase = f"{high_label} 대안은 {low_label}보다 {_format_krw(diff)}의 추가 재고비용이 필요하지만, 평균 부족 발생을 {low_evt:.2f}회에서 {high_evt:.2f}회로 {reduction:.1f}% 줄임."
        else:
            cost_phrase = f"{high_label} 대안은 {low_label}보다 {_format_krw(diff)}의 추가 재고비용이 필요하지만 평균 부족 발생 개선은 확인되지 않았습니다."
        cost_phrase += f" 중요도 가중 부족률은 {low_wr*100:.2f}%에서 {high_wr*100:.2f}%로 변함."

    return f"{cost_phrase} 미래 평균 Ao는 A {a_ao:.4f}, B {b_ao:.4f}임."

def _render_mc_summary_cards(mc_result: dict) -> None:
    if not isinstance(mc_result, dict):
        return
    A = mc_result.get("A", {})
    B = mc_result.get("B", {})
    iterations = int(mc_result.get("config", {}).get("iterations", 0))

    st.markdown("#### 시뮬레이션 결과")
    st.markdown(
        f"<div class='section-box'><b>{clean_chart_text(_build_mc_comparison_sentence(mc_result))}</b></div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

    left, right = st.columns(2, gap="large")
    for col, label, one in [(left, "A", A), (right, "B", B)]:
        with col:
            st.markdown(f"##### {label} 대안")
            c1, c2 = st.columns(2)
            with c1:
                show_metric_card("필요 재고비용", _format_krw(one.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
            with c2:
                show_metric_card("선택 해 Ao", f"{float(one.get('nominal_Ao', 0.0)):.4f}")
            c3, c4 = st.columns(2)
            with c3:
                show_metric_card("미래 평균 Ao", f"{float(one.get('mean_Ao', 0.0)):.4f}")
            with c4:
                show_metric_card("목표 Ao 달성률", f"{float(one.get('target_Ao_achievement_rate', 0.0))*100:.2f}%")
            c5, c6 = st.columns(2)
            with c5:
                show_metric_card("평균 부족 발생 횟수", f"{float(one.get('mean_shortage_events', one.get('mean_shortage_units', 0.0))):.2f}회")
            with c6:
                show_metric_card("전체 수요 중 부족 비율", f"{float(one.get('mean_shortage_rate', 0.0))*100:.2f}%")
            c7, c8 = st.columns(2)
            with c7:
                show_metric_card("중요도 가중 부족률", f"{float(one.get('mean_weighted_shortage_rate', one.get('mean_shortage_rate', 0.0)))*100:.2f}%")
            with c8:
                show_metric_card("평균 대기 DT", f"{float(one.get('mean_DT_wait_h', 0.0)):,.2f} h")


def _mc_result_frontend_payload(mc_result: dict | None) -> dict | None:
    if not isinstance(mc_result, dict):
        return None
    A = dict(mc_result.get("A", {})); B = dict(mc_result.get("B", {}))
    A["stock_cost_text"] = _format_krw(A.get("stock_cost", 0.0)); B["stock_cost_text"] = _format_krw(B.get("stock_cost", 0.0))
    scenario = {}
    df = mc_result.get("scenario_df")
    if isinstance(df, pd.DataFrame) and not df.empty:
        for c in ["A_Ao", "B_Ao", "A_shortage_events", "B_shortage_events"]:
            if c in df.columns:
                vals = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy()
                scenario[c] = np.round(vals, 6).tolist() if "Ao" in c else vals.astype(int).tolist()
    return {
        "A_key": str(mc_result.get("A_key", "")), "B_key": str(mc_result.get("B_key", "")),
        "iterations": int(mc_result.get("config", {}).get("iterations", 0)),
        "comparison_sentence": _build_mc_comparison_sentence(mc_result), "A": A, "B": B, "scenario": scenario,
        "xai_payload": mc_result.get("xai_payload"),
    }

def _render_risk_analysis_body(result: dict) -> None:
    st.markdown("#### 시뮬레이션 분석")
    st.caption("선택한 두 대안을 동일한 미래 시나리오에서 비교함.")

    c1, c2 = st.columns(2)
    with c1:
        show_metric_card("시뮬레이션", f"{int(st.session_state.risk_mc_iterations):,}회")
    with c2:
        show_metric_card("비교 대상", "A / B 대안")

    readiness = validate_monte_carlo_inputs(result)
    if not bool(readiness.get("ready", False)):
        st.warning(f"시뮬레이션 입력을 확인해 주세요: {readiness.get('reason', '원인 미상')}")
        return

    if _RISK_AB_MIRROR_COMPONENT is None:
        st.warning("현재 실행 환경에서는 A/B 시뮬레이션 선택 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.")
        return

    risk_run_id = str(result.get("_risk_run_id", "default"))
    existing_mc_result = st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result")
    frontend_mc_result = None
    # A new NSGA run clears risk_simulation_result, so any completed Monte Carlo
    # result still present in session_state belongs to the active analysis.
    # Do not hide it only because a UI run-id changed across fragment/full reruns.
    if isinstance(existing_mc_result, dict):
        frontend_mc_result = _mc_result_frontend_payload(existing_mc_result)

    component_result = _RISK_AB_MIRROR_COMPONENT(
        data={"risk_run_id": risk_run_id, "mc_result": frontend_mc_result},
        default={"run_request": None},
        on_run_request_change=(lambda: None),
        key=f"risk_ab_mirror_fullaudit_v2_{risk_run_id}",
        width="stretch",
        height=1280,
    )

    request = getattr(component_result, "run_request", None)
    # WHAT-IF is an independent simulation path. On older Streamlit builds a full-app
    # rerun can briefly replay the risk component's previous browser event. Ignore any
    # such replay during the short isolation window created by WHAT-IF execution.
    _xai_suppress_until = float(st.session_state.get("xai_suppress_risk_request_until", 0.0) or 0.0)
    _xai_risk_request_suppressed = bool(time.time() < _xai_suppress_until)
    if isinstance(request, dict) and not _xai_risk_request_suppressed:
        token = str(request.get("token", ""))
        a_key = str(request.get("A_key", ""))
        b_key = str(request.get("B_key", ""))
        if token and a_key and b_key and token != str(st.session_state.get("risk_last_request_token") or ""):
            st.session_state.risk_last_request_token = token
            with st.spinner(f"A/B 대안을 각각 {int(st.session_state.risk_mc_iterations):,}회 시뮬레이션하고 있음."):
                cfg = MonteCarloConfig(
                    iterations=int(st.session_state.risk_mc_iterations),
                    random_seed=42,
                    stress_factor=1.10,
                    failure_rate_lognormal_sigma=0.0,
                )
                mc_result = run_ab_monte_carlo(result, a_key, b_key, cfg)
                mc_result["risk_run_id"] = risk_run_id
                mc_result["A_key"] = a_key
                mc_result["B_key"] = b_key
                st.session_state.risk_simulation_result = mc_result
                # Build Explainable AI payload immediately from the completed A/B Monte Carlo result.
                # The risk fragment then publishes it to browser storage, so Explainable AI can refresh
                # without a full Streamlit rerun.
                try:
                    _xai_payload = _build_explainable_ab_payload_from_mc(result, mc_result)
                    mc_result["xai_payload"] = _xai_payload
                    st.session_state.risk_xai_payload = _xai_payload
                    st.session_state.risk_simulation_result = mc_result
                except Exception as _xai_build_error:
                    # Keep the simulation result and create a minimum payload from
                    # the completed A/B Monte Carlo summaries.  Item-level graphs
                    # may be empty, but Explainable AI itself must remain usable.
                    _xai_payload = _build_minimum_xai_payload_from_mc(result, mc_result)
                    mc_result["xai_payload"] = _xai_payload
                    mc_result["xai_payload_error"] = str(_xai_build_error)
                    st.session_state.risk_xai_payload = _xai_payload
                    st.session_state.risk_simulation_result = mc_result
                st.session_state.risk_mc_has_run = True
            # IMPORTANT: Explainable AI is rendered outside this risk fragment.
            # A fragment-only rerun updates the risk tab but leaves Explainable AI
            # frozen in its pre-simulation state. Persist the completed MC result
            # into both session_state and the active run_result, then force one
            # full-app rerun so every tab sees the same completed simulation.
            try:
                if isinstance(st.session_state.get("run_result"), dict):
                    st.session_state.run_result["_risk_simulation_result"] = mc_result
                    st.session_state.run_result["_risk_xai_payload"] = mc_result.get("xai_payload")
                result["_risk_simulation_result"] = mc_result
                result["_risk_xai_payload"] = mc_result.get("xai_payload")
            except Exception:
                pass
            # Refresh only the risk fragment.  This updates the button/result area
            # without rerunning the whole Streamlit app or jumping the user's screen.
            try:
                st.rerun(scope="fragment")
            except TypeError:
                # Older Streamlit: no explicit rerun here. The component event has
                # already committed the completed result into session_state.
                pass

    if not isinstance(st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result"), dict):
        st.info("A/B 대안을 선택한 뒤 '시뮬레이션 시작'을 눌러야 합니다.")


_fragment_decorator = getattr(st, "fragment", getattr(st, "experimental_fragment", None))

if _fragment_decorator is not None:
    @_fragment_decorator
    def render_risk_analysis_fragment(result: dict):
        _render_risk_analysis_body(result)
else:
    def render_risk_analysis_fragment(result: dict):
        _render_risk_analysis_body(result)


def _selected_payload_or_default(result: dict, selector_df: pd.DataFrame) -> dict | None:
    payload = st.session_state.get("selected_solution_payload")
    if isinstance(payload, dict):
        return payload

    default_payload = result.get("default_selected_payload") if isinstance(result, dict) else None
    if isinstance(default_payload, dict):
        st.session_state.selected_solution_payload = {
            "target_ao": float(default_payload.get("target_ao", 0.0)),
            "solution_id": int(default_payload.get("solution_id", 0)),
        }
        return st.session_state.selected_solution_payload

    if isinstance(selector_df, pd.DataFrame) and not selector_df.empty:
        row = selector_df.iloc[0]
        payload = {
            "target_ao": float(pd.to_numeric(row.get("target_ao", 0.0), errors="coerce")),
            "solution_id": int(pd.to_numeric(row.get("solution_id", 0), errors="coerce")),
        }
        st.session_state.selected_solution_payload = payload
        return payload
    return None


def _render_clicked_solution_summary(snapshot: dict | None):
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        st.info("선택한 해의 상세결과를 불러올 수 없습니다.")
        return
    summary = snapshot.get("summary", {})
    policy_df = snapshot.get("policy_df", pd.DataFrame())
    render_summary_panel(summary, policy_df)


def _interactive_ce_fragment_body(result: dict, selector_df: pd.DataFrame, chart_key: str, show_summary: bool):
    if not isinstance(selector_df, pd.DataFrame) or selector_df.empty:
        st.info("표시할 분석 해가 없습니다.")
        return

    current_payload = _selected_payload_or_default(result, selector_df)

    # Build once per run, then reuse. Only selectedpoints changes on click.
    fig = _get_ce_base_figure(result, selector_df)
    fig = _apply_persistent_selected_point(fig, current_payload)

    left, right = st.columns([1.65, 1.0], gap="large")

    with left:
        event = st.plotly_chart(
            fig,
            use_container_width=True,
            key=chart_key,
            on_select="rerun",
            selection_mode=("points",),
            config={
                "displaylogo": False,
                "scrollZoom": True,
                "doubleClick": "reset",
                "responsive": True,
            },
        )

    payload = extract_plotly_selected_payload(event)
    if isinstance(payload, dict):
        new_payload = {
            "target_ao": float(payload["target_ao"]),
            "solution_id": int(payload["solution_id"]),
        }
        # Update only if the actual selected point changed.
        if (
            not isinstance(current_payload, dict)
            or round(float(current_payload.get("target_ao", -999.0)), 6) != round(float(new_payload["target_ao"]), 6)
            or int(current_payload.get("solution_id", -999)) != int(new_payload["solution_id"])
        ):
            st.session_state.selected_solution_payload = new_payload
        current_payload = new_payload

    # Snapshot lookup is cached in nsga_engine.py.
    snapshot = get_selected_snapshot(result, current_payload)

    with right:
        if isinstance(snapshot, dict):
            selected = snapshot.get("summary", {})
            st.caption(
                f"현재 선택 해 · 목표 Ao {float(selected.get('target_ao', 0.0)):.2f} · "
                f"해 {int(selected.get('solution_id', 0))} · "
                f"Ao {float(selected.get('Ao', selected.get('best_Ao', 0.0))):.4f}"
