            )
            selected_df = snapshot.get("selected_solution_df", pd.DataFrame())
            if isinstance(selected_df, pd.DataFrame) and not selected_df.empty:
                render_solution_detail_card(selected_df.iloc[0])
            elif show_summary:
                _render_clicked_solution_summary(snapshot)
        else:
            st.warning("선택한 점과 분석결과를 연결하지 못했습니다.")


if _fragment_decorator is not None:
    @_fragment_decorator
    def render_ce_summary_fragment(result: dict, selector_df: pd.DataFrame):
        _interactive_ce_fragment_body(result, selector_df, "ce_curve_click_summary", True)

    @_fragment_decorator
    def render_ce_detail_fragment(result: dict, selector_df: pd.DataFrame):
        _interactive_ce_fragment_body(result, selector_df, "ce_curve_click_detail", False)
else:
    def render_ce_summary_fragment(result: dict, selector_df: pd.DataFrame):
        _interactive_ce_fragment_body(result, selector_df, "ce_curve_click_summary", True)

    def render_ce_detail_fragment(result: dict, selector_df: pd.DataFrame):
        _interactive_ce_fragment_body(result, selector_df, "ce_curve_click_detail", False)


def render_integrated_results(result: dict | None):
    st.markdown("### 통합 결과")
    if not result:
        st.info("아직 실행 결과가 없습니다.")
        return

    if "_risk_run_id" not in result:
        result["_risk_run_id"] = str(time.time_ns())

    tabs = st.tabs(["상세결과", "성과지표 달성지점", "시뮬레이션"])
    all_runs_df = result.get("all_target_runs_df", pd.DataFrame())
    pareto_df = result.get("pareto_df", pd.DataFrame())
    source_df = all_runs_df if isinstance(all_runs_df, pd.DataFrame) and not all_runs_df.empty else pareto_df
    selector_df = build_solution_selector_df(source_df)

    with tabs[0]:
        render_frontend_ce_component(
            result,
            selector_df,
            "ce_frontend_detail",
            show_policy=True,
            expanded=False,
        )

    with tabs[1]:
        render_frontend_ce_component(
            result,
            selector_df,
            "ce_frontend_performance_threshold",
            show_policy=False,
            expanded=True,
            threshold_mode=True,
            threshold_default=0.94,
        )

    with tabs[2]:
        render_risk_analysis_fragment(result)


def _selected_solution_result_for_ai(result: dict | None) -> dict | None:
    """현재 상세결과에서 클릭한 C-E 해의 exact snapshot을 AI 설명용 결과로 반환."""
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        return result
    payload = st.session_state.get("selected_solution_payload")
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return result
    try:
        snapshot = get_selected_snapshot(result, payload)
    except Exception:
        return result
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        return result

    selected_result = dict(result)
    selected_result["summary"] = snapshot.get("summary", result.get("summary", {}))
    selected_result["policy_df"] = snapshot.get("policy_df", result.get("policy_df", pd.DataFrame()))
    selected_result.pop("xai", None)
    selected_result.pop("prescriptive", None)
    return selected_result


def _selected_solution_ai_caption(result: dict | None) -> str:
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        return "선택 해 정보 없음"
    summary = result.get("summary", {}) if isinstance(result.get("summary"), dict) else {}
    ao = _safe_float(summary.get("Ao", summary.get("best_Ao", 0.0)))
    cost = _safe_float(summary.get("total_cost", summary.get("best_cost", 0.0)))
    stock = _safe_int(summary.get("total_stock_units", summary.get("best_total_stock_units", 0)))
    years = _safe_float(summary.get("analysis_years", 0.0))
    return (
        f"현재 선택 해 기준 · Ao {ao*100:.2f}% · "
        f"총 비용 {cost:,.0f}원 · 총 재고 {stock:,}개 · 분석기간 {years:g}년"
    )


def _build_minimum_xai_payload_from_mc(result: dict, mc: dict) -> dict:
    """Build a guaranteed A/B Explainable AI payload without item-vector joins."""
    if not isinstance(mc, dict):
        raise ValueError("Completed Monte Carlo result is unavailable.")
    A = dict(mc.get("A", {})); B = dict(mc.get("B", {}))
    for one in (A, B):
        for key in ["stock_cost", "mean_Ao", "mean_shortage_events", "mean_shortage_rate",
                    "mean_weighted_shortage_rate", "mean_DT_wait_h", "nominal_Ao"]:
            fallback = one.get("mean_shortage_units", 0.0) if key == "mean_shortage_events" else 0.0
            v = pd.to_numeric(one.get(key, fallback), errors="coerce")
            one[key] = float(v) if pd.notna(v) else 0.0
    a_key = str(mc.get("A_key", "")); b_key = str(mc.get("B_key", ""))
    run_id = str(mc.get("risk_run_id", "")) or str(result.get("_risk_run_id", "default") if isinstance(result, dict) else "default")
    scenario = []
    sdf = mc.get("scenario_df")
    wanted = ["simulation_no", "A_Ao", "B_Ao", "A_shortage_events", "B_shortage_events", "A_DT_wait_h", "B_DT_wait_h"]
    if isinstance(sdf, pd.DataFrame) and not sdf.empty and all(c in sdf.columns for c in wanted):
        has_rates = all(c in sdf.columns for c in ["A_shortage_rate", "B_shortage_rate"])
        has_failures = "total_failures" in sdf.columns
        use_cols = wanted + (["A_shortage_rate", "B_shortage_rate"] if has_rates else []) + (["total_failures"] if has_failures else [])
        for rec in sdf[use_cols].to_dict("records"):
            ash = int(rec.get("A_shortage_events", 0)); bsh = int(rec.get("B_shortage_events", 0))
            total_fail = float(rec.get("total_failures", max(ash, bsh, 1)))
            ar = float(rec.get("A_shortage_rate", ash / max(total_fail, 1.0)))
            br = float(rec.get("B_shortage_rate", bsh / max(total_fail, 1.0)))
            scenario.append({
                "n": int(rec.get("simulation_no", len(scenario)+1)),
                "A_Ao": float(rec.get("A_Ao", 0.0)), "B_Ao": float(rec.get("B_Ao", 0.0)),
                "A_short": ash, "B_short": bsh,
                "A_short_rate": ar, "B_short_rate": br,
                "A_DT": float(rec.get("A_DT_wait_h", 0.0)), "B_DT": float(rec.get("B_DT_wait_h", 0.0)),
                "total_failures": total_fail,
                "B_minus_A_short": bsh - ash,
            })
    cost_diff = A.get("stock_cost", 0.0) - B.get("stock_cost", 0.0)
    shortage_diff = B.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0) - A.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0)
    shortage_rate_diff_pp = (B.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0) - A.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0)) * 100.0
    ao_diff_pp = (A.get("mean_Ao", 0.0) - B.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)) * 100.0
    if cost_diff > 0 and shortage_diff > 0:
        headline = "A는 비용을 더 투입하는 대신 미래 재고부족을 줄이는 대안"
    elif cost_diff < 0 and shortage_diff < 0:
        headline = "B는 비용을 더 투입하는 대신 미래 재고부족을 줄이는 대안"
    elif abs(shortage_diff) < 0.1:
        headline = "A/B의 미래 부족 차이는 작고 비용·가용도 차이를 함께 볼 필요가 있는 대안"
    else:
        headline = f"{'A' if shortage_diff > 0 else 'B'}가 미래 재고부족 측면에서 더 안정적인 대안"
    return {
        "risk_run_id": run_id, "A_key": a_key, "B_key": b_key,
        "A": A, "B": B, "scenario": scenario, "item_points": [],
        "headline": headline, "cost_diff": float(cost_diff),
        "shortage_diff": float(shortage_diff),
        "shortage_rate_diff_pp": float(shortage_rate_diff_pp),
        "ao_diff_pp": float(ao_diff_pp),
        "iterations": int(mc.get("config", {}).get("iterations", len(scenario))),
    }


def _build_explainable_ab_payload_from_mc(result: dict, mc: dict) -> dict:
    """Build simulation-first Explainable AI payload for the selected A/B alternatives."""
    if not isinstance(result, dict) or not isinstance(mc, dict):
        raise ValueError("Explainable AI requires an NSGA result and completed Monte Carlo result.")

    run_id = str(result.get("_risk_run_id", "default"))
    mc_run_id = str(mc.get("risk_run_id", ""))
    # A new NSGA run clears risk_simulation_result before execution, so an existing
    # Monte Carlo result belongs to the active analysis session. Do not discard a
    # completed result only because a UI-generated run id changed across rerenders.
    if (not run_id or run_id == "default") and mc_run_id:
        run_id = mc_run_id

    a_key = str(mc.get("A_key", ""))
    b_key = str(mc.get("B_key", ""))
    if not a_key or not b_key:
        raise ValueError("Completed Monte Carlo result is missing A/B solution keys.")

    A = dict(mc.get("A", {})); B = dict(mc.get("B", {}))
    numeric_keys = ["stock_cost", "mean_Ao", "mean_shortage_events", "mean_shortage_rate", "mean_weighted_shortage_rate", "mean_DT_wait_h", "nominal_Ao"]
    for one in (A, B):
        for key in numeric_keys:
            fallback = one.get("mean_shortage_units", 0.0) if key == "mean_shortage_events" else 0.0
            one[key] = float(pd.to_numeric(one.get(key, fallback), errors="coerce") or 0.0)

    # Scenario-level arrays used for simulation-difference chart and separate A/B charts.
    scenario = []
    sdf = mc.get("scenario_df")
    if isinstance(sdf, pd.DataFrame) and not sdf.empty:
        wanted = ["simulation_no", "A_Ao", "B_Ao", "A_shortage_events", "B_shortage_events", "A_DT_wait_h", "B_DT_wait_h"]
        if all(c in sdf.columns for c in wanted):
            has_rates = all(c in sdf.columns for c in ["A_shortage_rate", "B_shortage_rate"])
            has_failures = "total_failures" in sdf.columns
            use_cols = wanted + (["A_shortage_rate", "B_shortage_rate"] if has_rates else []) + (["total_failures"] if has_failures else [])
            for rec in sdf[use_cols].to_dict("records"):
                ash = int(rec.get("A_shortage_events", 0)); bsh = int(rec.get("B_shortage_events", 0))
                total_fail = float(rec.get("total_failures", max(ash, bsh, 1)))
                ar = float(rec.get("A_shortage_rate", ash / max(total_fail, 1.0)))
                br = float(rec.get("B_shortage_rate", bsh / max(total_fail, 1.0)))
                scenario.append({
                    "n": int(rec.get("simulation_no", len(scenario)+1)),
                    "A_Ao": float(rec.get("A_Ao", 0.0)), "B_Ao": float(rec.get("B_Ao", 0.0)),
                    "A_short": ash, "B_short": bsh,
                    "A_short_rate": ar, "B_short_rate": br,
                    "A_DT": float(rec.get("A_DT_wait_h", 0.0)), "B_DT": float(rec.get("B_DT_wait_h", 0.0)),
                    "total_failures": total_fail,
                    "B_minus_A_short": bsh - ash,
                })

    # Merge actual per-item Monte Carlo shortage aggregation with A/B stock decisions and metadata.
    candidate_meta, vectors = _build_frontend_policy_payload(result)
    va = vectors.get(a_key, {}) if isinstance(vectors, dict) else {}
    vb = vectors.get(b_key, {}) if isinstance(vectors, dict) else {}
    item_df = mc.get("item_risk_df")
    item_lookup = {}
    if isinstance(item_df, pd.DataFrame) and not item_df.empty and "candidate_index" in item_df.columns:
        for rec in item_df.to_dict("records"):
            try: item_lookup[int(rec.get("candidate_index"))] = rec
            except Exception: pass

    def _qty(vec, i):
        try:
            return int(vec.get("sO", [])[i]) + int(vec.get("sI", [])[i]) + int(vec.get("sD", [])[i])
        except Exception:
            return 0

    item_points = []
    for i, meta in enumerate(candidate_meta):
        rec = item_lookup.get(i, {})
        qa, qb = _qty(va, i), _qty(vb, i)
        demand = int(pd.to_numeric(rec.get("demand_events", 0), errors="coerce") or 0)
        a_short = int(pd.to_numeric(rec.get("A_shortage_events", 0), errors="coerce") or 0)
        b_short = int(pd.to_numeric(rec.get("B_shortage_events", 0), errors="coerce") or 0)
        diff = b_short - a_short
        item_points.append({
            "candidate_index": int(i),
            "part_id": str(meta.get("part_id", "")),
            "item_name": str(meta.get("item_name", meta.get("category", "")) or ""),
            "A_qty": int(qa), "B_qty": int(qb), "stock_diff": int(qa - qb),
            "demand_events": demand, "A_shortage_events": a_short, "B_shortage_events": b_short,
            "risk_diff_events": int(diff),
            "risk_diff_rate_pp": float(pd.to_numeric(rec.get("B_minus_A_shortage_rate", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0) * 100.0,
            "unit_cost": float(pd.to_numeric(meta.get("proc_cost", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0),
            "lead_time_h": float(pd.to_numeric(meta.get("lead_time", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0),
            "p_need_pct": float(pd.to_numeric(meta.get("p_need_period", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0) * 100.0,
        })
    item_points.sort(key=lambda r: abs(r["risk_diff_events"]), reverse=True)

    # Simulation-first interpretation; no target-Ao/94%-based judgement is used here.
    cost_diff = A["stock_cost"] - B["stock_cost"]
    shortage_diff = B["mean_shortage_events"] - A["mean_shortage_events"]
    shortage_rate_diff_pp = (B["mean_shortage_rate"] - A["mean_shortage_rate"]) * 100.0
    ao_diff_pp = (A["mean_Ao"] - B["mean_Ao"]) * 100.0
    if cost_diff > 0 and shortage_diff > 0:
        headline = f"A는 비용을 더 투입하는 대신 미래 재고부족을 줄이는 대안"
    elif cost_diff < 0 and shortage_diff < 0:
        headline = f"B는 비용을 더 투입하는 대신 미래 재고부족을 줄이는 대안"
    elif abs(shortage_diff) < 0.1:
        headline = "A/B의 미래 부족 차이는 작고 비용·가용도 차이를 함께 볼 필요가 있는 대안"
    else:
        better = "A" if shortage_diff > 0 else "B"
        headline = f"{better}가 미래 재고부족 측면에서 더 안정적인 대안"

    return {
        "risk_run_id": run_id, "A_key": a_key, "B_key": b_key,
        "A": A, "B": B, "scenario": scenario, "item_points": item_points,
        "headline": headline,
        "cost_diff": float(cost_diff),
        "shortage_diff": float(shortage_diff),
        "shortage_rate_diff_pp": float(shortage_rate_diff_pp),
        "ao_diff_pp": float(ao_diff_pp),
        "iterations": int(mc.get("config", {}).get("iterations", len(scenario))),
    }



def _build_explainable_ab_payload(result: dict) -> dict | None:
    mc = st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result")
    if not isinstance(result, dict) or not isinstance(mc, dict):
        return None
    try:
        return _build_explainable_ab_payload_from_mc(result, mc)
    except Exception:
        try:
            return _build_minimum_xai_payload_from_mc(result, mc)
        except Exception:
            return None


_XAI_AB_COMPONENT = None  # Explainable AI is intentionally rendered natively in Streamlit.


def _xai_num(v, default=0.0) -> float:
    try:
        x = float(pd.to_numeric(v, errors="coerce"))
        return x if np.isfinite(x) else float(default)
    except Exception:
        return float(default)


def _xai_strategy_sentence(label: str, self_row: dict, other_row: dict) -> str:
    sc = _xai_num(self_row.get("stock_cost")); oc = _xai_num(other_row.get("stock_cost"))
    sr = _xai_num(self_row.get("mean_shortage_rate")); orr = _xai_num(other_row.get("mean_shortage_rate"))
    sa = _xai_num(self_row.get("mean_Ao")); oa = _xai_num(other_row.get("mean_Ao"))
    more_cost = sc > oc + 1e-9
    less_risk = sr < orr - 1e-12
    higher_ao = sa > oa + 1e-12
    if more_cost and less_risk:
        return f"{label}는 상대 대안보다 더 투자하지만 미래 재고부족을 줄이는 방향의 대안임"
    if (not more_cost) and less_risk:
        return f"{label}는 상대 대안보다 비용이 낮으면서 미래 재고부족도 적은 방향의 대안임"
    if more_cost and (not less_risk) and higher_ao:
        return f"{label}는 투자비용은 더 크지만 미래 운용가용도를 높이는 방향의 대안임"
    if (not more_cost) and (not less_risk):
        return f"{label}는 투자비용을 낮추는 대신 미래 재고부족을 더 감수하는 방향의 대안임"
    return f"{label}는 비용·미래 부족·운용가용도의 차이를 함께 보고 판단해야 하는 대안임"


def _xai_native_payload(result: dict) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    # Primary source: live session state.
    mc = st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result")
    # Durable fallback: the active NSGA result also keeps the latest completed MC
    # result. This protects Explainable AI from fragment/full-rerun timing issues.
    if not isinstance(mc, dict) and isinstance(result, dict):
        mc = result.get("_risk_simulation_result")
    if not isinstance(mc, dict):
        run_result = st.session_state.get("run_result")
        if isinstance(run_result, dict):
            mc = run_result.get("_risk_simulation_result")
    if not isinstance(mc, dict):
        return None, "완료된 A/B 시뮬레이션 결과가 없습니다."

    # Rehydrate the canonical session key when the durable backup was used.
    st.session_state.risk_simulation_result = mc

    # Prefer the full item-level payload. If item/vector linkage is incomplete,
    # fall back to the guaranteed A/B/scenario payload instead of hiding the tab.
    try:
        payload = _build_explainable_ab_payload_from_mc(result, mc)
        st.session_state.risk_xai_payload = payload
        mc["xai_payload"] = payload
        st.session_state.risk_simulation_result = mc
        return payload, None
    except Exception as full_error:
        try:
            payload = _build_minimum_xai_payload_from_mc(result, mc)
            payload["item_points"] = []
            st.session_state.risk_xai_payload = payload
            mc["xai_payload"] = payload
            mc["xai_payload_error"] = str(full_error)
            st.session_state.risk_simulation_result = mc
            return payload, str(full_error)
        except Exception as minimum_error:
            return None, f"Explainable AI 데이터 변환 실패: {minimum_error}"


def _xai_apply_plot_style(fig: go.Figure, x_title: str = "", y_title: str = "", height: int = 340) -> go.Figure:
    fig.update_layout(
        height=height,
        margin=dict(l=45, r=20, t=34, b=45),
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(248,251,247,0.72)",
        font=dict(family="Malgun Gothic", size=11, color="#314638"),
        legend=dict(orientation="h", yanchor="bottom", y=1.02, xanchor="right", x=1),
        hoverlabel=dict(font_family="Malgun Gothic"),
        colorway=["#2F6BFF", "#E66A2C", "#6A9A76", "#9A6BC6"],
    )
    fig.update_xaxes(title=x_title, showgrid=True, gridcolor="#e6ece5", zerolinecolor="#bdc9be")
    fig.update_yaxes(title=y_title, showgrid=True, gridcolor="#e6ece5", zerolinecolor="#bdc9be")
    return fig



def _xai_clickable_plot(fig: go.Figure, key: str, height: int = 455, instruction: str = "점을 클릭하면 해당 점의 의미가 표시됨.") -> None:
    """Plotly click detail stays browser-side; no Streamlit rerun and no annotation replacement."""
    # Symmetric margins keep scatter plots visually centered.  The bottom margin is kept compact
    # because the explanation is rendered immediately below the plot rather than inside it.
    fig.update_layout(
        height=height,
        margin=dict(l=48, r=48, t=62, b=54),
        hovermode="closest",
        clickmode="event",
        uirevision="xai_click_keep_v5",
        selectionrevision="xai_no_selection_v5",
    )
    fig.update_xaxes(automargin=True)
    fig.update_yaxes(automargin=True)

    initial_msg = instruction or "점을 클릭하면 해당 점의 의미가 표시됨."
    post_script = r"""
    (function(){
      var gd = document.getElementById('{plot_id}');
      if (!gd || gd.__xaiClickBoundV5) return;
      gd.__xaiClickBoundV5 = true;
      var initialMsg = __INITIAL_MSG__;
      var box = document.getElementById('{plot_id}_detail');
      function setBox(msg){
        if(!box) return;
        box.textContent = msg || initialMsg;
      }
      setBox(initialMsg);

      gd.on('plotly_click', function(ev){
        if(!ev || !ev.points || !ev.points.length) return;
        var pt = ev.points[0];
        var cd = pt.customdata;
        var msg = '';
        var detail = '';
        if (Array.isArray(cd)) {
          msg = String(cd.length > 0 && cd[0] != null ? cd[0] : '');
          detail = String(cd.length > 1 && cd[1] != null ? cd[1] : msg);
        } else if (cd != null) {
          msg = String(cd);
          detail = msg;
        }
        if (!detail) {
          var name = (pt.data && pt.data.name) ? pt.data.name : '선택 점';
          detail = name + '의 선택 결과임.';
        }
        // Do not relayout annotations here.  Existing explanatory annotations remain visible.
        try { Plotly.restyle(gd, {'selectedpoints': null}); } catch(e) {}
        setBox(detail);
      });

      // Reset axes / Autoscale / double-click restores the original explanation.
      gd.on('plotly_relayout', function(ev){
        if(!ev) return;
        if(ev['xaxis.autorange'] === true || ev['yaxis.autorange'] === true || ev['autosize'] === true){
          setBox(initialMsg);
          try { Plotly.restyle(gd, {'selectedpoints': null}); } catch(e) {}
        }
      });
      gd.on('plotly_doubleclick', function(){
        setBox(initialMsg);
        try { Plotly.restyle(gd, {'selectedpoints': null}); } catch(e) {}
      });
    })();
    """.replace('__INITIAL_MSG__', json.dumps(initial_msg, ensure_ascii=False))

    plot_html = pio.to_html(
        fig,
        full_html=False,
        include_plotlyjs=True,
        config={
            "displaylogo": False,
            "responsive": True,
            "scrollZoom": False,
            "modeBarButtonsToRemove": ["select2d", "lasso2d"],
        },
        post_script=post_script,
        default_height=f"{int(height)}px",
        div_id=f"xai_click_{key}",
    )
    wrapped = (
        '<div style="width:100%;overflow:visible">'
        + plot_html
        + f'<div id="xai_click_{key}_detail" style="margin:2px 12px 0 12px;min-height:28px;max-height:70px;overflow:auto;'
        + 'padding:6px 9px;border:1px solid #dce5da;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(248,251,247,.98);'
        + 'color:#536458;font-family:Malgun Gothic;font-size:10px;line-height:1.4"></div>'
        + '</div>'
    )
    st.components.v1.html(wrapped, height=int(height) + 46, scrolling=False)

def _xai_visual_width(a: float, b: float) -> tuple[float, float]:
    a = max(0.0, float(a)); b = max(0.0, float(b))
    mx = max(a, b)
    if mx <= 1e-12:
        return 50.0, 50.0
    # Keep the smaller side visible while preserving a strong visual difference.
    return 18.0 + 82.0 * (a / mx), 18.0 + 82.0 * (b / mx)



def _xai_compact_money(v: float) -> str:
    v = abs(float(v))
    if v >= 1e8:
        return f"{v/1e8:.2f}억원"
    if v >= 1e4:
        return f"{v/1e4:,.0f}만원"
    return f"{v:,.0f}원"


def _xai_profile_diff_texts(A: dict, B: dict) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    ca, cb = _xai_num(A.get("stock_cost")), _xai_num(B.get("stock_cost"))
    ra, rb = _xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_rate"))*100.0, _xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_rate"))*100.0
    ea, eb = _xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_events")), _xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_events"))
    aa, ab = _xai_num(A.get("mean_Ao"))*100.0, _xai_num(B.get("mean_Ao"))*100.0
    if abs(ca-cb) < 1:
        cost_txt = "A/B 투자비용 유사함"
    else:
        hi = "A" if ca > cb else "B"; lo = min(ca,cb); pct = abs(ca-cb)/max(lo,1.0)*100.0
        cost_txt = f"{hi}가 {_xai_compact_money(ca-cb)} 더 큼 · 약 {pct:.1f}% 차이"
    if abs(ra-rb) < 1e-6:
        short_txt = "A/B 부족 위험 유사함"
    else:
        low = "A" if ra < rb else "B"
        high_rate = max(ra, rb)
        rel_reduction = abs(ra-rb) / max(high_rate, 1e-9) * 100.0
        short_txt = f"{low}가 {abs(ra-rb):.2f}%p 낮음 · 상대적으로 {rel_reduction:.1f}% 감소 · 시나리오당 {abs(ea-eb):.1f}회 차이"
    if abs(aa-ab) < 1e-6:
        ao_txt = "A/B 운용가용도 유사함"
    else:
        hi = "A" if aa > ab else "B"
        ao_txt = f"{hi}가 {abs(aa-ab):.2f}%p 높음"
    return cost_txt, short_txt, ao_txt



def _xai_cov_ellipse_points(x_vals, y_vals, level: float = 0.90, num: int = 240):
    """Return ellipse coordinates for a 2D confidence region based on sample covariance."""
    x = np.asarray(list(x_vals), dtype=float)
    y = np.asarray(list(y_vals), dtype=float)
    mask = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)
    x = x[mask]; y = y[mask]
    if len(x) < 3:
        return np.array([]), np.array([])
    cov = np.cov(np.vstack([x, y]))
    if cov.shape != (2, 2) or not np.all(np.isfinite(cov)):
        return np.array([]), np.array([])
    cov = cov + np.eye(2) * 1e-9
    vals, vecs = np.linalg.eigh(cov)
    order = vals.argsort()[::-1]
    vals = np.clip(vals[order], 1e-12, None)
    vecs = vecs[:, order]
    chi2_lookup = {0.50: 1.38629436112, 0.90: 4.60517018599}
    scale = math.sqrt(chi2_lookup.get(round(float(level), 2), 4.60517018599))
    theta = np.linspace(0, 2 * math.pi, int(num))
    circle = np.vstack([np.cos(theta), np.sin(theta)])
    transform = vecs @ np.diag(np.sqrt(vals) * scale)
    pts = transform @ circle
    mean_x = float(np.mean(x)); mean_y = float(np.mean(y))
    return pts[0] + mean_x, pts[1] + mean_y


def _xai_render_future_scenario_map(A: dict, B: dict, scenario: list[dict] | None):
    st.markdown("#### 미래 운용 시나리오 맵")
    sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario) if scenario else pd.DataFrame()
    if sdf.empty or ("A_Ao" not in sdf.columns) or ("B_Ao" not in sdf.columns):
        st.info("완료된 A/B 시뮬레이션 결과가 있어야 미래 운용 시나리오 맵을 표시할 수 있음.")
        return

    if "A_short_rate" not in sdf.columns:
        total = pd.to_numeric(sdf.get("total_failures", pd.Series(np.ones(len(sdf)))), errors="coerce").fillna(1.0).replace(0, 1.0)
        sdf["A_short_rate"] = pd.to_numeric(sdf.get("A_short", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0) / total
        sdf["B_short_rate"] = pd.to_numeric(sdf.get("B_short", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0) / total

    xa = pd.to_numeric(sdf["A_short_rate"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    ya = pd.to_numeric(sdf["A_Ao"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    xb = pd.to_numeric(sdf["B_short_rate"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    yb = pd.to_numeric(sdf["B_Ao"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0

    fig = go.Figure()
    base_opacity = 0.10 if len(sdf) >= 5000 else 0.18
    fig.add_trace(go.Scattergl(x=xa, y=ya, mode="markers", name="A 시나리오 분포", marker=dict(size=4, color="#2F6BFF", opacity=base_opacity), hovertemplate="A 시뮬레이션<extra></extra>", showlegend=False))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scattergl(x=xb, y=yb, mode="markers", name="B 시나리오 분포", marker=dict(size=4, color="#E66A2C", opacity=base_opacity), hovertemplate="B 시뮬레이션<extra></extra>", showlegend=False))

    for label, xdat, ydat, color, fill, level in [
        ("A 50% 영역", xa, ya, "#2F6BFF", "rgba(47,107,255,0.14)", 0.50),
        ("A 90% 영역", xa, ya, "#2F6BFF", "rgba(47,107,255,0.05)", 0.90),
        ("B 50% 영역", xb, yb, "#E66A2C", "rgba(230,106,44,0.16)", 0.50),
        ("B 90% 영역", xb, yb, "#E66A2C", "rgba(230,106,44,0.06)", 0.90),
    ]:
        ex, ey = _xai_cov_ellipse_points(xdat, ydat, level=level)
        if len(ex):
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=ex, y=ey, mode="lines", name=label, fill="toself", fillcolor=fill, line=dict(color=color, width=2 if level == 0.50 else 1.5, dash="solid" if level == 0.50 else "dot"), hovertemplate=label + "<extra></extra>"))

    a_mean_x = float(np.nanmean(xa)) if len(xa) else _xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_rate")) * 100.0
    a_mean_y = float(np.nanmean(ya)) if len(ya) else _xai_num(A.get("mean_Ao")) * 100.0
    b_mean_x = float(np.nanmean(xb)) if len(xb) else _xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_rate")) * 100.0
    b_mean_y = float(np.nanmean(yb)) if len(yb) else _xai_num(B.get("mean_Ao")) * 100.0
    delta_short = b_mean_x - a_mean_x
    delta_ao = a_mean_y - b_mean_y

    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[a_mean_x], y=[a_mean_y], mode="markers+text", name="A/B 평균점", text=["A 평균"], textposition="top center", marker=dict(size=14, color="#2F6BFF", line=dict(width=2, color="white")), hovertemplate="A/B 평균점<extra></extra>", legendgroup="mean"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[b_mean_x], y=[b_mean_y], mode="markers+text", name="A/B 평균점", text=["B 평균"], textposition="top center", marker=dict(size=14, color="#E66A2C", line=dict(width=2, color="white")), hovertemplate="A/B 평균점<extra></extra>", legendgroup="mean", showlegend=False))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[a_mean_x, b_mean_x], y=[a_mean_y, b_mean_y], mode="lines+markers", name="평균점 연결선", marker=dict(size=7, color="#6C757D"), line=dict(color="#6C757D", width=2.5, dash="dash"), hovertemplate="평균점 연결선<extra></extra>"))

    mid_x = (a_mean_x + b_mean_x) / 2.0
    mid_y = (a_mean_y + b_mean_y) / 2.0
    fig.add_annotation(x=mid_x, y=mid_y, text=f"Δ 부족 {delta_short:+.2f}%p<br>Δ Ao {delta_ao:+.2f}%p", showarrow=False, bgcolor="rgba(255,255,255,0.92)", bordercolor="#cfd8d0", font=dict(size=11, color="#34463a"))

    fig.update_layout(
        title="미래 운용 시나리오 맵",
        updatemenus=[dict(
            type="buttons", direction="right", x=0.0, y=1.17, xanchor="left", yanchor="top",
            buttons=[
                dict(label="전체", method="update", args=[{"visible": [True, True, True, True, True, True, True, True, True]}, {"title": "미래 운용 시나리오 맵"}]),
                dict(label="영역", method="update", args=[{"visible": [False, False, True, True, True, True, True, True, True]}, {"title": "미래 운용 시나리오 맵"}]),
                dict(label="평균점", method="update", args=[{"visible": [False, False, False, False, False, False, True, True, True]}, {"title": "미래 운용 시나리오 맵"}]),
            ], pad={"r": 8, "t": 0}
        )],
    )
    _xai_apply_plot_style(fig, "재고부족 위험 (%)", "운용가용도 (%)", 560)
    fig.update_layout(margin=dict(l=52, r=28, t=78, b=52), legend=dict(orientation="h", y=1.02, x=1.0, xanchor="right"))
    fig.update_xaxes(automargin=True)
    fig.update_yaxes(automargin=True)
    st.plotly_chart(fig, use_container_width=True, key="xai_future_scenario_map")
    # Simple, data-driven interpretation under the representative map.
    a90x, a90y = _xai_cov_ellipse_points(xa, ya, level=0.90)
    b90x, b90y = _xai_cov_ellipse_points(xb, yb, level=0.90)
    def _ellipse_area(ex, ey):
        if len(ex) < 3 or len(ey) < 3:
            return float("nan")
        # polygon area; used only to compare relative spread of the two 90% regions
        return 0.5 * abs(float(np.dot(ex, np.roll(ey, 1)) - np.dot(ey, np.roll(ex, 1))))
    a_area = _ellipse_area(a90x, a90y)
    b_area = _ellipse_area(b90x, b90y)
    low_short_side = "A" if a_mean_x < b_mean_x else ("B" if b_mean_x < a_mean_x else "A/B")
    high_ao_side = "A" if a_mean_y > b_mean_y else ("B" if b_mean_y > a_mean_y else "A/B")
    if np.isfinite(a_area) and np.isfinite(b_area) and max(a_area, b_area) > 1e-12:
        tight_side = "A" if a_area < b_area else ("B" if b_area < a_area else "A/B")
        spread_pct = abs(a_area-b_area)/max(a_area,b_area)*100.0
        spread_txt = f"90% 영역은 {tight_side}가 상대적으로 더 좁고 약 {spread_pct:.1f}% 덜 퍼져 있음." if tight_side != "A/B" else "A/B의 90% 영역 크기가 유사함."
    else:
        spread_txt = "A/B 90% 영역의 퍼짐을 함께 비교해 결과 변동성을 확인할 수 있음."
    interpretation = (
        f"평균 기준으로 {low_short_side}의 재고부족 위험이 더 낮고, {high_ao_side}의 운용가용도가 더 높게 형성됨. "
        f"A/B 평균점 차이는 부족 {abs(delta_short):.2f}%p, Ao {abs(delta_ao):.2f}%p 수준임. {spread_txt}"
    )
    st.markdown(
        f"<div style='margin-top:-4px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #dfe7de;border-radius:10px;background:#f8fbf7;font-size:0.84rem;color:#526258;line-height:1.55;'><b style='color:#324d39;'>간단 해석</b><br>{interpretation}</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )


def _xai_render_cost_shortage_map(A: dict, B: dict, scenario: list[dict] | None):
    """Investment-cost vs shortage-risk decision map using the completed A/B Monte Carlo distribution."""
    st.markdown("#### 투자비용-재고부족 위험 맵")
    sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario) if scenario else pd.DataFrame()
    if sdf.empty:
        st.info("완료된 A/B 시뮬레이션 결과가 있어야 투자비용-재고부족 위험 맵을 표시할 수 있습니다.")
        return

    if "A_short_rate" not in sdf.columns:
        total = pd.to_numeric(sdf.get("total_failures", pd.Series(np.ones(len(sdf)))), errors="coerce").fillna(1.0).replace(0, 1.0)
        sdf["A_short_rate"] = pd.to_numeric(sdf.get("A_short", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0) / total
        sdf["B_short_rate"] = pd.to_numeric(sdf.get("B_short", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0) / total

    a_risk = pd.to_numeric(sdf["A_short_rate"], errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    b_risk = pd.to_numeric(sdf["B_short_rate"], errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    if len(a_risk) == 0 or len(b_risk) == 0:
        st.info("재고부족 위험 분포를 계산할 수 있는 시뮬레이션 데이터가 없습니다.")
        return

    cost_a = _xai_num(A.get("stock_cost")) / 1e8
    cost_b = _xai_num(B.get("stock_cost")) / 1e8
    gap = abs(cost_b - cost_a)
    scale = max(abs(cost_a), abs(cost_b), 1.0)
    visual_span = max(gap, scale * 0.12, 0.15)
    jitter_w = visual_span * 0.055
    band_w_90 = visual_span * 0.075
    band_w_50 = visual_span * 0.050

    # deterministic display-only jitter; actual investment cost stays fixed per alternative
    na = len(a_risk); nb = len(b_risk)
    ja = (((np.arange(na) * 37) % 101) - 50) / 50.0 * jitter_w
    jb = (((np.arange(nb) * 53) % 101) - 50) / 50.0 * jitter_w

    def q(v, p):
        return float(np.nanquantile(v, p))
    a05, a25, a50, a75, a95 = [q(a_risk, p) for p in (0.05,0.25,0.50,0.75,0.95)]
    b05, b25, b50, b75, b95 = [q(b_risk, p) for p in (0.05,0.25,0.50,0.75,0.95)]
    a_mean = float(np.nanmean(a_risk)); b_mean = float(np.nanmean(b_risk))

    fig = go.Figure()
    fig.add_trace(go.Scattergl(
        x=cost_a + ja, y=a_risk, mode="markers", name="A 시나리오 분포",
        marker=dict(size=4, color="#2F6BFF", opacity=0.11), showlegend=False,
        hovertemplate="A 시뮬레이션<extra></extra>"
    ))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scattergl(
        x=cost_b + jb, y=b_risk, mode="markers", name="B 시나리오 분포",
        marker=dict(size=4, color="#E66A2C", opacity=0.11), showlegend=False,
        hovertemplate="B 시뮬레이션<extra></extra>"
    ))

    def band_trace(cx, y0, y1, half_w, label, line_color, fill_color, dash="dot"):
        xs = [cx-half_w, cx+half_w, cx+half_w, cx-half_w, cx-half_w]
        ys = [y0, y0, y1, y1, y0]
        return go.Scatter(x=xs, y=ys, mode="lines", name=label, fill="toself",
                          fillcolor=fill_color, line=dict(color=line_color, width=2, dash=dash),
                          hovertemplate=label + "<extra></extra>")

    fig.add_trace(band_trace(cost_a, a25, a75, band_w_50, "A 50% 영역", "#2F6BFF", "rgba(47,107,255,0.17)", "solid"))
    fig.add_trace(band_trace(cost_a, a05, a95, band_w_90, "A 90% 영역", "#2F6BFF", "rgba(47,107,255,0.055)", "dot"))
    fig.add_trace(band_trace(cost_b, b25, b75, band_w_50, "B 50% 영역", "#E66A2C", "rgba(230,106,44,0.18)", "solid"))
    fig.add_trace(band_trace(cost_b, b05, b95, band_w_90, "B 90% 영역", "#E66A2C", "rgba(230,106,44,0.06)", "dot"))

    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[cost_a], y=[a_mean], mode="markers+text", name="A/B 평균점",
                             text=["A 평균"], textposition="top center",
                             marker=dict(size=15,color="#2F6BFF",line=dict(width=2,color="white")),
                             hovertemplate="A/B 평균점<extra></extra>"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[cost_b], y=[b_mean], mode="markers+text", name="A/B 평균점",
                             text=["B 평균"], textposition="top center", showlegend=False,
                             marker=dict(size=15,color="#E66A2C",line=dict(width=2,color="white")),
                             hovertemplate="A/B 평균점<extra></extra>"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[cost_a,cost_b], y=[a_mean,b_mean], mode="lines+markers",
                             name="평균점 연결선", line=dict(color="#6C757D",width=2.5,dash="dash"),
                             marker=dict(size=7,color="#6C757D"), hovertemplate="평균점 연결선<extra></extra>"))

    delta_cost = cost_b - cost_a
    delta_risk = b_mean - a_mean
    fig.add_annotation(
        x=(cost_a+cost_b)/2.0, y=(a_mean+b_mean)/2.0,
        text=f"Δ 비용 {delta_cost:+.2f}억원<br>Δ 부족 {delta_risk:+.2f}%p",
        showarrow=False, bgcolor="rgba(255,255,255,.94)", bordercolor="#cad5cc",
        font=dict(size=11,color="#33463a")
    )

    visible_all = [True]*9
    visible_50 = [False,False,True,False,True,False,True,True,True]
    visible_90 = [False,False,False,True,False,True,True,True,True]
    visible_mean = [False,False,False,False,False,False,True,True,True]
    fig.update_layout(
        title="투자비용-재고부족 위험 맵",
        updatemenus=[dict(
            type="buttons", direction="right", x=0.0, y=1.17, xanchor="left", yanchor="top",
            buttons=[
                dict(label="전체 보기", method="update", args=[{"visible":visible_all}]),
                dict(label="50% 영역", method="update", args=[{"visible":visible_50}]),
                dict(label="90% 영역", method="update", args=[{"visible":visible_90}]),
                dict(label="평균 비교", method="update", args=[{"visible":visible_mean}]),
            ]
        )]
    )
    _xai_apply_plot_style(fig, "투자비용 (억원)", "미래 재고부족 위험 (%)", 560)
    fig.update_layout(margin=dict(l=55,r=30,t=80,b=55),legend=dict(orientation="h",y=1.02,x=1,xanchor="right"))
    fig.update_xaxes(automargin=True)
    fig.update_yaxes(automargin=True)
    st.plotly_chart(fig,use_container_width=True,key="xai_cost_shortage_decision_map")

    # Short graph interpretation intentionally kept in ~음/~슴 style.
    expensive = "A" if cost_a > cost_b else ("B" if cost_b > cost_a else "A/B")
    safer = "A" if a_mean < b_mean else ("B" if b_mean < a_mean else "A/B")
    direction = "오른쪽 아래" if ((cost_b-cost_a)*(b_mean-a_mean) < 0 and cost_b != cost_a) else ("오른쪽 위" if cost_b>cost_a and b_mean>a_mean else "수평 또는 유사한 방향")
    if expensive != "A/B" and safer != "A/B":
        interp = f"{expensive}가 더 높은 투자비용 위치에 있고, {safer}의 평균 재고부족 위험이 더 낮게 형성됨. 평균점 연결선은 {direction} 방향으로 나타남. Δ 비용은 {abs(delta_cost):.2f}억원, Δ 부족은 {abs(delta_risk):.2f}%p 수준임."
    else:
        interp = f"A/B 평균점 위치와 50%·90% 영역을 함께 보면 투자비용 차이와 재고부족 위험 분포 차이를 동시에 비교할 수 있음. Δ 비용은 {abs(delta_cost):.2f}억원, Δ 부족은 {abs(delta_risk):.2f}%p 수준임."
    st.markdown(
        f"<div style='margin-top:-4px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #dfe7de;border-radius:10px;background:#f8fbf7;font-size:0.84rem;color:#526258;line-height:1.55;'><b style='color:#324d39;'>간단 해석</b><br>{interp}</div>",
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )



def _xai_render_future_risk_coverage_map(A: dict, B: dict, scenario: list[dict] | None):
    """Show how lower-tail Ao changes as the future becomes more adverse."""
    st.markdown("#### 미래 리스크 커버리지 맵")
    sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario) if scenario else pd.DataFrame()
    if sdf.empty or "A_Ao" not in sdf.columns or "B_Ao" not in sdf.columns:
        st.info("완료된 A/B 시뮬레이션 결과가 있어야 미래 리스크 커버리지 맵을 표시할 수 있습니다.")
        return

    a = pd.to_numeric(sdf["A_Ao"], errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    b = pd.to_numeric(sdf["B_Ao"], errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    if len(a) < 10 or len(b) < 10:
        st.info("운용가용도 분포 데이터가 부족해 리스크 커버리지 맵을 표시할 수 없습니다.")
        return

    ps = np.arange(1, 51, dtype=float)
    qa = np.quantile(a, ps / 100.0)
    qb = np.quantile(b, ps / 100.0)
    fig = go.Figure()
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=ps, y=qa, mode="lines", name="A Ao 분위곡선",
        line=dict(color="#2F6BFF", width=3),
        hovertemplate="하위 %{x:.0f}% 경계<br>A Ao %{y:.2f}%<extra></extra>"
    ))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=ps, y=qb, mode="lines", name="B Ao 분위곡선",
        line=dict(color="#E66A2C", width=3), fill="tonexty",
        fillcolor="rgba(120,130,140,0.08)",
        hovertemplate="하위 %{x:.0f}% 경계<br>B Ao %{y:.2f}%<extra></extra>"
    ))
    # Key tail markers use actual simulated quantiles.
    for p, label in [(5, "하위 5%"), (10, "하위 10%"), (50, "중앙 50%")]:
        av=float(np.quantile(a,p/100.0)); bv=float(np.quantile(b,p/100.0))
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=[p,p], y=[av,bv], mode="markers+lines", name=label,
            marker=dict(size=10, color=["#2F6BFF","#E66A2C"], line=dict(width=1.5,color="white")),
            line=dict(color="#7C8780", width=1.5, dash="dot"),
            hovertemplate=label+"<br>Ao %{y:.2f}%<extra></extra>"
        ))
        fig.add_annotation(
            x=p, y=(av+bv)/2.0, text=f"Δ Ao {abs(av-bv):.2f}%p",
            showarrow=False, bgcolor="rgba(255,255,255,.90)", bordercolor="#d4ddd5",
            font=dict(size=9,color="#415248")
        )

    fig.add_vline(x=5, line_width=1, line_dash="dot", line_color="#aab6ad")
    fig.add_vline(x=10, line_width=1, line_dash="dot", line_color="#aab6ad")
    fig.update_layout(
        title="미래 리스크 커버리지 맵",
        updatemenus=[dict(
            type="buttons", direction="right", x=0, y=1.16, xanchor="left", yanchor="top",
            buttons=[
                dict(label="전체", method="relayout", args=[{"xaxis.range":[1,50]}]),
                dict(label="하위 5%", method="relayout", args=[{"xaxis.range":[1,5]}]),
                dict(label="하위 10%", method="relayout", args=[{"xaxis.range":[1,10]}]),
                dict(label="중앙 50%", method="relayout", args=[{"xaxis.range":[1,50]}]),
            ]
        )]
    )
    _xai_apply_plot_style(fig, "불리한 미래 누적비율 (%)", "운용가용도 분위값 (%)", 520)
    fig.update_layout(margin=dict(l=58,r=30,t=78,b=56), legend=dict(orientation="h",y=1.02,x=1,xanchor="right"))
    st.plotly_chart(fig, use_container_width=True, key="xai_future_risk_coverage_map")

    a5=float(np.quantile(a,.05)); b5=float(np.quantile(b,.05))
    a10=float(np.quantile(a,.10)); b10=float(np.quantile(b,.10))
    better5="A" if a5>b5 else ("B" if b5>a5 else "A/B")
    better10="A" if a10>b10 else ("B" if b10>a10 else "A/B")
    st.markdown(
        f"<div style='margin-top:-4px;padding:9px 12px;border:1px solid #dfe7de;border-radius:10px;background:#f8fbf7;font-size:.83rem;color:#526258;line-height:1.5;'>"
        f"하위 5% 미래에서는 {better5}가 Ao를 {abs(a5-b5):.2f}%p 더 높게 유지함. 하위 10%에서도 {better10}가 {abs(a10-b10):.2f}%p 더 높게 유지함. "
        f"왼쪽으로 갈수록 더 불리한 미래 구간이며 두 곡선 간 간격이 클수록 해당 구간에서 A/B 방어력 차이가 큼."
        f"</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True
    )


def _xai_render_investment_ao_defense_curve(A: dict, B: dict, scenario: list[dict] | None):
    """Compare actual A/B investment positions against mean and lower-tail Ao."""
    st.markdown("#### 투자-Ao 방어 곡선")
    sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario) if scenario else pd.DataFrame()
    if sdf.empty or "A_Ao" not in sdf.columns or "B_Ao" not in sdf.columns:
        st.info("완료된 A/B 시뮬레이션 결과가 있어야 투자-Ao 방어 곡선을 표시할 수 있습니다.")
        return
    a = pd.to_numeric(sdf["A_Ao"], errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    b = pd.to_numeric(sdf["B_Ao"], errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float) * 100.0
    cost_a=_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost"))/1e8; cost_b=_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost"))/1e8
    xs=[cost_a,cost_b]
    rows=[
        ("평균 Ao", [float(np.mean(a)),float(np.mean(b))], "solid", "#5D6B63"),
        ("하위 10% Ao", [float(np.quantile(a,.10)),float(np.quantile(b,.10))], "dash", "#6A9A76"),
        ("하위 5% Ao", [float(np.quantile(a,.05)),float(np.quantile(b,.05))], "dot", "#B06E55"),
    ]
    fig=go.Figure()
    for label, ys, dash, color in rows:
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
            x=xs,y=ys,mode="lines+markers+text",name=label,
            text=["A","B"],textposition="top center",
            marker=dict(size=13,color=["#2F6BFF","#E66A2C"],line=dict(width=2,color="white")),
            line=dict(color=color,width=2.5,dash=dash),
            hovertemplate=label+"<br>투자비용 %{x:.2f}억원<br>Ao %{y:.2f}%<extra></extra>"
        ))
    # one decision annotation for the worst-tail view
    a5,b5=rows[2][1]
    fig.add_annotation(
        x=(cost_a+cost_b)/2.0,y=(a5+b5)/2.0,
        text=f"Δ 비용 {cost_b-cost_a:+.2f}억원<br>하위 5% Ao Δ {b5-a5:+.2f}%p",
        showarrow=False,bgcolor="rgba(255,255,255,.93)",bordercolor="#ccd7ce",font=dict(size=10,color="#34463a")
    )
    fig.update_layout(
        title="투자-Ao 방어 곡선",
        updatemenus=[dict(type="buttons",direction="right",x=0,y=1.16,xanchor="left",yanchor="top",buttons=[
            dict(label="전체",method="update",args=[{"visible":[True,True,True]}]),
            dict(label="평균",method="update",args=[{"visible":[True,False,False]}]),
            dict(label="하위 10%",method="update",args=[{"visible":[False,True,False]}]),
            dict(label="하위 5%",method="update",args=[{"visible":[False,False,True]}]),
        ])]
    )
    _xai_apply_plot_style(fig,"투자비용 (억원)","운용가용도 (%)",500)
    fig.update_layout(margin=dict(l=58,r=30,t=78,b=56),legend=dict(orientation="h",y=1.02,x=1,xanchor="right"))
    st.plotly_chart(fig,use_container_width=True,key="xai_investment_ao_defense_curve")
    higher="A" if cost_a>cost_b else ("B" if cost_b>cost_a else "A/B")
    tail_better="A" if a5>b5 else ("B" if b5>a5 else "A/B")
    st.markdown(
        f"<div style='margin-top:-4px;padding:9px 12px;border:1px solid #dfe7de;border-radius:10px;background:#f8fbf7;font-size:.83rem;color:#526258;line-height:1.5;'>"
        f"{higher}가 더 높은 투자비용 위치에 있음. 하위 5%의 불리한 미래에서는 {tail_better}가 Ao를 {abs(a5-b5):.2f}%p 더 높게 유지함. "
        f"현재 실제 시뮬레이션을 완료한 A/B 두 투자위치만 연결했으며 중간 투자금액을 임의로 보간하지 않음."
        f"</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True
    )


def _xai_render_incremental_investment_composition(A: dict, B: dict, idf: pd.DataFrame):
    """Show where the actual incremental A/B inventory investment is allocated."""
    st.markdown("#### 추가 투자 구성")
    if not isinstance(idf,pd.DataFrame) or idf.empty:
        st.info("품목별 A/B 재고 차이가 있어야 추가 투자 구성을 표시할 수 있습니다.")
        return
    cost_a=_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost")); cost_b=_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost"))
    high="A" if cost_a>cost_b else ("B" if cost_b>cost_a else "A")
    low="B" if high=="A" else "A"
    work=idf.copy()
    if high=="A":
        work["추가수량"]=work["stock_diff"].clip(lower=0)
        work["부족감소기여"]=work["risk_diff_events"].clip(lower=0)
    else:
        work["추가수량"]=(-work["stock_diff"]).clip(lower=0)
        work["부족감소기여"]=(-work["risk_diff_events"]).clip(lower=0)
    work["추가비용"]=work["추가수량"]*work["unit_cost"].clip(lower=0)
    work=work[work["추가수량"]>0].copy()
    if work.empty:
        st.info(f"{high} 대안에서 {low} 대비 추가 확보된 수리부속이 없어 구성 그래프를 표시할 수 없습니다.")
        return
    # keep all positive-cost items; fallback to quantity when unit cost is missing
    values=work["추가비용"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    if float(np.nansum(values))<=0:
        values=work["추가수량"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        value_label="추가수량"
    else:
        value_label="추가비용"
    custom=np.column_stack([
        work["추가수량"].round().astype(int).astype(str),
        work["추가비용"].round().astype(int).astype(str),
        work["부족감소기여"].round().astype(int).astype(str),
    ])
    fig=go.Figure(go.Treemap(
        labels=work["display_name"].astype(str),parents=[""]*len(work),values=values,
        customdata=custom,
        texttemplate="%{label}<br>%{percentRoot:.1%}",
        hovertemplate="%{label}<br>추가수량 %{customdata[0]}개<br>추가비용 %{customdata[1]}원<br>10,000회 부족감소 기여 %{customdata[2]}회<extra></extra>",
        marker=dict(colors=work["부족감소기여"],colorscale=[[0,"#eef3ef"],[1,"#4e8a62"]],line=dict(width=1,color="white")),
    ))
    fig.update_layout(height=500,margin=dict(l=10,r=10,t=48,b=10),title=f"추가 투자 구성 · {low} → {high}",font=dict(family="Malgun Gothic",size=11,color="#314638"),paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)")
    st.plotly_chart(fig,use_container_width=True,key="xai_incremental_investment_composition")
    top=work.sort_values(["추가비용","부족감소기여"],ascending=False).head(12).copy()
    tbl=pd.DataFrame({
        "수리부속명":top["display_name"],
        "추가수량":top["추가수량"].round().astype(int),
        "추가비용":top["추가비용"].round().astype(int),
        "10,000회 부족감소 기여":top["부족감소기여"].round().astype(int),
    })
    st.dataframe(tbl,use_container_width=True,hide_index=True,height=min(360,42+35*max(1,min(len(tbl),9))))
    total_item_cost=float(work["추가비용"].sum())
    total_gap=abs(cost_a-cost_b)
    st.markdown(
        f"<div style='margin-top:-2px;font-size:.82rem;color:#617066;line-height:1.5;'>"
        f"{high}가 {low}보다 더 확보한 CATEGORY 품목을 보여줌. 트리맵 면적은 {value_label} 비중을 의미하고 색이 진할수록 10,000회 시뮬레이션에서 부족감소 기여가 큰 품목임. "
        f"품목 단가×추가수량 기준 합계는 {_xai_compact_money(total_item_cost)}이며 전체 A/B 투자비용 차이({_xai_compact_money(total_gap)})와는 운송비·기타 비용 구성 때문에 차이가 날 수 있음."
        f"</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True
    )

def _xai_shortage_context(A: dict, B: dict) -> tuple[str, str]:
    ra = _xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_rate"))*100.0
    rb = _xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_rate"))*100.0
    ea = _xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_events"))
    eb = _xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_events"))
    if abs(ra-rb) < 1e-9:
        return "A/B 부족 위험이 거의 같은 수준임", "두 대안의 시나리오당 평균 부족 횟수도 거의 같아 부족 위험만으로는 우열 판단이 어려움"
    low = "A" if ra < rb else "B"
    high = "B" if low == "A" else "A"
    low_rate, high_rate = (ra, rb) if low == "A" else (rb, ra)
    low_evt, high_evt = (ea, eb) if low == "A" else (eb, ea)
    rel = (high_rate-low_rate)/max(high_rate,1e-9)*100.0
    title = f"{low}의 부족 위험이 {high}보다 {high_rate-low_rate:.2f}%p 낮음"
    detail = f"{high} {high_rate:.2f}% → {low} {low_rate:.2f}% 수준으로, 상대적으로 약 {rel:.1f}% 감소한 차이임. 시나리오당 평균 부족도 약 {abs(high_evt-low_evt):.1f}회 적어 단순 %p 값보다 실제 차이가 더 직관적으로 보임"
    return title, detail

def _xai_render_compare_header(A: dict, B: dict, payload: dict) -> None:
    """Visual A/B comparison: relative shape first, numeric gap second."""
    cost_a, cost_b = _xai_num(A.get("stock_cost")), _xai_num(B.get("stock_cost"))
    short_a = _xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_rate")) * 100.0
    short_b = _xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_rate")) * 100.0
    ao_a, ao_b = _xai_num(A.get("mean_Ao")) * 100.0, _xai_num(B.get("mean_Ao")) * 100.0
    cw_a, cw_b = _xai_visual_width(cost_a, cost_b)
    sw_a, sw_b = _xai_visual_width(short_a, short_b)
    aw_a, aw_b = _xai_visual_width(ao_a, ao_b)

    cd = _xai_num(payload.get("cost_diff"))
    sd = _xai_num(payload.get("shortage_diff"))
    ad = _xai_num(payload.get("ao_diff_pp"))
    a_sentence = _xai_strategy_sentence("A", A, B)
    b_sentence = _xai_strategy_sentence("B", B, A)
    cost_diff_txt, short_diff_txt, ao_diff_txt = _xai_profile_diff_texts(A, B)
    shortage_note_title, shortage_note_detail = _xai_shortage_context(A, B)

    cost_side = "A" if cost_a > cost_b else ("B" if cost_b > cost_a else "A/B")
    cost_pct = abs(cost_a-cost_b) / max(min(cost_a,cost_b), 1.0) * 100.0 if abs(cost_a-cost_b) >= 1 else 0.0
    short_side = "A" if short_a < short_b else ("B" if short_b < short_a else "A/B")
    short_gap = abs(short_a-short_b)
    event_gap = abs(_xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_events"))-_xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_events")))
    ao_side = "A" if ao_a > ao_b else ("B" if ao_b > ao_a else "A/B")
    ao_gap = abs(ao_a-ao_b)

    def tone(side: str) -> str:
        if side == "A": return "sum-a"
        if side == "B": return "sum-b"
        return "sum-n"

    st.markdown(
        f"""
        <style>
        .xv-wrap{{border:1px solid #dce6db;border-radius:18px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbfdf9,#f3f8f1);padding:18px 20px;margin:6px 0 14px}}
        .xv-head{{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 220px 1fr;align-items:center;gap:14px;margin-bottom:14px}}
        .xv-ab{{font-size:31px;font-weight:950;letter-spacing:-.03em;line-height:1}}
        .xv-a{{color:#2F6BFF;text-align:left}} .xv-b{{color:#E66A2C;text-align:right}}
        .xv-center{{font-size:13px;font-weight:900;color:#43594a;text-align:center;letter-spacing:-.01em}}
        .xv-lane{{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 245px 1fr;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:12px 0}}
        .xv-track{{height:24px;background:#eef2ed;border-radius:999px;overflow:hidden;display:flex;align-items:center}}
        .xv-track.left{{justify-content:flex-end}} .xv-track.right{{justify-content:flex-start}}
        .xv-fill{{height:100%;border-radius:999px;min-width:10px}}
        .xv-fill.a{{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#c7d8ff,#2F6BFF)}}
        .xv-fill.b{{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#E66A2C,#f2c1aa)}}
        .xv-label{{font-size:12px;font-weight:900;color:#304b36;text-align:center;line-height:1.15}}
        .xv-label small{{display:block;font-size:9px;color:#829087;font-weight:700;margin-top:3px}}
        .xv-caption{{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:12px;margin-top:15px;padding-top:13px;border-top:1px solid #e2e9df}}
        .xv-caption>div{{font-size:11px;line-height:1.55;color:#59695d}}
        .xv-caption b{{display:block;font-size:12px;color:#284c31;margin-bottom:3px}}
        .xv-summary{{margin-top:12px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:10px}}
        .xv-summary>div{{border-radius:13px;padding:11px 12px;font-size:10px;text-align:left;min-height:70px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(50,80,50,.04)}}
        .xv-summary strong{{display:block;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:3px}}
        .xv-summary small{{display:block;font-size:10px;margin-top:4px;line-height:1.4;opacity:.84}}
        .xv-risk-note{{margin-top:10px;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 12px;background:#f7faf7;border:1px solid #dde7dc;color:#52665a;font-size:10.5px;line-height:1.55}}
        .xv-risk-note strong{{display:block;font-size:12px;color:#2f4f38;margin-bottom:3px}}
        .sum-a{{border:1px solid #cddcff;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#f4f7ff,#e8efff);color:#254fbc}} .sum-a strong{{color:#2459dd}}
        .sum-b{{border:1px solid #f0d0bd;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff8f4,#fae9df);color:#99502c}} .sum-b strong{{color:#c45620}}
        .sum-n{{border:1px solid #e3e9e1;background:#fff;color:#66756a}} .sum-n strong{{color:#506258}}
        @media(max-width:900px){{.xv-head{{grid-template-columns:1fr 130px 1fr}}.xv-lane{{grid-template-columns:1fr 180px 1fr}}.xv-caption,.xv-summary{{grid-template-columns:1fr}}}}
        </style>
        <div class="xv-wrap">
          <div class="xv-head"><div class="xv-ab xv-a">A</div><div class="xv-center">A/B 미래 시뮬레이션 결과</div><div class="xv-ab xv-b">B</div></div>
          <div class="xv-lane"><div class="xv-track left"><span class="xv-fill a" style="width:{cw_a:.1f}%"></span></div><div class="xv-label">투자비용<small>{cost_diff_txt}</small></div><div class="xv-track right"><span class="xv-fill b" style="width:{cw_b:.1f}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="xv-lane"><div class="xv-track left"><span class="xv-fill a" style="width:{sw_a:.1f}%"></span></div><div class="xv-label">미래 재고부족 위험<small>{short_diff_txt}</small></div><div class="xv-track right"><span class="xv-fill b" style="width:{sw_b:.1f}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="xv-lane"><div class="xv-track left"><span class="xv-fill a" style="width:{aw_a:.1f}%"></span></div><div class="xv-label">운용가용도<small>{ao_diff_txt}</small></div><div class="xv-track right"><span class="xv-fill b" style="width:{aw_b:.1f}%"></span></div></div>
          <div class="xv-caption"><div><b>A 대안</b>{a_sentence}</div><div style="text-align:right"><b>B 대안</b>{b_sentence}</div></div>
          <div class="xv-summary">
            <div class="{tone(cost_side)}"><strong>{cost_side} 투자비용이 더 큼</strong>{_xai_compact_money(abs(cost_a-cost_b))} 차이<small>{('약 %.1f%% 차이임' % cost_pct) if cost_pct>0 else '두 대안 투자비용이 유사함'}</small></div>
            <div class="{tone(short_side)}"><strong>{short_side} 재고부족 위험이 더 낮음</strong>{short_gap:.2f}%p 차이<small>상대 감소율과 시나리오당 {event_gap:.1f}회 차이를 함께 봐야 의미를 판단할 수 있음</small></div>
            <div class="{tone(ao_side)}"><strong>{ao_side} 운용가용도가 더 높음</strong>{ao_gap:.2f}%p 차이<small>10,000회 평균 운용가용도 비교임</small></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        """,
        unsafe_allow_html=True,
    )

def _xai_category_map(df_input: pd.DataFrame | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    if not isinstance(df_input, pd.DataFrame) or df_input.empty:
        return {}
    cols = {str(c).strip().upper(): c for c in df_input.columns}
    part_col = cols.get("PART_ID") or cols.get("PART ID")
    category_col = cols.get("CATEGORY")
    if part_col is None or category_col is None:
        return {}
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for pid, cat in zip(df_input[part_col], df_input[category_col]):
        key = str(pid).strip()
        name = str(cat).strip()
        if key and key.lower() not in {"nan","none","<na>"} and name and name.lower() not in {"nan","none","<na>"}:
            out.setdefault(key, name)
    return out


def _xai_prepare_item_df(items: list, df_input: pd.DataFrame | None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    idf = pd.DataFrame(items).copy() if items else pd.DataFrame()
    if idf.empty:
        return idf
    cmap = _xai_category_map(df_input)
    if "part_id" not in idf.columns:
        idf["part_id"] = [str(i+1) for i in range(len(idf))]
    if "item_name" not in idf.columns:
        idf["item_name"] = ""
    if "category" not in idf.columns:
        idf["category"] = ""
    def display_name(row):
        pid = str(row.get("part_id", "")).strip()
        mapped = cmap.get(pid, "")
        if mapped:
            return mapped
        for k in ("category", "item_name"):
            val = str(row.get(k, "")).strip()
            if val and val.lower() not in {"nan","none","<na>"}:
                return val
        return pid or "미상 품목"
    idf["display_name"] = idf.apply(display_name, axis=1)
    for col in ["stock_diff","risk_diff_events","unit_cost","demand_events","A_qty","B_qty","A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events","lead_time_h"]:
        if col not in idf.columns:
            idf[col] = 0.0
        idf[col] = pd.to_numeric(idf[col], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
    return idf


def _xai_item_explanation(row: pd.Series) -> str:
    sd = float(row.get("stock_diff", 0.0)); rd = float(row.get("risk_diff_events", 0.0))
    if sd > 0 and rd > 0:
        return "A가 더 확보했고 실제 시뮬레이션에서도 A의 부족을 줄이는 데 기여"
    if sd < 0 and rd < 0:
        return "B가 더 확보했고 실제 시뮬레이션에서도 B의 부족을 줄이는 데 기여"
    if abs(sd) > 0 and abs(rd) < 1:
        return "재고 수량 차이는 있으나 부족 감소 효과는 제한적"
    if abs(sd) < 1e-9 and abs(rd) > 0:
        return "재고 수량은 같지만 미래 수요 발생 과정에서 부족 차이가 나타남"
    if sd > 0 and rd < 0:
        return "A가 더 확보했지만 이번 시뮬레이션에서는 B의 부족이 더 적었던 품목"
    if sd < 0 and rd > 0:
        return "B가 더 확보했지만 이번 시뮬레이션에서는 A의 부족이 더 적었던 품목"
    return "A/B 간 재고 및 부족 차이가 제한적인 품목"



def _xai_jarvis_stage_payload(sdf: pd.DataFrame, A: dict, B: dict) -> list[dict]:
    if sdf.empty:
        return []
    work = sdf.copy()
    for c in ["total_failures", "A_Ao", "B_Ao", "A_short", "B_short", "A_DT", "B_DT", "A_short_rate", "B_short_rate"]:
        if c not in work.columns:
            work[c] = 0.0
        work[c] = pd.to_numeric(work[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)

    # 내부 계산에는 분위수를 사용하지만 화면에는 P97.5 같은 통계용어를 노출하지 않음.
    # 일반구간도 촘촘히 재생하고, 불리한 꼬리로 갈수록 더 세밀하게 탐색함.
    anchors = list(np.arange(0.05, 0.905, 0.025)) + [
        0.915, 0.925, 0.935, 0.945, 0.955, 0.965, 0.9725, 0.980,
        0.985, 0.990, 0.993, 0.995, 0.997, 0.999,
    ]
    anchors = sorted(set(float(min(max(q, 0.001), 0.999)) for q in anchors))
    n = len(work)
    median_fail = float(work["total_failures"].median())
    median_fail = median_fail if median_fail > 1e-9 else max(float(work["total_failures"].mean()), 1.0)

    def _friendly_name(q: float) -> str:
        if q < 0.30: return "여유로운 미래"
        if q < 0.60: return "보통의 미래"
        if q < 0.78: return "조금 불리한 미래"
        if q < 0.90: return "불리한 미래"
        if q < 0.97: return "매우 불리한 미래"
        return "가장 불리한 미래"
    out = []
    n = len(work)
    for idx, q in enumerate(anchors):
        target = float(work["total_failures"].quantile(q))
        # 각 기준점 주변의 실제 시나리오를 국소 평균하여 단일 표본 노이즈를 줄임.
        local_n = max(24, min(220, int(round(n * (0.030 if q < 0.80 else (0.020 if q < 0.95 else 0.012))))))
        nearest = (work["total_failures"] - target).abs().nsmallest(local_n).index
        part = work.loc[nearest].copy()
        a_ao = float(part["A_Ao"].mean()) * 100.0
        b_ao = float(part["B_Ao"].mean()) * 100.0
        a_short = float(part["A_short"].mean())
        b_short = float(part["B_short"].mean())
        a_rate = float(part["A_short_rate"].mean()) * 100.0
        b_rate = float(part["B_short_rate"].mean()) * 100.0
        a_dt = float(part["A_DT"].mean())
        b_dt = float(part["B_DT"].mean())
        failures = float(part["total_failures"].mean())
        shock_pct = (failures / median_fail - 1.0) * 100.0
        delta = b_ao - a_ao
        better = "B" if delta > 0.02 else ("A" if delta < -0.02 else "A/B")
        name = _friendly_name(q)
        if q <= 0.50:
            headline = f"{name}에서는 A와 B의 차이가 {'크지 않음' if abs(delta) < 0.5 else '확인됨'}."
        elif q < 0.90:
            headline = f"{name}에서 A/B가 버티는 차이가 점차 드러남."
        elif abs(delta) < 0.35:
            headline = f"{name}에서도 A/B의 Ao 차이는 제한적임."
        else:
            headline = f"{name}에서 {better}가 더 안정적으로 버팀."
        if abs(delta) < 0.05:
            detail = "A/B 평균 Ao 차이가 거의 없으며, 미래수요 증가에 따라 부족과 대기시간이 함께 변하는 구간임."
        else:
            short_better = "A" if a_short < b_short else "B"
            detail = (f"같은 미래수요 조건에서 {better}의 Ao가 {abs(delta):.2f}%p 높음. "
                      f"부족 발생은 {short_better}가 시나리오당 평균 {abs(a_short-b_short):.1f}회 적고, "
                      f"대기시간 차이는 {abs(a_dt-b_dt):,.0f}시간 수준임.")
        out.append({"idx": idx, "q": q, "percentile": q * 100.0, "name": name,
                    "count": int(len(part)), "failures": failures, "shockPct": shock_pct, "aAo": a_ao, "bAo": b_ao,
                    "deltaAo": delta, "aShort": a_short, "bShort": b_short,
                    "aShortRate": a_rate, "bShortRate": b_rate, "aDt": a_dt, "bDt": b_dt,
                    "headline": headline, "detail": detail})
    max_fail = max([x["failures"] for x in out] + [1.0])
    max_short = max([max(x["aShort"], x["bShort"]) for x in out] + [1.0])
    max_dt = max([max(x["aDt"], x["bDt"]) for x in out] + [1.0])
    max_loss = max([max(100-x["aAo"], 100-x["bAo"]) for x in out] + [1.0])
    max_rate = max([max(x["aShortRate"], x["bShortRate"]) for x in out] + [1.0])
    for x in out:
        common = min(100.0, 100*x["failures"]/max_fail)
        x["radarA"] = [common, min(100,100*x["aShort"]/max_short), min(100,100*x["aDt"]/max_dt), min(100,100*(100-x["aAo"])/max_loss), min(100,100*x["aShortRate"]/max_rate)]
        x["radarB"] = [common, min(100,100*x["bShort"]/max_short), min(100,100*x["bDt"]/max_dt), min(100,100*(100-x["bAo"])/max_loss), min(100,100*x["bShortRate"]/max_rate)]
    return out

def _xai_render_jarvis_console(A: dict, B: dict, scenario: list[dict] | None, idf: pd.DataFrame, whatif_result: dict | None = None, whatif_history: list[dict] | None = None) -> None:
    sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario) if scenario else pd.DataFrame()
    if sdf.empty:
        st.info("완료된 A/B Monte Carlo 시뮬레이션 결과가 있어야 Explainable AI 콘솔을 표시할 수 있습니다.")
        return
    alias = {"A_short":"A_shortage_events","B_short":"B_shortage_events","A_short_rate":"A_shortage_rate","B_short_rate":"B_shortage_rate","A_DT":"A_DT_wait_h","B_DT":"B_DT_wait_h"}
    for dst, src in alias.items():
        if dst not in sdf.columns and src in sdf.columns:
            sdf[dst] = sdf[src]
    for c in ["n","A_Ao","B_Ao","A_short","B_short","A_short_rate","B_short_rate","A_DT","B_DT","total_failures"]:
        if c not in sdf.columns:
            sdf[c] = 0.0
        sdf[c] = pd.to_numeric(sdf[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
    if float(sdf["A_Ao"].max()) <= 1.5: sdf["A_Ao"] *= 100.0
    if float(sdf["B_Ao"].max()) <= 1.5: sdf["B_Ao"] *= 100.0
    if (sdf["n"] <= 0).all(): sdf["n"] = np.arange(1, len(sdf) + 1)
    base_fail = max(float(sdf["total_failures"].median()), 1e-9)
    sdf["shock_pct"] = (sdf["total_failures"] / base_fail - 1.0) * 100.0

    def _pack(r, x):
        return {"x":float(x),"shockPct":float(r["shock_pct"]),"n":int(round(float(r["n"]))),"aAo":float(r["A_Ao"]),"bAo":float(r["B_Ao"]),"deltaAo":float(r["B_Ao"]-r["A_Ao"]),"aDt":float(r["A_DT"]),"bDt":float(r["B_DT"]),"aShort":float(r["A_short"]),"bShort":float(r["B_short"]),"aShortRate":float(r["A_short_rate"]),"bShortRate":float(r["B_short_rate"])}

    # 고장수요 증가율 모드: 501개의 촘촘한 구간으로 요약
    rs = sdf.sort_values(["total_failures","n"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    rcount = min(501, max(80, len(rs)))
    risk_points=[]
    for ch in np.array_split(rs, rcount):
        if ch.empty: continue
        m=ch[["n","A_Ao","B_Ao","A_short","B_short","A_short_rate","B_short_rate","A_DT","B_DT","total_failures","shock_pct"]].median(numeric_only=True)
        p=_pack(m,float(m["shock_pct"])); s=p["shockPct"]
        p["name"] = "여유로운 미래" if s<=-10 else ("보통의 미래" if s<=10 else ("조금 불리한 미래" if s<=20 else ("불리한 미래" if s<=35 else ("매우 불리한 미래" if s<=55 else "가장 불리한 미래"))))
        risk_points.append(p)

    # 위험 낮음 → 높음 모드: 같은 미래를 위험 위치 0~100으로 일렬 정렬
    gap_points=[]
    _gap_den=max(len(risk_points)-1,1)
    for _i,_p in enumerate(risk_points):
        _q=dict(_p)
        _q["x"]=100.0*_i/_gap_den
        _q["riskLevel"]=_q["x"]
        _q["name"] = "낮은 위험" if _q["x"]<20 else ("보통 위험" if _q["x"]<50 else ("주의 구간" if _q["x"]<75 else ("높은 위험" if _q["x"]<92 else "매우 높은 위험")))
        gap_points.append(_q)

    # 시뮬레이션 순번 모드: 실제 순서를 보존한 최대 1401개 시나리오
    run_sorted=sdf.sort_values("n").reset_index(drop=True)
    take=np.unique(np.linspace(0,len(run_sorted)-1,min(1401,len(run_sorted))).astype(int))
    run_points=[dict(_pack(r,float(r["n"])),name="서로 다른 미래 시나리오") for _,r in run_sorted.iloc[take].iterrows()]

    # What-if가 완료되면 동일 10,000개 미래의 보강안 결과를 세 번째 정책선으로 연결함.
    wf_risk_points=[]; wf_gap_points=[]; wf_run_points=[]; wf_meta={}
    if isinstance(whatif_result, dict) and isinstance(whatif_result.get("scenario_df"), pd.DataFrame):
        wdf=whatif_result.get("scenario_df").copy()
        if not wdf.empty and "simulation_no" in wdf.columns and "After_Ao" in wdf.columns:
            wdf["simulation_no"]=pd.to_numeric(wdf["simulation_no"],errors="coerce")
            wdf["After_Ao"]=pd.to_numeric(wdf["After_Ao"],errors="coerce")
            wdf=wdf.dropna(subset=["simulation_no","After_Ao"]).copy()
            if not wdf.empty:
                if float(wdf["After_Ao"].max()) <= 1.5: wdf["After_Ao"]*=100.0
                wdf["simulation_no"]=wdf["simulation_no"].astype(int)
                base_cols=sdf[["n","total_failures","shock_pct"]].copy()
                base_cols["n"]=pd.to_numeric(base_cols["n"],errors="coerce").round().astype(int)
                wm=base_cols.merge(wdf[["simulation_no","After_Ao"]],left_on="n",right_on="simulation_no",how="inner")
                if not wm.empty:
                    wm_r=wm.sort_values(["total_failures","n"]).reset_index(drop=True)
                    for ch in np.array_split(wm_r, rcount):
                        if ch.empty: continue
                        wf_risk_points.append({"x":float(ch["shock_pct"].median()),"n":int(round(float(ch["n"].median()))),"wAo":float(ch["After_Ao"].median())})
                    _wf_den=max(len(wf_risk_points)-1,1)
                    for _i,_p in enumerate(wf_risk_points):
                        wf_gap_points.append({"x":100.0*_i/_wf_den,"n":_p["n"],"wAo":_p["wAo"]})
                    wm_run=wm.sort_values("n").reset_index(drop=True)
                    take_w=np.unique(np.linspace(0,len(wm_run)-1,min(1401,len(wm_run))).astype(int))
                    for _,rr in wm_run.iloc[take_w].iterrows():
                        wf_run_points.append({"x":float(rr["n"]),"n":int(rr["n"]),"wAo":float(rr["After_Ao"])})
                    base_label=str(whatif_result.get("base_label","A")).upper().strip()
                    if base_label not in {"A","B"}: base_label="A"
                    wf_meta={
                        "active":True,
                        "base":base_label,
                        "label":f"{base_label}+보강",
                        "item":str(whatif_result.get("item_label","보강 패키지")),
                        "items":int(whatif_result.get("item_count",1) or 1),
                        "units":int(whatif_result.get("total_add_units",whatif_result.get("add_units",0)) or 0),
                        "cost":float(whatif_result.get("additional_cost",0) or 0),
                    }

    def _steep(side):
        x=np.asarray([p["x"] for p in risk_points],float); y=np.asarray([p[f"{side}Ao"] for p in risk_points],float)
        sl=np.gradient(y,x) if len(x)>3 and np.nanmax(x)-np.nanmin(x)>1e-9 else np.gradient(y)
        i=int(np.nanargmin(sl)); return i,float(sl[i])
    ia,sa=_steep("a"); ib,sb=_steep("b"); ca=float(risk_points[ia]["shockPct"]); cb=float(risk_points[ib]["shockPct"])
    steep_txt = "특정 한 지점에서 갑자기 무너지는 모습은 크지 않습니다." if abs(sa)<.002 and abs(sb)<.002 else f"A는 고장수요가 평소보다 약 {ca:+.0f}% 많아지는 부근, B는 약 {cb:+.0f}% 부근에서 Ao가 가장 빠르게 낮아집니다."
    _gap_arr=np.asarray([abs(p["deltaAo"]) for p in gap_points],float)
    if len(_gap_arr):
        _imax=int(np.nanargmax(_gap_arr)); _imin=int(np.nanargmin(_gap_arr))
        gap_txt=f"A/B 차이는 위험 수준 약 {gap_points[_imax]['x']:.0f}% 부근에서 가장 크게 벌어지고, 약 {gap_points[_imin]['x']:.0f}% 부근에서 가장 비슷하게 움직입니다."
    else:
        gap_txt="A/B 간격을 계산할 미래 시나리오가 충분하지 않습니다."

    top_items=[]
    if isinstance(idf,pd.DataFrame) and not idf.empty:
        temp=idf.copy(); temp["impact"]=pd.to_numeric(temp.get("risk_diff_events",0),errors="coerce").fillna(0).abs(); temp["demand"]=pd.to_numeric(temp.get("demand_events",0),errors="coerce").fillna(0); temp["score"]=temp["impact"]+.02*temp["demand"]
        for _,r in temp.sort_values(["score","impact"],ascending=False).head(5).iterrows():
            sd=float(r.get("stock_diff",0)); rd=float(r.get("risk_diff_events",0)); top_items.append({"name":str(r.get("display_name","수리부속")),"impact":float(r.get("impact",0)),"more":"A" if sd>0 else ("B" if sd<0 else "동일"),"qty":abs(int(round(sd))),"lower":"A" if rd>0 else ("B" if rd<0 else "유사"),"shortDiff":abs(int(round(rd))),"leadDays":float(r.get("lead_time_h",0))/24.0})
    if not top_items: top_items=[{"name":"품목 상세 미연결","impact":1,"more":"동일","qty":0,"lower":"유사","shortDiff":0,"leadDays":0}]

    # ② AI 보강 탐색 모드
    # 목적: What-if를 실행하기 전에 "어느 비용 구간의 어떤 품목을 먼저 검증할지"를 안내하고,
    # 실행 후에는 실제 10,000회 재시뮬레이션 결과를 같은 화면에 누적한다.
    # X = 현재 선택한 기준대안에서 추가로 투자하는 비용(억원)
    # Y = 기준대안 대비 실제 평균 부족발생 감소율(%). 아직 검증하지 않은 품목은 하단 후보 레인에 비용 위치만 표시한다.
    cost_a=float(_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost"))); cost_b=float(_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost")))
    risk_a=float(pd.to_numeric(sdf["A_short"],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).mean())
    risk_b=float(pd.to_numeric(sdf["B_short"],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).mean())
    scan_base=str(st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_base_btn","A") or "A").upper().strip()
    if scan_base not in {"A","B"}: scan_base="A"
    scan_other="B" if scan_base=="A" else "A"
    base_cost=cost_a if scan_base=="A" else cost_b
    other_cost=cost_b if scan_base=="A" else cost_a
    base_risk=risk_a if scan_base=="A" else risk_b
    other_risk=risk_b if scan_base=="A" else risk_a
    base_risk_den=max(abs(base_risk),1e-12)
    target_add=(other_cost-base_cost)/1e8
    target_improve=(base_risk-other_risk)/base_risk_den*100.0

    def _risk_improve(risk_value: float) -> float:
        return (base_risk-float(risk_value))/base_risk_den*100.0

    def _gap_recovery(improve_value: float) -> float:
        if target_improve <= 1e-12:
            return 0.0
        return 100.0*float(improve_value)/target_improve

    cost_points=[
        {"label":scan_base,"kind":"BASE","base":scan_base,"addCost":0.0,"totalCost":base_cost/1e8,"risk":base_risk,"improve":0.0,"recovery":0.0,"extra":0.0},
        {"label":scan_other,"kind":"OTHER","base":scan_base,"addCost":target_add,"totalCost":other_cost/1e8,"risk":other_risk,"improve":target_improve,"recovery":100.0 if target_improve>1e-12 else 0.0,"extra":other_cost-base_cost},
    ]
    hist = whatif_history if isinstance(whatif_history,list) else []
    for h in hist[-12:]:
        if not isinstance(h,dict): continue
        try:
            base_label=str(h.get("base_label","A")).upper()
            if base_label != scan_base: continue
            total_cost=float(h.get("total_cost",0.0)); after_risk=float(h.get("after_shortage",0.0))
            improve=_risk_improve(after_risk)
            cost_points.append({
                "label":str(h.get("short_label",h.get("item_label",base_label+"+보강"))),"kind":"WF","base":base_label,
                "addCost":(total_cost-base_cost)/1e8,"totalCost":total_cost/1e8,"risk":after_risk,"improve":improve,
                "extra":float(h.get("additional_cost",0.0)),"recovery":_gap_recovery(improve),
                "items":int(h.get("item_count",0) or 0),"units":int(h.get("total_add_units",0) or 0)
            })
        except Exception:
            pass
    if isinstance(whatif_result,dict) and isinstance(whatif_result.get("after"),dict):
        try:
            base_label=str(whatif_result.get("base_label","A")).upper()
            if base_label == scan_base:
                total_cost=float(whatif_result.get("reinforced_stock_cost",0.0) or 0.0)
                if total_cost<=0: total_cost=base_cost+float(whatif_result.get("additional_cost",0.0) or 0.0)
                after_risk=float(whatif_result.get("after",{}).get("mean_shortage_events",0.0) or 0.0)
                improve=_risk_improve(after_risk)
                key=(round(total_cost,2),round(after_risk,8),base_label)
                exists=any((round(float(p.get("totalCost",0))*1e8,2),round(float(p.get("risk",0)),8),str(p.get("base","")))==key for p in cost_points if p.get("kind")=="WF")
                if not exists:
                    cost_points.append({"label":f"{base_label}+보강","kind":"WF","base":base_label,"addCost":(total_cost-base_cost)/1e8,"totalCost":total_cost/1e8,"risk":after_risk,"improve":improve,"extra":float(whatif_result.get("additional_cost",0.0) or 0.0),"recovery":_gap_recovery(improve),"items":int(whatif_result.get("item_count",0) or 0),"units":int(whatif_result.get("total_add_units",0) or 0)})
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 미실행 후보도 A/B에서 이미 관측된 재고차이↔부족차이를 이용해 '예상 위험감소'를 계산한다.
    # 이는 What-if 10,000회 재시뮬레이션 전 우선순위용 추정치이며, 실제 효과로 단정하지 않는다.
    candidate_points=[]
    if isinstance(idf,pd.DataFrame) and not idf.empty:
        ctmp=idf.copy()
        ctmp["unit_cost"]=pd.to_numeric(ctmp.get("unit_cost",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        ctmp["impact"]=pd.to_numeric(ctmp.get("risk_diff_events",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).abs()
        ctmp["demand"]=pd.to_numeric(ctmp.get("demand_events",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        ctmp["stock_gap"]=pd.to_numeric(ctmp.get("stock_diff",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).abs()
        ctmp["score"]=ctmp["impact"]+0.02*ctmp["demand"]
        for _,r in ctmp[(ctmp["unit_cost"]>0)].sort_values(["score","impact"],ascending=False).head(10).iterrows():
            impact=float(r.get("impact",0.0)); stock_gap=float(r.get("stock_gap",0.0)); demand=float(r.get("demand",0.0))
            # A/B 사이의 실제 부족차이를 재고차이 1개당 한계효과로 환산. 재고차이가 0이면 수요증거 기반의 보수적 추정.
            if stock_gap >= 0.5:
                pred_events=impact/max(stock_gap,1.0)
                confidence="높음" if stock_gap>=1.0 and impact>=5 else "보통"
                basis="A/B 실제 재고차이와 부족차이 기반"
            else:
                pred_events=min(impact, max(demand*0.08,0.0))
                confidence="낮음"
                basis="직접 재고차이가 없어 수요·부족 증거로 보수 추정"
            pred_improve=max(0.0, min(100.0, pred_events/base_risk_den*100.0))
            candidate_points.append({
                "label":str(r.get("display_name","수리부속")),
                "addCost":float(r.get("unit_cost",0.0))/1e8,
                "candidateIndex":int(r.get("candidate_index",-1)),
                "impact":impact,"predEvents":pred_events,"predImprove":pred_improve,
                "confidence":confidence,"basis":basis
            })

    # 실제 검증된 보강안 중 비용이 늘어날수록 지금까지 가장 큰 위험감소를 만든 경로만 연결한다.
    efficient=[]; best_seen=-1e18
    actual_for_frontier=[p for p in cost_points if p.get("kind") in {"BASE","WF","OTHER"}]
    for p in sorted(actual_for_frontier,key=lambda z:(float(z.get("addCost",0)), -float(z.get("improve",0)))):
        if float(p.get("improve",0)) > best_seen + 1e-9:
            efficient.append(p); best_seen=float(p.get("improve",0))

    _wf_cost_pts=[p for p in cost_points if p.get("kind")=="WF"]
    if _wf_cost_pts:
        _cw=_wf_cost_pts[-1]
        if target_improve>0:
            gap_txt=f"현재 {scan_base}+보강안은 추가 {_cw.get('addCost',0):.2f}억원으로 상대대안 {scan_other}가 만드는 부족위험 개선효과의 약 {float(_cw.get('recovery',0)):.0f}%를 회복했습니다."
        else:
            gap_txt=f"현재 {scan_base}+보강안은 추가 {_cw.get('addCost',0):.2f}억원을 사용했습니다. 상대대안보다 기준 위험이 이미 낮거나 유사해 회복률 대신 실제 부족감소율을 확인하세요."
    else:
        gap_txt=f"상단 ② AI 보강 탐색은 {scan_base}에서 시작해 품목별 1개 보강비용을 스캔합니다. 회색 후보는 A/B에서 이미 관측된 재고·부족 차이로 예상효과를 먼저 보여주며, 아래 What-if 10,000회 실행 후 실제 검증점으로 바뀝니다."

    scan_values=[0.0,target_add]+[float(p.get("addCost",0)) for p in cost_points if p.get("kind")=="WF"]+[float(p.get("addCost",0)) for p in candidate_points]
    scan_min=min(scan_values+[0.0]); scan_max=max(scan_values+[0.0])
    scan_pad=max((scan_max-scan_min)*0.14,0.08)
    scan_meta={"base":scan_base,"other":scan_other,"baseCost":base_cost/1e8,"otherCost":other_cost/1e8,"targetAdd":target_add,"targetImprove":target_improve,"scanMin":scan_min-scan_pad,"scanMax":scan_max+scan_pad}

    # ① 고장수요 증가율의 배경구간은 고정 목표 Ao가 아니라, 이번 시뮬레이션에서 관측된 평균 Ao 하락폭을 상대적으로 나눔.
    _avg_ao=np.asarray([(p["aAo"]+p["bAo"])/2.0 for p in risk_points],float)
    _best=float(np.nanmax(_avg_ao)) if len(_avg_ao) else 0.0
    _worst=float(np.nanmin(_avg_ao)) if len(_avg_ao) else 0.0
    _span=max(_best-_worst,1e-9)
    _loss_frac=(_best-_avg_ao)/_span
    def _first_x_at(frac, fallback):
        idx=np.where(_loss_frac>=frac)[0]
        return float(risk_points[int(idx[0])]["x"]) if len(idx) else float(fallback)
    _risk_xmin=float(min(p["x"] for p in risk_points)); _risk_xmax=float(max(p["x"] for p in risk_points))
    _x_stable=_first_x_at(.25,_risk_xmax); _x_warn=_first_x_at(.60,_risk_xmax)
    risk_band_meta={"xmin":_risk_xmin,"xmax":_risk_xmax,"stableEnd":_x_stable,"warnEnd":_x_warn}

    rp=risk_points; xa=[p["x"] for p in rp]; ya=[p["aAo"] for p in rp]; yb=[p["bAo"] for p in rp]; first=rp[0]
    yw=[p["wAo"] for p in wf_risk_points] if wf_risk_points else []
    all_y=ya+yb+yw
    ymin=min(all_y); ymax=max(all_y); pad=max((ymax-ymin)*.10,.25)
    fig=go.Figure()
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=xa,y=ya,mode="lines",name="A",meta={"xaiRole":"lineA"},visible=True,line=dict(color="#73A9D8",width=3.5,shape="spline",smoothing=.6),opacity=(.30 if (wf_risk_points and wf_meta.get("base")=="A") else .94)))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=xa,y=yb,mode="lines",name="B",meta={"xaiRole":"lineB"},visible=True,line=dict(color="#D89A77",width=3.5,shape="spline",smoothing=.6),opacity=(.30 if (wf_risk_points and wf_meta.get("base")=="B") else .94)))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[first["x"],first["x"]],y=[ymin-pad,ymax+pad],mode="lines",line=dict(color="#F3D38A",width=2,dash="dot"),meta={"xaiRole":"cursor"},showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[first["x"]],y=[first["aAo"]],mode="markers",marker=dict(size=10,color="#55A4FF",line=dict(width=2,color="white")),meta={"xaiRole":"markerA"},showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[first["x"]],y=[first["bAo"]],mode="markers",marker=dict(size=10,color="#FF955C",line=dict(width=2,color="white")),meta={"xaiRole":"markerB"},showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[first["x"],first["x"]],y=[first["aAo"],first["bAo"]],mode="lines",line=dict(color="#F3D38A",width=5),meta={"xaiRole":"deltaAB"},showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip"))
    if wf_risk_points:
        wf0=wf_risk_points[0]; base0=first["aAo"] if wf_meta.get("base")=="A" else first["bAo"]
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[p["x"] for p in wf_risk_points],y=[p["wAo"] for p in wf_risk_points],mode="lines",name=wf_meta.get("label","보강안"),meta={"xaiRole":"lineWF"},visible=True,line=dict(color="#006CFF",width=5,dash="solid",shape="spline",smoothing=.7),opacity=1.0))
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[wf0["x"]],y=[wf0["wAo"]],mode="markers",marker=dict(size=15,color="#006CFF",symbol="diamond",line=dict(width=2,color="white")),meta={"xaiRole":"markerWF"},visible=True,showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip"))
