        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[wf0["x"],wf0["x"]],y=[base0,wf0["wAo"]],mode="lines",line=dict(color="#006CFF",width=4,dash="solid"),meta={"xaiRole":"deltaWF"},visible=True,showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip"))
    else:
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[],y=[],mode="lines",name="보강안",meta={"xaiRole":"lineWF"},visible=False,line=dict(color="#006CFF",width=4,dash="solid")))
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[],y=[],mode="markers",meta={"xaiRole":"markerWF"},visible=False,showlegend=False))
        fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[],y=[],mode="lines",meta={"xaiRole":"deltaWF"},visible=False,showlegend=False))
    # ② AI 보강 탐색 전용 traces (초기에는 숨김)
    _cbase=[p for p in cost_points if p.get("kind")=="BASE"][0]
    _cother=[p for p in cost_points if p.get("kind")=="OTHER"][0]
    _cw=[p for p in cost_points if p.get("kind")=="WF"]
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[_cbase["addCost"]],y=[_cbase["improve"]],mode="markers+text",text=[_cbase["label"]],textposition="top center",name="기준대안",meta={"xaiRole":"costA"},visible=False,marker=dict(size=16,color="#73A9D8",line=dict(width=2,color="#DCE6EC"))))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[_cother["addCost"]],y=[_cother["improve"]],mode="markers+text",text=[_cother["label"]],textposition="top center",name="상대대안",meta={"xaiRole":"costB"},visible=False,marker=dict(size=16,color="#D89A77",line=dict(width=2,color="#DCE6EC"))))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[p["addCost"] for p in _cw],y=[p["improve"] for p in _cw],mode="markers+text",text=[p["label"] for p in _cw],textposition="top center",name="실제 검증 보강안",meta={"xaiRole":"costWF"},visible=False,marker=dict(size=14,color="#006CFF",symbol="diamond",line=dict(width=2,color="#BFD8FF"))))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[p["addCost"] for p in efficient],y=[p["improve"] for p in efficient],mode="lines",name="검증된 효율 경로",meta={"xaiRole":"costFrontier"},visible=False,line=dict(color="#4F88B8",width=2.5,dash="dot",shape="spline",smoothing=.5)))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
        x=[p["addCost"] for p in candidate_points],
        y=[p["predImprove"] for p in candidate_points],
        mode="markers+text",text=[p["label"] for p in candidate_points],textposition="bottom center",
        customdata=[[p["predImprove"],p["confidence"],p["basis"]] for p in candidate_points],
        name="AI 예상 보강후보",meta={"xaiRole":"costCandidate"},visible=False,
        marker=dict(size=12,color="#6D8290",symbol="triangle-up",line=dict(width=1.5,color="#AFC0CA")),
        hovertemplate="%{text}<br>1개 보강비용 %{x:.2f}억원<br>예상 부족위험 감소 %{customdata[0]:.1f}%<br>예상 신뢰도 %{customdata[1]}<br>%{customdata[2]}<extra></extra>"))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[],y=[],mode="markers",meta={"xaiRole":"costFocus"},visible=False,showlegend=False,marker=dict(size=24,color="#006CFF",symbol="circle-open",line=dict(width=4,color="#79B7FF"))))
    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[0,0],y=[-10,110],mode="lines",meta={"xaiRole":"costScan"},visible=False,showlegend=False,hoverinfo="skip",line=dict(color="#7CD8FF",width=2,dash="dot")))
    fig.update_layout(height=500,margin=dict(l=55,r=20,t=12,b=55),paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",plot_bgcolor="#0E1720",hovermode="x unified",xaxis=dict(title="평소 대비 미래 고장수요 변화 (%)",gridcolor="#263645",color="#C6D1D9",zeroline=True,zerolinecolor="#425766"),yaxis=dict(title="운용가용도 Ao (%)",gridcolor="#263645",color="#C6D1D9",range=[ymin-pad,ymax+pad],zeroline=False),legend=dict(orientation="h",y=1.08,x=.01,font=dict(color="#DCE6EC")),font=dict(family="Malgun Gothic",color="#DCE6EC"))
    plot_html=pio.to_html(fig,full_html=False,include_plotlyjs=True,div_id="xai_jarvis_dual",config={"displaylogo":False,"scrollZoom":True,"responsive":True})

    cost_a=float(_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost"))); cost_b=float(_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost"))); higher="A" if cost_a>cost_b else ("B" if cost_b>cost_a else "A/B"); gap=abs(cost_a-cost_b); cost_txt=f"{higher}가 {_xai_compact_money(gap)} 더 투자" if higher!="A/B" else "A/B 투자비용이 유사"
    positions=[(50,50),(20,24),(80,24),(20,78),(80,78)]; mx=max([float(x.get("impact",0)) for x in top_items]+[1]); nodes=[]
    for i,it in enumerate(top_items[:5]):
        x,y=positions[i]; rr=48+32*float(it.get("impact",0))/mx; nodes.append(f'<button type="button" class="jnode" data-i="{i}" style="left:{x}%;top:{y}%;width:{rr}px;height:{rr}px"><span>{html.escape(str(it.get("name","품목")))}</span></button>')
    risk_json=json.dumps(risk_points,ensure_ascii=False); gap_json=json.dumps(gap_points,ensure_ascii=False); run_json=json.dumps(run_points,ensure_ascii=False); cost_json=json.dumps(cost_points,ensure_ascii=False); candidate_json=json.dumps(candidate_points,ensure_ascii=False); scan_meta_json=json.dumps(scan_meta,ensure_ascii=False); risk_meta_json=json.dumps(risk_band_meta,ensure_ascii=False); item_json=json.dumps(top_items,ensure_ascii=False); wf_risk_json=json.dumps(wf_risk_points,ensure_ascii=False); wf_gap_json=json.dumps(wf_gap_points,ensure_ascii=False); wf_run_json=json.dumps(wf_run_points,ensure_ascii=False); wf_meta_json=json.dumps(wf_meta,ensure_ascii=False)

    html_template=r'''<div id="xai-jarvis-root"><style>
#xai-jarvis-root{font-family:'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;color:#DCE6EC;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#071018,#0B1822 58%,#0A121A);border:1px solid #20313D;border-radius:20px;padding:15px;box-sizing:border-box;overflow:visible}#xai-jarvis-root *{box-sizing:border-box}#xai-jarvis-root .muted{color:#8799A7}#xai-jarvis-root .head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:10px}#xai-jarvis-root .title{font-size:24px;font-weight:800}#xai-jarvis-root .badge{border:1px solid #2D4656;background:#0E202C;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 11px;font-size:12px;color:#B9C9D3}#xai-jarvis-root .grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.82fr) minmax(280px,.72fr);gap:12px}#xai-jarvis-root .panel{background:rgba(13,26,36,.86);border:1px solid #203543;border-radius:16px;padding:12px;min-width:0}#xai-jarvis-root .modebar{display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:9px 0 7px}#xai-jarvis-root button.ctrl{background:#112733;border:1px solid #294556;color:#DCE6EC;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700}#xai-jarvis-root button.ctrl:hover,#xai-jarvis-root button.ctrl.active{border-color:#58A6FF;background:#17384C}#xai-jarvis-root .plotwrap,#xai-jarvis-root 추가 투자 (억원)','yaxis.title.text':'AI 예상 / 실제 부족위험 감소 (%)','xaxis.zeroline':true,'xaxis.zerolinecolor':'#425766','xaxis.range':[scanMeta.scanMin,scanMeta.scanMax],'yaxis.range':[ymin,ymax],'hovermode':'closest','shapes':[{type:'line',x0:scanMeta.targetAdd,x1:scanMeta.targetAdd,y0:ymin,y1:ymax,line:{color:'#D89A77',width:1.6,dash:'dot'}}]});}}root.querySelector('#xj-chart-desc').textContent=m==='risk'?'고장수요가 많아질수록 A/B와 보강안의 Ao가 어떻게 달라지는지 보여줍니다.':(m==='cost'?'추가 투자비용과 AI 예상 위험감소를 함께 스캔합니다. What-if를 실행하면 예상점이 실제 10,000회 검증점으로 바뀌어 누적됩니다.':'1회부터 10,000회까지 서로 다른 미래를 넘겨보며 A/B와 보강안의 반응 차이를 보여줍니다.');root.querySelector('#xj-what').textContent=m==='risk'?'고장수요 증가 → A/B Ao → 보강효과가 한 흐름으로 stop(){playing=false;if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=null;play.textContent='▶ 재생'}slider.addEventListener('input',e=>{stop();update(+e.target.value)});root.querySelector('#xj-riskmode').onclick=()=>setMode('risk');root.querySelector('#xj-gapmode').onclick=()=>setMode('cost');root.querySelector('#xj-runmode').onclick=()=>setMode('run');play.onclick=()=>{if(playing){stop();return}playing=true;play.textContent='Ⅱ 정지';const startPos=+slider.value>=9990?0:+slider.value,duration=mode==='risk'?42000:(mode==='cost'?22000:44000),start=performance.now();function frame(now){if(!playing)return;let e=Math.min(1,(now-start)/duration);e=e*e*(3-2*e);const pos=startPos+(10000-startPos)*e;update(pos);if(e<1)raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);else{playing=false;raf=null;play.textContent='▶ 다시 더 확보';const rr=it.lower==='유사'?'부족발생 차이 제한적':it.lower+'의 누적 부족이 '+it.shortDiff.toLocaleString()+'회 적음';root.querySelector('#xj-item-detail').textContent=it.name+' · '+stock+' · '+rr+' · 리드타임 '+it.leadDays.toFixed(1)+'일';};ev.appendChild(b)});root.querySelectorAll('.jnode').forEach((n,i)=>n.onclick=()=>{const it=items[i];if(!it)return;root.querySelectorAll('.jnode').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));n.classList.add('active');const stock=it.more==='동일'?'A/B 재고수량 동일':it.more+'가 '+it.qty+'개 더 확보';const '+it.leadDays.toFixed(1)+'일';});setMode('risk')})();</script></div>'''
    html_block=(html_template.replace('__COST__',html.escape(cost_txt)).replace('__STEEP__',html.escape(steep_txt)).replace('__GAPTXT__',html.escape(gap_txt)).replace('__NODES__',''.join(nodes)).replace('__PLOT__',plot_html).replace('__RISK__',risk_json).replace('__GAP__',gap_json).replace('__RUN__',run_json).replace('__COSTDATA__',cost_json).replace('__CANDIDATES__',candidate_json).replace('__SCANMETA__',scan_meta_json).replace('__RISK_META__',risk_meta_json).replace('__ITEMS__',item_json).replace('__WFRISK__',wf_risk_json).replace('__WFGAP__',wf_gap_json).replace('__WFRUN__',wf_run_json).replace('__WFMETA__',wf_meta_json))

    st.components.v1.html(html_block, height=1350, scrolling=True)
    st.caption("같은 Monte Carlo 결과를 세 방식으로 볼 수 있습니다. ① 고장수요 증가율은 원인→결과, ② AI 보강 탐색은 보강 후보의 비용과 예상 위험감소를 먼저 스캔하고 What-if 실행 후 실제 위험감소 결과를, ③ 시뮬레이션 순번은 10,000개의 서로 다른 미래를 체감하는 모드입니다. 재생은 실제 결과 사이를 시각적으로 보간하며 새로운 통계값을 만들어내지 않습니다.")


def _xai_policy_signature_from_mc(mc: dict | None) -> str:
    """Stable identity for the currently simulated A/B policy pair.

    What-if results are valid only for the exact A/B pair that produced them.
    Tab changes and ordinary rerenders must not clear the result, but selecting
    a new A/B pair and completing Monte Carlo must invalidate the old third line.
    """
    if not isinstance(mc, dict):
        return ""
    a_key = str(mc.get("A_key", "") or "")
    b_key = str(mc.get("B_key", "") or "")
    run_id = str(mc.get("risk_run_id", "") or "")
    if not a_key or not b_key:
        return ""
    return f"{run_id}|{a_key}|{b_key}"


def _xai_reset_whatif_if_policy_changed(mc: dict | None) -> bool:
    """Clear stale A/B What-if states only when the completed A/B pair changes."""
    current_sig = _xai_policy_signature_from_mc(mc)
    if not current_sig:
        return False
    saved_sig = str(st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_policy_sig", "") or "")
    if not saved_sig:
        for _k in ("xai_whatif_result_A", "xai_whatif_result_B", "xai_whatif_result"):
            _wr = st.session_state.get(_k)
            if isinstance(_wr, dict):
                saved_sig = str(_wr.get("_xai_policy_sig", "") or "")
                if saved_sig:
                    break
    if saved_sig and saved_sig != current_sig:
        for _k in [
            "xai_whatif_result", "xai_whatif_result_A", "xai_whatif_result_B",
            "xai_whatif_history", "xai_whatif_history_A", "xai_whatif_history_B",
            "xai_wf_A_items", "xai_wf_B_items",
        ]:
            st.session_state.pop(_k, None)
        st.session_state["xai_whatif_policy_sig"] = current_sig
        for key in list(st.session_state.keys()):
            sk=str(key)
            if sk.startswith("xai_wf_A_qty_") or sk.startswith("xai_wf_B_qty_") or sk.startswith("wf18_"):
                st.session_state.pop(key, None)
        return True
    if not saved_sig:
        st.session_state["xai_whatif_policy_sig"] = current_sig
    return False

def _xai_render_whatif_panel(result: dict, idf: pd.DataFrame, A: dict | None = None, B: dict | None = None) -> None:
    mc = st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result")
    if not isinstance(mc, dict) or not isinstance(idf, pd.DataFrame) or idf.empty:
        return
    a_key = str(mc.get("A_key", "")); b_key = str(mc.get("B_key", ""))
    if not a_key or not b_key:
        return
    A = A if isinstance(A, dict) else {}
    B = B if isinstance(B, dict) else {}
    cost_a = float(_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
    cost_b = float(_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
    work = idf.copy()
    if "candidate_index" not in work.columns:
        return
    work["candidate_index"] = pd.to_numeric(work["candidate_index"], errors="coerce")
    work = work.dropna(subset=["candidate_index"]).copy()
    if work.empty:
        return
    work["candidate_index"] = work["candidate_index"].astype(int)
    work["impact_abs"] = pd.to_numeric(work.get("risk_diff_events", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0).abs()
    work["demand_events"] = pd.to_numeric(work.get("demand_events", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0)
    work["unit_cost"] = pd.to_numeric(work.get("unit_cost", 0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
    work = work.sort_values(["impact_abs", "demand_events"], ascending=False).head(20).reset_index(drop=True)

    st.markdown("""
    <style>
    .xai-wf-head{background:linear-gradient(145deg,#071018,#0B1822 60%,#0A121A);border:1px solid #20313D;border-radius:20px;padding:16px;color:#DCE6EC;margin-top:10px}.xai-wf-head b{font-size:21px}.xai-wf-sub{font-size:12px;color:#8EA1AE;margin-top:4px;line-height:1.6}.xai-wf-ai{margin:10px 0;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0D1E29,#102A39);border:1px solid #294556;border-radius:14px;padding:12px 14px;color:#E8F2F7}.xai-wf-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:9px;margin:10px 0}.xai-wf-cost-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:9px;margin:10px 0}.xai-wf-card{background:#091721;border:1px solid #253B48;border-radius:12px;padding:10px}.xai-wf-card small{display:block;color:#7F929E;font-size:10px}.xai-wf-card strong{display:block;font-size:19px;color:#E7F0F4;margin-top:3px}.xai-wf-card.accent{border-color:#3B725F;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#0A1C1A,#0C2823)}.xai-wf-card.accent strong{color:#91F2CD}.xai-wf-judge{margin:10px 0;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#091721,#0B1B26);border:1px solid #284354;border-radius:13px;padding:12px;color:#D8E4EA;line-height:1.65;font-size:13px}.xai-wf-package{background:#08131C;border:1px solid #243A47;border-radius:16px;padding:12px 14px;margin:8px 0;color:#DCE6EC}.xai-wf-package strong{color:#91F2CD}.xai-wf-chip{display:inline-block;margin:4px 6px 2px 0;padding:5px 9px;border-radius:999px;background:#102632;border:1px solid #315266;color:#CFE4EE;font-size:11px}@media(max-width:800px){.xai-wf-grid,.xai-wf-cost-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}}
    </style>
    <div class="xai-wf-head"><b>WHAT-IF · 보강 패키지를 만들면 같은 미래에서 얼마나 더 버틸까?</b><div class="xai-wf-sub">상단 ② AI 보강 탐색에서 비용과 예상 위험감소를 먼저 확인합니다. 아래에서는 수량을 직접 고르는 대신 AI가 비용효율형·균형형·안정형 보강 패키지를 먼저 구성하고, 필요할 때만 세부 조정한 뒤 동일한 10,000개 미래로 실제 효과를 검증합니다.</div></div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)

    rec=work.iloc[0]; rec_name=str(rec.get("display_name","수리부속")); rec_impact=int(round(abs(float(rec.get("risk_diff_events",0)))))
    st.markdown(f"<div class='xai-wf-ai'><span style='font-size:11px;color:#8FA5B3'>자동 추천 시작점</span><br><b style='font-size:17px'>{html.escape(rec_name)}</b><div style='font-size:12px;color:#B8C7D0;margin-top:4px'>A/B 결과 차이를 크게 만든 품목입니다. 누적 부족 차이 약 {rec_impact:,}회를 먼저 확인하고, 필요하면 다른 취약품목을 함께 묶어 보강 패키지를 만들 수 있습니다.</div></div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    if "xai_whatif_base_btn" not in st.session_state:
        _ra=float(_xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0))); _rb=float(_xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))
        st.session_state.xai_whatif_base_btn="A" if _ra>=_rb else "B"
    st.markdown("**1. 어느 대안을 보강할까요?**")
    c1,c2=st.columns(2)
    with c1:
        if st.button(("✓ " if st.session_state.xai_whatif_base_btn=="A" else "")+"A 대안에서 시작",use_container_width=True,key="xai_wf_A_multi"):
            st.session_state.xai_whatif_base_btn="A"
    with c2:
        if st.button(("✓ " if st.session_state.xai_whatif_base_btn=="B" else "")+"B 대안에서 시작",use_container_width=True,key="xai_wf_B_multi"):
            st.session_state.xai_whatif_base_btn="B"
    base_label=st.session_state.xai_whatif_base_btn

    # 자동 추천 전략 패키지: 단순 수량 조작보다 먼저 AI가 목적별 후보 조합을 제안한다.
    _eff=work.copy()
    _eff["cost_safe"]=_eff["unit_cost"].where(_eff["unit_cost"]>0, np.nan)
    _eff["eff_score"]=_eff["impact_abs"]/_eff["cost_safe"]
    _eff["eff_score"]=_eff["eff_score"].replace([np.inf,-np.inf],np.nan).fillna(0.0)
    _balanced=work.assign(_bal=work["impact_abs"]+0.02*work["demand_events"]).sort_values("_bal",ascending=False)
    _stable=work.sort_values(["impact_abs","demand_events"],ascending=False)
    _efficient=_eff.sort_values(["eff_score","impact_abs"],ascending=False)
    st.markdown("**2. 보강 목적별 자동 추천**")
    st.caption("패키지를 고르면 후보와 권장수량이 자동으로 채워집니다. 아래 세부조정은 필요할 때만 사용하세요.")
    pc1,pc2,pc3=st.columns(3)
    def _apply_pkg(mode):
        if mode=="EFF": src=_efficient.head(min(2,len(_efficient))); qtys=[1]*len(src)
        elif mode=="BAL": src=_balanced.head(min(3,len(_balanced))); qtys=[2 if j==0 else 1 for j in range(len(src))]
        else: src=_stable.head(min(5,len(_stable))); qtys=[2 if j<2 else 1 for j in range(len(src))]
        idxs=[int(x) for x in src.index.tolist()]
        st.session_state.xai_whatif_items_multi=idxs
        for j,ix in enumerate(idxs):
            ci=int(work.iloc[ix]["candidate_index"]); st.session_state[f"xai_wf_units_{ci}"]=int(qtys[j])
        st.session_state.xai_wf_pkg_mode=mode
    with pc1:
        if st.button("⚡ 비용효율형",use_container_width=True,key="xai_pkg_eff"): _apply_pkg("EFF")
        st.caption("적은 추가비용으로 효과가 큰 후보부터")
    with pc2:
        if st.button("◈ 균형형",use_container_width=True,key="xai_pkg_bal"): _apply_pkg("BAL")
        st.caption("비용과 위험방어를 함께 고려")
    with pc3:
        if st.button("⬡ 안정형",use_container_width=True,key="xai_pkg_stable"): _apply_pkg("STABLE")
        st.caption("취약품목을 더 넓게 묶어 방어")

    st.markdown("**3. 추천 패키지를 확인하세요.**")
    st.caption("상단 ② AI 보강 탐색의 회색 삼각형은 A/B 결과를 근거로 계산한 예상 우선순위입니다. 실제 10,000회 결과는 파란 검증점으로 확정됩니다.")
    opts=list(range(len(work)))
    def _fmt(i):
        r=work.iloc[int(i)]
        nm=str(r.get("display_name","수리부속"))
        unit=float(r.get("unit_cost",0.0))
        return (("최우선 · " if int(i)==0 else ("우선 · " if int(i)<4 else "후보 · ")) + nm + ((" · 1개 " + _xai_compact_money(unit)) if unit>0 else ""))
    if "xai_whatif_items_multi" in st.session_state:
        chosen=st.multiselect(
            "필요하면 최대 6개까지 조합을 수정할 수 있습니다.", opts,
            format_func=_fmt, key="xai_whatif_items_multi",
            max_selections=min(6,len(opts)),
        )
    else:
        chosen=st.multiselect(
            "필요하면 최대 6개까지 조합을 수정할 수 있습니다.", opts, default=[0],
            format_func=_fmt, key="xai_whatif_items_multi",
            max_selections=min(6,len(opts)),
        )
    if not chosen:
        st.markdown("<div class='xai-wf-judge'><b>보강 패키지가 비어 있습니다.</b><br>수리부속을 하나 이상 선택하면 품목별 추가수량을 설정할 수 있습니다.</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)
        return

    st.markdown("**4. 필요할 때만 보강 강도를 미세 조정하세요.**")
    adjustments=[]; chips=[]; estimated_extra=0.0
    for pos,i in enumerate(chosen):
        r=work.iloc[int(i)]; ci=int(r["candidate_index"]); nm=str(r.get("display_name","수리부속")); unit_cost=float(r.get("unit_cost",0.0))
        key=f"xai_wf_units_{ci}"
        cols=st.columns([3.2,1.0,1.6])
        with cols[0]:
            st.markdown(f"<div class='xai-wf-package'><span style='font-size:11px;color:#8499A6'>보강 품목 {pos+1}</span><br><b>{html.escape(nm)}</b></div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)
        with cols[1]:
            if key in st.session_state:
                units=st.select_slider("권장 보강수량",options=list(range(1,11)),key=key,label_visibility="collapsed")
            else:
                units=st.select_slider("권장 보강수량",options=list(range(1,11)),value=1,key=key,label_visibility="collapsed")
        item_cost=unit_cost*int(units); estimated_extra += item_cost
        with cols[2]:
            st.markdown(f"<div class='xai-wf-package' style='text-align:right'><span style='font-size:11px;color:#8499A6'>추가비용</span><br><b>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(item_cost))}</b></div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)
        adjustments.append({"candidate_index":ci,"add_units":int(units)})
        chips.append(f"<span class='xai-wf-chip'>{html.escape(nm)} +{int(units)} · {html.escape(_xai_compact_money(item_cost))}</span>")

    # AI 사전예측: 선택 패키지의 품목별 A/B 관측 한계효과를 합산한 우선순위용 예상치.
    _mc_sdf = mc.get("scenario_df") if isinstance(mc,dict) else None
    _base_short_mean = 0.0
    if isinstance(_mc_sdf,pd.DataFrame) and not _mc_sdf.empty:
        _col = f"{base_label}_shortage_events"
        if _col not in _mc_sdf.columns: _col=f"{base_label}_short"
        if _col in _mc_sdf.columns: _base_short_mean=float(pd.to_numeric(_mc_sdf[_col],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).mean())
    _pred_events_total=0.0; _conf_pts=[]
    for adj in adjustments:
        rr=work[work["candidate_index"]==int(adj["candidate_index"])]
        if rr.empty: continue
        r0=rr.iloc[0]; imp=abs(float(r0.get("risk_diff_events",0) or 0)); sg=abs(float(r0.get("stock_diff",0) or 0)); dem=float(r0.get("demand_events",0) or 0)
        if sg>=0.5:
            per_unit=imp/max(sg,1.0); _conf_pts.append(2 if imp>=5 else 1)
        else:
            per_unit=min(imp,max(dem*0.08,0.0)); _conf_pts.append(0)
        _pred_events_total += per_unit*int(adj["add_units"])
    _pred_pct = max(0.0,min(100.0,100.0*_pred_events_total/max(_base_short_mean,1e-9))) if _base_short_mean>0 else 0.0
    _conf_avg=(sum(_conf_pts)/max(len(_conf_pts),1)) if _conf_pts else 0.0
    _conf_txt="높음" if _conf_avg>=1.5 else ("보통" if _conf_avg>=.7 else "낮음")
    st.markdown(f"<div class='xai-wf-ai'><span style='font-size:11px;color:#8FA5B3'>자동 추천 사전예측 · 실제 재시뮬레이션 전</span><br><b style='font-size:18px'>이 패키지는 부족위험을 약 {_pred_pct:.1f}% 낮출 가능성이 있습니다.</b><div style='font-size:12px;color:#B8C7D0;margin-top:4px'>예상 신뢰도 {_conf_txt} · A/B에서 이미 관측된 재고차이와 부족차이를 품목별 한계효과로 환산한 우선순위용 추정입니다. 실제 효과는 아래 10,000회 재시뮬레이션으로 확정됩니다.</div></div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)

    total_units=sum(x["add_units"] for x in adjustments)
    base_cost=cost_a if base_label=="A" else cost_b
    other_cost=cost_b if base_label=="A" else cost_a
    reinforced_cost=base_cost+estimated_extra
    other_label="B" if base_label=="A" else "A"
    diff_after=reinforced_cost-other_cost
    st.markdown("<div class='xai-wf-cost-grid'>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>A 현재 재고비용</small><strong>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(cost_a))}</strong></div>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>B 현재 재고비용</small><strong>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(cost_b))}</strong></div>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>이번 보강 추가비용</small><strong>+{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(estimated_extra))}</strong></div>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card accent'><small>{html.escape(base_label)}+보강 예상 총비용</small><strong>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(reinforced_cost))}</strong><div style='font-size:11px;color:#9FC2D4;margin-top:3px'>{html.escape(other_label)} 대비 {('+' if diff_after>=0 else '')}{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(diff_after))}</div></div>"
                "</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)
    st.markdown("<div class='xai-wf-judge'><b>현재 AI 시뮬레이션 명령</b><br>"+html.escape(base_label)+" 대안에 아래 보강 패키지를 적용하고 동일한 10,000개 미래를 다시 비교합니다.<br><div style='margin-top:7px'>"+"".join(chips)+f"</div><div style='margin-top:7px;color:#92A8B5'>총 {len(adjustments)}개 품목 · 추가 {total_units}개 · 예상 추가비용 {html.escape(_xai_compact_money(estimated_extra))}</div></div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)

    run=st.button("✦ 이 보강 패키지로 AI 10,000회 재시뮬레이션",use_container_width=True,key="xai_whatif_run_multi")
    if run:
        cfg_d=mc.get("config",{}) if isinstance(mc.get("config"),dict) else {}
        cfg=MonteCarloConfig(iterations=int(cfg_d.get("iterations",10000)),random_seed=int(cfg_d.get("random_seed",42)),stress_factor=float(cfg_d.get("stress_factor",1.10)),failure_rate_lognormal_sigma=float(cfg_d.get("failure_rate_lognormal_sigma",0.0)))
        with st.spinner(f"{len(adjustments)}개 품목 보강안을 같은 미래 10,000개에 다시 적용하고 있습니다..."):
            wr=run_multi_item_stock_whatif(result,a_key,b_key,base_label,adjustments,cfg)
        current_policy_sig = _xai_policy_signature_from_mc(mc)
        if isinstance(wr, dict):
            wr["_xai_policy_sig"] = current_policy_sig
            wr["base_stock_cost"] = base_cost
            wr["other_stock_cost"] = other_cost
            wr["reinforced_stock_cost"] = base_cost + float(wr.get("additional_cost",0.0))
        if isinstance(wr, dict):
            _hist = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_history", [])
            if not isinstance(_hist, list): _hist=[]
            _base_label=str(wr.get("base_label",base_label)).upper()
            _total_cost=float(wr.get("reinforced_stock_cost", base_cost+float(wr.get("additional_cost",0.0))))
            _after_short=float(wr.get("after",{}).get("mean_shortage_events",0.0) or 0.0)
            _items=wr.get("adjustments",[]) if isinstance(wr.get("adjustments"),list) else []
            _short_label=(" + ".join(str(x.get("item_label","수리부속")) for x in _items[:2]) + (f" 외 {len(_items)-2}개" if len(_items)>2 else "")) if _items else f"{_base_label}+보강"
            _hist.append({"policy_sig":current_policy_sig,"base_label":_base_label,"item_label":str(wr.get("item_label","보강 패키지")),"short_label":_short_label,"item_count":int(wr.get("item_count",0) or 0),"total_add_units":int(wr.get("total_add_units",0) or 0),"additional_cost":float(wr.get("additional_cost",0.0) or 0.0),"total_cost":_total_cost,"after_shortage":_after_short})
            st.session_state.xai_whatif_history=_hist[-12:]
        st.session_state.xai_whatif_result=wr
        st.session_state.xai_whatif_policy_sig=current_policy_sig
        if _fragment_decorator is not None: st.rerun(scope="fragment")
        else: st.rerun()

    wr=st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_result")
    if not isinstance(wr,dict):
        st.markdown("<div class='xai-wf-judge'><b>AI 대기 중</b><br>보강 패키지를 만든 뒤 재시뮬레이션을 누르면 상단 그래프에 새로운 보강정책 선이 추가됩니다.</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)
        return

    before=wr.get("before",{}); after=wr.get("after",{}); delta=wr.get("delta",{}); extra=float(wr.get("additional_cost",0)); items=wr.get("adjustments",[]) if isinstance(wr.get("adjustments"),list) else []
    actual_base=float(wr.get("base_stock_cost", cost_a if str(wr.get("base_label","A")).upper()=="A" else cost_b))
    actual_total=float(wr.get("reinforced_stock_cost", actual_base+extra))
    actual_other=float(wr.get("other_stock_cost", cost_b if str(wr.get("base_label","A")).upper()=="A" else cost_a))
    other_label2="B" if str(wr.get("base_label","A")).upper()=="A" else "A"
    if items:
        summary=" · ".join(f"{str(x.get('item_label','수리부속'))} +{int(x.get('add_units',0))}" for x in items[:4])
        if len(items)>4: summary += f" · 외 {len(items)-4}개"
    else:
        summary=str(wr.get("item_label","수리부속"))
    st.markdown(f"<div class='xai-wf-ai'><span style='font-size:11px;color:#8FA5B3'>재시뮬레이션 완료 · 상단 그래프에 새 정책선 반영</span><br><b style='font-size:17px'>{html.escape(str(wr.get('base_label','A')))}+보강 · {html.escape(summary)}</b><div style='font-size:12px;color:#B8C7D0;margin-top:4px'>총 {int(wr.get('item_count',len(items)))}개 품목 / +{int(wr.get('total_add_units',wr.get('add_units',0)))}개 · 추가 투자 {_xai_compact_money(extra)} · 보강 후 총비용 {_xai_compact_money(actual_total)} · 동일한 미래를 사용해 보강 전후를 직접 비교했습니다.</div></div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)

    st.markdown("<div class='xai-wf-cost-grid'>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>보강 전 기준비용</small><strong>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(actual_base))}</strong></div>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>실제 추가 보강비용</small><strong>+{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(extra))}</strong></div>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card accent'><small>보강 후 총비용</small><strong>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(actual_total))}</strong></div>"
                f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>{html.escape(other_label2)} 대안과 비용차이</small><strong>{('+' if actual_total-actual_other>=0 else '')}{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(actual_total-actual_other))}</strong></div>"
                "</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)

    cards=[("전체 평균 Ao",f"{float(after.get('mean_Ao',0))*100:.2f}%",f"{float(delta.get('mean_Ao_pp',0)):+.2f}%p"),("보통의 미래 Ao",f"{float(after.get('p50_Ao',0))*100:.2f}%",f"{float(delta.get('p50_Ao_pp',0)):+.2f}%p"),("많이 불리한 미래 Ao",f"{float(after.get('p10_Ao',0))*100:.2f}%",f"{float(delta.get('p10_Ao_pp',0)):+.2f}%p"),("가장 불리한 미래 Ao",f"{float(after.get('p05_Ao',0))*100:.2f}%",f"{float(delta.get('p05_Ao_pp',0)):+.2f}%p")]
    st.markdown("<div class='xai-wf-grid'>"+"".join([f"<div class='xai-wf-card'><small>{html.escape(k)}</small><strong>{html.escape(v)}</strong><div style='font-size:11px;color:#9FC2D4;margin-top:3px'>변화 {html.escape(dd)}</div></div>" for k,v,dd in cards])+"</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)

    cats=["전체 평균","보통의 미래","많이 불리한 미래","가장 불리한 미래"]
    bv=[float(before.get('mean_Ao',0))*100,float(before.get('p50_Ao',0))*100,float(before.get('p10_Ao',0))*100,float(before.get('p05_Ao',0))*100]
    av=[float(after.get('mean_Ao',0))*100,float(after.get('p50_Ao',0))*100,float(after.get('p10_Ao',0))*100,float(after.get('p05_Ao',0))*100]
    f=go.Figure(); f.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=cats,y=bv,mode="lines+markers",name="보강 전",line=dict(width=4,color="#778995"),marker=dict(size=10,color="#778995"))); f.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=cats,y=av,mode="lines+markers",name="보강 후",line=dict(width=4,color="#006CFF"),marker=dict(size=11,color="#006CFF")))
    f.update_layout(height=350,margin=dict(l=45,r=20,t=35,b=45),yaxis_title="운용가용도 Ao (%)",hovermode="x unified",legend=dict(orientation="h",y=1.08,font=dict(color="#DCE6EC")),paper_bgcolor="#0A121A",plot_bgcolor="#0E1720",font=dict(family="Malgun Gothic",color="#DCE6EC"),xaxis=dict(gridcolor="#263645"),yaxis=dict(gridcolor="#263645"))
    st.plotly_chart(f,use_container_width=True,key="xai_whatif_before_after_multi")

    tail=float(delta.get("p05_Ao_pp",0)); mean=float(delta.get("mean_Ao_pp",0)); mid=float(delta.get("p50_Ao_pp",0)); short=float(delta.get("mean_shortage_events",0))
    if tail>mean+.05: txt=f"이 보강 패키지는 평소보다 나쁜 미래에서 더 큰 효과를 보입니다. 평균 Ao는 {mean:+.2f}%p, 가장 불리한 미래에서는 {tail:+.2f}%p 변했습니다. 추가비용은 {_xai_compact_money(extra)}입니다."
    elif abs(tail)<.05 and abs(mean)<.05: txt="이 보강 패키지는 미래 운용분포를 거의 바꾸지 못했습니다. 추가비용 {_xai_compact_money(extra)}까지 고려하면 품목 조합을 다시 구성하는 편이 합리적입니다."
    else: txt=f"이 보강 패키지로 보통의 미래 Ao는 {mid:+.2f}%p, 가장 불리한 미래 Ao는 {tail:+.2f}%p 변했습니다. 미래 한 번당 평균 부족발생 변화는 {short:+.2f}회이며 추가비용은 {_xai_compact_money(extra)}입니다."
    st.markdown(f"<div class='xai-wf-judge'><b>자동 추천 판단</b><br>{html.escape(txt)}</div>",unsafe_allow_html=True)




def _xai_render_whatif_compact(result: dict, idf: pd.DataFrame, A: dict | None = None, B: dict | None = None) -> None:
    """WHAT-IF: A/B를 각각 독립 추천하고 A+, B+를 동일 미래에서 검증한다."""
    mc = st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result")
    if not isinstance(mc, dict) or not isinstance(idf, pd.DataFrame) or idf.empty:
        return
    a_key = str(mc.get("A_key", "") or "")
    b_key = str(mc.get("B_key", "") or "")
    if not a_key or not b_key:
        return

    A = A if isinstance(A, dict) else {}
    B = B if isinstance(B, dict) else {}
    work0 = idf.copy()
    if "candidate_index" not in work0.columns:
        return
    work0["candidate_index"] = pd.to_numeric(work0["candidate_index"], errors="coerce")
    work0 = work0.dropna(subset=["candidate_index"]).copy()
    if work0.empty:
        return
    work0["candidate_index"] = work0["candidate_index"].astype(int)
    for _c in ["demand_events", "unit_cost", "A_shortage_events", "B_shortage_events", "lead_time_h", "A_qty", "B_qty"]:
        if _c not in work0.columns:
            work0[_c] = 0.0
        work0[_c] = pd.to_numeric(work0[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
    if "display_name" not in work0.columns:
        work0["display_name"] = work0.get("part_id", pd.Series(["수리부속"] * len(work0))).astype(str)
    work0["common_short"] = work0[["A_shortage_events", "B_shortage_events"]].min(axis=1)

    costA = float(_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
    costB = float(_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
    riskA = float(_xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0)))
    riskB = float(_xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0)))
    aoA = _xai_pct(A.get("mean_Ao", A.get("nominal_Ao", 0.0)))
    aoB = _xai_pct(B.get("mean_Ao", B.get("nominal_Ao", 0.0)))
    p05A = _xai_pct(A.get("p05_Ao", A.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)))
    p05B = _xai_pct(B.get("p05_Ao", B.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)))

    def _rank_for(side: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        w = work0.copy()
        own = w[f"{side}_shortage_events"]
        other = w["B_shortage_events" if side == "A" else "A_shortage_events"]
        w["own_short"] = own
        w["relative_gap"] = (own - other).clip(lower=0.0)
        max_own = max(float(w["own_short"].max()), 1.0)
        max_gap = max(float(w["relative_gap"].max()), 1.0)
        max_common = max(float(w["common_short"].max()), 1.0)
        max_dem = max(float(w["demand_events"].max()), 1.0)
        max_lead = max(float(w["lead_time_h"].max()), 1.0)
        # Independent recommendation: own repeated shortage first, then relative gap,
        # common missed risk, demand exposure and long lead time.
        w["recommend_score"] = (
            .44 * (w["own_short"] / max_own)
            + .22 * (w["relative_gap"] / max_gap)
            + .16 * (w["common_short"] / max_common)
            + .11 * (w["demand_events"] / max_dem)
            + .07 * (w["lead_time_h"] / max_lead)
        )
        w = w[(w["own_short"] > 0) | (w["common_short"] > 0)].copy()
        return w.sort_values(["recommend_score", "own_short", "demand_events"], ascending=False).head(20).reset_index(drop=True)

    rankA = _rank_for("A")
    rankB = _rank_for("B")
    _manual_lookup = work0.drop_duplicates("candidate_index").set_index("candidate_index", drop=False)

    st.markdown("""<style>
    .wf28-shell{position:relative;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#06131d 0%,#071b27 52%,#06131d 100%);border:1px solid #24536a;border-radius:22px;padding:0;overflow:hidden;margin:12px 0 8px;color:#dcebf3;box-shadow:inset 0 1px 0 rgba(133,215,255,.05)}
    .wf28-topline{height:2px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,#55c9ff 22%,#8ce9c5 52%,#ffad70 82%,transparent);opacity:.72}.wf28-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;align-items:center;padding:15px 18px 13px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(76,131,158,.28);background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(14,45,61,.56),rgba(7,22,31,.18))}.wf28-kicker{font-size:9px;letter-spacing:2px;color:#6fa5ba;font-weight:800}.wf28-title{font-size:19px;font-weight:900;color:#e7f7ff;letter-spacing:.2px;margin-top:2px}.wf28-live{font-size:10px;color:#8ce9c5;border:1px solid #285f5a;background:#082823;border-radius:999px;padding:7px 10px;white-space:nowrap}.wf28-guide{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(76,131,158,.22)}.wf28-step{padding:9px 12px;font-size:10px;color:#779dac;border-right:1px solid rgba(76,131,158,.18)}.wf28-step:last-child{border-right:0}.wf28-step b{display:block;color:#bfe9f8;font-size:11px;margin-bottom:2px}.wf28-bodyhint{padding:10px 18px 12px;color:#7ea6b6;font-size:10px;line-height:1.6}.wf28-bodyhint strong{color:#c9eefb}
    .wf18sum{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px;margin:10px 0}.wf18c{border:1px solid #24485a;background:linear-gradient(160deg,#081d29,#071722);border-radius:13px;padding:10px;box-shadow:inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.025)}.wf18c small{display:block;color:#789cae;font-size:9px}.wf18c b{display:block;font-size:15px;color:#ebf9ff;margin-top:3px}.wf18move{display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:7px 0 3px}.wf18chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:6px 9px;border-radius:999px;background:#0b2633;border:1px solid #2b5366;color:#d9eef7;font-size:11px}.wf18chip b{color:#91f2cd}.wf18result{border:1px solid #24566e;background:#072331;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 12px;color:#b7dfef;font-size:11px;line-height:1.7;margin-top:8px}.wf18hint{font-size:10px;color:#7599aa;margin:4px 0 8px}
    div[data-testid="stTabs"] button{font-weight:800!important}div[data-testid="stMultiSelect"],div[data-testid="stTextInput"],div[data-testid="stSelectSlider"]{border-radius:12px}.stButton button p{white-space:nowrap!important}.stMultiSelect label p,.stSelectbox label p{white-space:nowrap!important}@media(max-width:850px){.wf18sum,.wf28-guide{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.wf28-head{align-items:flex-start;flex-direction:column}}
    </style><div class="wf28-shell"><div class="wf28-topline"></div><div class="wf28-head"><div><div class="wf28-kicker">VISUAL INFORM · 04</div><div class="wf28-title">WHAT IF · 수리부속 보강 시뮬레이션</div></div><div class="wf28-live">● 10,000 SCENARIO READY</div></div><div class="wf28-guide"><div class="wf28-step"><b>01 · 선택</b>자동추천 또는 직접 검색</div><div class="wf28-step"><b>02 · 수량</b>품목별 +1~+10개 지정</div><div class="wf28-step"><b>03 · 실행</b>A / B / 동시 재시뮬레이션</div><div class="wf28-step"><b>04 · 확인</b>RISK COVERAGE 변화 재생</div></div><div class="wf28-bodyhint"><strong>자동추천에 없는 품목도 직접 선택할 수 있습니다.</strong> 선택한 재고를 실제 A+ / B+ 정책으로 적용해 동일한 미래 10,000개 시나리오에서 다시 계산합니다.</div></div>""", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    def _render_side(side: str, rank: pd.DataFrame):
        side_cost = costA if side == "A" else costB
        side_ao = aoA if side == "A" else aoB
        side_p05 = p05A if side == "A" else p05B
        side_risk = riskA if side == "A" else riskB
        st.markdown(
            f"<div class='wf18sum'><div class='wf18c'><small>현재 비용</small><b>{_xai_compact_money(side_cost)}</b></div>"
            f"<div class='wf18c'><small>평균 Ao</small><b>{side_ao:.2f}%</b></div>"
            f"<div class='wf18c'><small>하위 5% Ao</small><b>{side_p05:.2f}%</b></div>"
            f"<div class='wf18c'><small>평균 부족</small><b>{side_risk:.2f}회</b></div></div>",
            unsafe_allow_html=True,
        )
        # V26: 자동추천 후보가 없어도 수동 보강은 항상 사용할 수 있어야 한다.
        # 이전 V25는 여기서 return 하여 수동 검색/선택 UI가 렌더링되지 않는 문제가 있었다.
        if rank.empty:
            st.caption(f"{side} 자동추천 후보는 없습니다. 아래 수동 보강에서 전체 품목을 직접 선택할 수 있습니다.")

        opts = list(range(len(rank)))
        state_key = f"xai_wf_{side}_items"
        if state_key not in st.session_state:
            st.session_state[state_key] = list(range(min(3, len(opts))))
            for j, i in enumerate(st.session_state[state_key]):
                ci = int(rank.iloc[i]["candidate_index"])
                st.session_state[f"xai_wf_{side}_qty_{ci}"] = 2 if j == 0 else 1

        def _fmt(i):
            r = rank.iloc[int(i)]
            return f"{str(r.get('display_name','수리부속'))} · 부족 {float(r.get('own_short',0)):.0f}회 · 1개 {_xai_compact_money(float(r.get('unit_cost',0)))}"

        defaults = [i for i in st.session_state.get(state_key, []) if isinstance(i, int) and 0 <= i < len(opts)]
        if opts:
            chosen = st.multiselect(
                "자동 추천 보강", opts, default=defaults, format_func=_fmt,
                max_selections=min(6, len(opts)), key=f"wf18_{side}_items_widget"
            )
        else:
            chosen = []
        st.session_state[state_key] = list(chosen)

        auto_rows = [rank.iloc[int(i)] for i in chosen]
        auto_ci = {int(r["candidate_index"]) for r in auto_rows}

        # V25 · Manual reinforcement is intentionally explicit and searchable.
        # It is not limited to the automatic recommendation list.
        st.markdown("**수동 보강 품목 직접 추가 · 실제 재시뮬레이션 반영**")
        st.caption("자동추천과 무관하게 전체 품목에서 직접 선택할 수 있습니다. 품목 선택 → +1~+10개 수량 지정 → 하단 '보강 실행' 버튼을 누르면 동일 미래 10,000회에 실제 반영됩니다.")
        _search_key=f"wf25_{side}_manual_search"
        _manual_widget_key=f"wf25_{side}_manual_widget"
        _query=st.text_input("품번 또는 품명 검색", key=_search_key, placeholder="예: Part_ID 또는 품명 입력")
        _manual_df=_manual_lookup.loc[[int(x) for x in _manual_lookup.index.tolist() if int(x) not in auto_ci]].copy()
        if _query and str(_query).strip():
            _q=str(_query).strip().lower()
            _name_s=_manual_df.get("display_name",pd.Series("",index=_manual_df.index)).astype(str).str.lower()
            _part_s=_manual_df.get("part_id",pd.Series("",index=_manual_df.index)).astype(str).str.lower()
            _manual_df=_manual_df[_name_s.str.contains(_q,regex=False)|_part_s.str.contains(_q,regex=False)]
        else:
            # 검색어가 없어도 바로 선택할 수 있도록 수요/리드타임 상위 품목을 제시한다.
            _manual_df=_manual_df.sort_values(["demand_events","lead_time_h","unit_cost"],ascending=[False,False,False])
        _existing=[int(x) for x in st.session_state.get(_manual_widget_key,[]) if int(x) in _manual_lookup.index and int(x) not in auto_ci]
        _matches=[int(x) for x in _manual_df.index.tolist()[:80]]
        manual_opts=list(dict.fromkeys(_existing+_matches))
        def _fmt_manual(ci):
            r = _manual_lookup.loc[int(ci)]
            own = float(r.get(f"{side}_shortage_events",0.0))
            pid=str(r.get("part_id","") or "")
            nm=str(r.get("display_name","수리부속"))
            lead=float(r.get("lead_time_h",0.0) or 0.0)/24.0
            return f"{pid + ' · ' if pid else ''}{nm} · 부족 {own:.0f}회 · 리드타임 {lead:.0f}일 · 1개 {_xai_compact_money(float(r.get('unit_cost',0)))}"
        if _manual_widget_key not in st.session_state:
            st.session_state[_manual_widget_key]=[]
        manual = st.multiselect(
            "검색 결과에서 보강할 품목 선택", manual_opts,
            format_func=_fmt_manual, max_selections=min(8, max(len(manual_opts),1)),
            key=_manual_widget_key,
            help="선택한 품목은 자동추천 품목과 합쳐져 동일한 WHAT-IF 10,000회 재시뮬레이션에 실제 반영됩니다."
        )
        if _query and not _matches:
            st.caption("검색 결과가 없습니다. 품번 또는 품명의 일부만 입력해 다시 검색해 보세요.")

        selected_rows = list(auto_rows)
        for ci in manual:
            if int(ci) not in {int(r["candidate_index"]) for r in selected_rows}:
                selected_rows.append(_manual_lookup.loc[int(ci)])

        if not selected_rows:
            return [], 0.0, 0.0, []

        adjustments, extra, chips, names = [], 0.0, [], []
        cols = st.columns(min(3, len(selected_rows)))
        for j, r in enumerate(selected_rows):
            ci = int(r["candidate_index"])
            nm = str(r.get("display_name", "수리부속"))
            unit = float(r.get("unit_cost", 0.0))
            qkey = f"wf18_{side}_qty_{ci}"
            default_q = int(st.session_state.get(f"xai_wf_{side}_qty_{ci}", 2 if j == 0 and ci in auto_ci else 1))
            with cols[j % len(cols)]:
                q = st.select_slider(nm, options=list(range(1, 11)), value=max(1, min(10, default_q)), key=qkey)
                st.caption(f"+{q}개 · {_xai_compact_money(unit*q)}")
            st.session_state[f"xai_wf_{side}_qty_{ci}"] = int(q)
            adjustments.append({"candidate_index": ci, "add_units": int(q)})
            extra += unit * q
            chips.append(f"<span class='wf18chip'>{html.escape(nm)} <b>+{int(q)}</b> ⇢ {side}+</span>")
            names.append(nm)
        st.markdown("<div class='wf18move'>" + "".join(chips) + "</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

        own_col = f"{side}_shortage_events"
        own_total = max(float(pd.to_numeric(work0[own_col],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).sum()), 1e-9)
        selected_idx = {int(x["candidate_index"]) for x in adjustments}
        selected_short = float(pd.to_numeric(work0.loc[work0["candidate_index"].isin(selected_idx), own_col],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).sum())
        share = max(0.0, min(100.0, 100.0 * selected_short / own_total)) if own_total > 1e-9 else 0.0
        st.markdown(
            f"<div class='wf18hint'>선택 품목이 {side} 보강 후보군의 반복 부족 중 {share:.0f}%에 해당 · 추가비용 {_xai_compact_money(extra)}</div>",
            unsafe_allow_html=True,
        )
        return adjustments, extra, share, names

    tabA, tabB = st.tabs(["A", "B"])
    with tabA:
        adjA, extraA, shareA, namesA = _render_side("A", rankA)
    with tabB:
        adjB, extraB, shareB, namesB = _render_side("B", rankB)

    def _cfg():
        cfgd = mc.get("config", {}) if isinstance(mc.get("config"), dict) else {}
        return MonteCarloConfig(
            iterations=int(cfgd.get("iterations", 10000)),
            random_seed=int(cfgd.get("random_seed", 42)),
            stress_factor=float(cfgd.get("stress_factor", 1.10)),
            failure_rate_lognormal_sigma=float(cfgd.get("failure_rate_lognormal_sigma", 0.0)),
        )

    sig = _xai_policy_signature_from_mc(mc)

    def _execute_side(side: str, adjustments: list[dict]):
        if not adjustments:
            return None
        try:
            wr = run_multi_item_stock_whatif(result, a_key, b_key, side, adjustments, _cfg())
        except Exception as exc:
            st.session_state["xai_whatif_last_error"] = f"{side} 보강 실행 실패: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            return None
        if isinstance(wr, dict):
            base_cost = costA if side == "A" else costB
            other_cost = costB if side == "A" else costA
            wr["_xai_policy_sig"] = sig
            wr["base_stock_cost"] = base_cost
            wr["other_stock_cost"] = other_cost
            wr["reinforced_stock_cost"] = base_cost + float(wr.get("additional_cost", 0.0) or 0.0)
            st.session_state[f"xai_whatif_result_{side}"] = wr
            st.session_state["xai_whatif_policy_sig"] = sig
            st.session_state["xai_whatif_result"] = wr
            st.session_state["xai_whatif_last_error"] = ""
            _aft = wr.get("after", {}) if isinstance(wr.get("after"), dict) else {}
            _bef = wr.get("before", {}) if isinstance(wr.get("before"), dict) else {}
            _br = float(_bef.get("mean_risk_coverage", 0.0) or 0.0) * 100.0
            _ar = float(_aft.get("mean_risk_coverage", 0.0) or 0.0) * 100.0
            st.session_state["xai_whatif_last_message"] = (
                f"{side}+ 보강 완료 · {len(wr.get('adjustments', []))}종 / +{int(wr.get('total_add_units', 0) or 0)}개 · "
                f"Risk Coverage {_br:.2f}% → {_ar:.2f}%"
            )
        return wr

    cA, cB, cBoth = st.columns(3)
    runA = cA.button("A만 보강 실행", key="wf18_run_A", use_container_width=True, disabled=not bool(adjA))
    runB = cB.button("B만 보강 실행", key="wf18_run_B", use_container_width=True, disabled=not bool(adjB))
    runBoth = cBoth.button("둘 다 실행", key="wf18_run_both", use_container_width=True, disabled=not bool(adjA and adjB))
    _wf_err = str(st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_last_error", "") or "")
    _wf_msg = str(st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_last_message", "") or "")
    if _wf_err:
        st.error(_wf_err)
    elif _wf_msg:
        st.success(_wf_msg + " · 아래 VISUAL INFORM의 WHAT-IF 재생 버튼과 INVESTMENT VALUE에 반영됩니다.")

    if runA or runB or runBoth:
        # Isolate WHAT-IF from the Integrated Results A/B simulation component.
        # This also protects older Streamlit versions that fall back to a full-app rerun.
        st.session_state["xai_suppress_risk_request_until"] = time.time() + 8.0
        if runBoth:
            with st.spinner("A+와 B+를 같은 미래 10,000개에서 각각 재시뮬레이션하고 있습니다..."):
                _execute_side("A", adjA)
                _execute_side("B", adjB)
        elif runA:
            with st.spinner("A+를 같은 미래 10,000개에서 재시뮬레이션하고 있습니다..."):
                _execute_side("A", adjA)
        elif runB:
            with st.spinner("B+를 같은 미래 10,000개에서 재시뮬레이션하고 있습니다..."):
                _execute_side("B", adjB)
        if _fragment_decorator is not None:
            st.rerun(scope="fragment")
        else:
            st.rerun()

    wrA = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_result_A")
    wrB = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_result_B")
    if not isinstance(wrA, dict) and not isinstance(wrB, dict):
        st.caption("A 또는 B를 실행하면 A / B / A+ / B+를 같은 미래에서 비교합니다.")
        return

    def _result_point(wr, side):
        if not isinstance(wr, dict):
            return None
        aft = wr.get("after", {}) if isinstance(wr.get("after"), dict) else {}
        bef = wr.get("before", {}) if isinstance(wr.get("before"), dict) else {}
        base_cost = costA if side == "A" else costB
        total_cost = float(wr.get("reinforced_stock_cost", base_cost + float(wr.get("additional_cost", 0.0) or 0.0)))
        ao = _xai_pct(aft.get("mean_Ao", 0.0))
        p05 = _xai_pct(aft.get("p05_Ao", aft.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)))
        risk = float(aft.get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0) or 0.0)
        before_ao = _xai_pct(bef.get("mean_Ao", aoA if side == "A" else aoB))
        before_risk = float(bef.get("mean_shortage_events", riskA if side == "A" else riskB) or 0.0)
        extra = float(wr.get("additional_cost", 0.0) or 0.0)
        return {"cost": total_cost, "ao": ao, "p05": p05, "risk": risk, "before_ao": before_ao, "before_risk": before_risk, "extra": extra}

    pA = _result_point(wrA, "A")
    pB = _result_point(wrB, "B")

    # 4-policy summary cards (available reinforced policies only).
    cards = [
        ("A", costA, aoA, riskA, ""),
        ("B", costB, aoB, riskB, ""),
    ]
    if pA: cards.append(("A+", pA["cost"], pA["ao"], pA["risk"], "ra"))
    if pB: cards.append(("B+", pB["cost"], pB["ao"], pB["risk"], "rb"))
    st.markdown("<div class='wf18sum'>" + "".join(
        f"<div class='wf18c {cls}'><small>{lab} · 비용 / Ao / 부족</small><b>{_xai_compact_money(c)} · {ao:.2f}% · {rk:.2f}</b></div>"
        for lab,c,ao,rk,cls in cards
    ) + "</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    # Main graph 1: cost vs Ao, A/B/A+/B+
    fig1 = go.Figure()
    pts = [("A", costA, aoA, "#59c9ff", 17), ("B", costB, aoB, "#ff9a60", 17)]
    if pA: pts.append(("A+", pA["cost"], pA["ao"], "#1877ff", 21))
    if pB: pts.append(("B+", pB["cost"], pB["ao"], "#d66d2d", 21))
    for lab,c,ao,col,sz in pts:
        fig1.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[c/1e8], y=[ao], mode="markers+text", text=[lab], textposition="top center", name=lab,
                                  marker=dict(size=sz, color=col, line=dict(width=2, color="#c9ecff" if lab.startswith("A") else "#ffd3b8")),
                                  hovertemplate=f"{lab}<br>비용 %{{x:.2f}}억원<br>평균 Ao %{{y:.2f}}%<extra></extra>"))
    fig1.update_layout(height=390, margin=dict(l=55,r=25,t=55,b=55), title=dict(text="비용 vs 운용가용도", font=dict(size=17)),
                       xaxis_title="총 재고비용 (억원)", yaxis_title="평균 Ao (%)", showlegend=False,
                       paper_bgcolor="#07131c", plot_bgcolor="#0b1923", font=dict(family="Malgun Gothic", color="#dcebf3"),
                       xaxis=dict(gridcolor="#203542"), yaxis=dict(gridcolor="#203542"))
    st.plotly_chart(fig1, use_container_width=True, key="wf18_cost_ao")

    # Main graph 2: cost vs shortage risk.
    fig2 = go.Figure()
    pts2 = [("A", costA, riskA, "#59c9ff", 16), ("B", costB, riskB, "#ff9a60", 16)]
    if pA: pts2.append(("A+", pA["cost"], pA["risk"], "#1877ff", 20))
    if pB: pts2.append(("B+", pB["cost"], pB["risk"], "#d66d2d", 20))
    for lab,c,rk,col,sz in pts2:
        fig2.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=[c/1e8], y=[rk], mode="markers+text", text=[lab], textposition="top center", name=lab,
                                  marker=dict(size=sz, color=col), hovertemplate=f"{lab}<br>비용 %{{x:.2f}}억원<br>평균 부족 %{{y:.2f}}회<extra></extra>"))
    fig2.update_layout(height=330, margin=dict(l=55,r=25,t=45,b=55), title=dict(text="비용 vs 부족위험", font=dict(size=15)),
                       xaxis_title="총 재고비용 (억원)", yaxis_title="미래 1회당 평균 부족발생", showlegend=False,
                       paper_bgcolor="#07131c", plot_bgcolor="#0b1923", font=dict(family="Malgun Gothic", color="#dcebf3"),
                       xaxis=dict(gridcolor="#203542"), yaxis=dict(gridcolor="#203542"))
    st.plotly_chart(fig2, use_container_width=True, key="wf18_cost_risk")

    # Compact, result-grounded sentences for each completed reinforced policy.
    lines = []
    if pA:
        red = 100.0 * (riskA - pA["risk"]) / riskA if riskA > 1e-9 else 0.0
        lines.append(f"A+ · {_xai_compact_money(pA['extra'])} 추가 · 평균 Ao {pA['ao']-aoA:+.2f}%p · 부족위험 {red:+.1f}%")
    if pB:
        red = 100.0 * (riskB - pB["risk"]) / riskB if riskB > 1e-9 else 0.0
        lines.append(f"B+ · {_xai_compact_money(pB['extra'])} 추가 · 평균 Ao {pB['ao']-aoB:+.2f}%p · 부족위험 {red:+.1f}%")
    if pA and pB:
        dc = pA["cost"] - pB["cost"]
        da = pA["ao"] - pB["ao"]
        dr = pA["risk"] - pB["risk"]
        lines.append(f"A+ ↔ B+ · 비용 {('+' if dc>=0 else '')}{_xai_compact_money(dc)} · Ao {da:+.2f}%p · 평균 부족 {dr:+.2f}회")
    st.markdown("<div class='wf18result'>" + "<br>".join(html.escape(x) for x in lines) + "</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

def _xai_policy_inventory_table(result: dict, solution_key: str, label: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Build a compact user-facing inventory list without O/I/D stage columns."""
    try:
        meta, vectors = _build_frontend_policy_payload(result)
    except Exception:
        return pd.DataFrame()
    if not isinstance(meta, list) or not isinstance(vectors, dict):
        return pd.DataFrame()
    vec = vectors.get(str(solution_key), {})
    if not isinstance(vec, dict):
        return pd.DataFrame()
    sO = list(vec.get("sO", []) or [])
    sI = list(vec.get("sI", []) or [])
    sD = list(vec.get("sD", []) or [])
    rows = []
    for i, m in enumerate(meta):
        try:
            qty = (int(sO[i]) if i < len(sO) else 0) + (int(sI[i]) if i < len(sI) else 0) + (int(sD[i]) if i < len(sD) else 0)
        except Exception:
            qty = 0
        if qty <= 0:
            continue
        unit_cost = float(pd.to_numeric(m.get("proc_cost", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
        lead_h = float(pd.to_numeric(m.get("lead_time", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
        nm = str(m.get("item_name", m.get("category", "")) or "").strip()
        pid = str(m.get("part_id", "") or "").strip()
        rows.append({
            "대안": label,
            "수리부속": nm if nm else pid,
            "Part_ID": pid,
            "총수량": qty,
            "단가(원)": unit_cost,
            "재고금액(원)": unit_cost * qty,
            "리드타임(일)": lead_h / 24.0,
            "선발주": "O" if bool(m.get("prebuy_flag", False)) else "",
            "보호재고": "O" if bool(m.get("protection_flag", False)) else "",
        })
    cols=["대안","수리부속","Part_ID","총수량","단가(원)","재고금액(원)","리드타임(일)","선발주","보호재고"]
    if not rows:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=cols)
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)[cols]
    return df.sort_values(["재고금액(원)", "총수량", "수리부속"], ascending=[False, False, True]).reset_index(drop=True)


def _xai_pct(v: float) -> float:
    x = float(_xai_num(v))
    return x * 100.0 if abs(x) <= 1.5 else x


def _xai_render_mission_briefing(result: dict, A: dict, B: dict, payload: dict, scenario: list | None = None, idf: pd.DataFrame | None = None) -> None:
    """JARVIS-style top visual INFORM layer."""
    a_key = str(payload.get("A_key", "") or "")
    b_key = str(payload.get("B_key", "") or "")
    inv_a = _xai_policy_inventory_table(result, a_key, "A")
    inv_b = _xai_policy_inventory_table(result, b_key, "B")

    # V31 · INVESTMENT VALUE XLSX export helpers (A / A+ / B / B+)
    def _xai_apply_whatif_inventory(_base_df: pd.DataFrame, _side: str, _label: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        _out = _base_df.copy() if isinstance(_base_df, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
        _wr = st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{str(_side).upper()}")
        if not isinstance(_wr, dict):
            return pd.DataFrame()
        try:
            _meta, _vectors = _build_frontend_policy_payload(result)
        except Exception:
            _meta = []
        if not isinstance(_meta, list):
            _meta = []
        if _out.empty:
            _out = pd.DataFrame(columns=["대안","수리부속","Part_ID","총수량","단가(원)","재고금액(원)","리드타임(일)","선발주","보호재고"])
        else:
            _out = _out.copy()
            _out["대안"] = _label
        for _adj in (_wr.get("adjustments", []) or []):
            if not isinstance(_adj, dict):
                continue
            try:
                _ci = int(_adj.get("candidate_index", -1))
                _add = int(_adj.get("add_units", 0))
            except Exception:
                continue
            if _ci < 0 or _add <= 0 or _ci >= len(_meta):
                continue
            _m = _meta[_ci] if isinstance(_meta[_ci], dict) else {}
            _pid = str(_m.get("part_id", "") or "").strip()
            _nm = str(_m.get("item_name", _m.get("category", "")) or "").strip()
            _unit = float(pd.to_numeric(_m.get("proc_cost", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
            _lead_h = float(pd.to_numeric(_m.get("lead_time", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
            _mask = (_out.get("Part_ID", pd.Series(dtype=str)).astype(str) == _pid) if _pid else pd.Series([False]*len(_out), index=_out.index)
            if bool(_mask.any()):
                _idx = _out.index[_mask][0]
                _out.at[_idx, "총수량"] = int(pd.to_numeric(_out.at[_idx, "총수량"], errors="coerce") or 0) + _add
                _out.at[_idx, "재고금액(원)"] = float(pd.to_numeric(_out.at[_idx, "단가(원)"], errors="coerce") or _unit) * int(_out.at[_idx, "총수량"] or 0)
                _out.at[_idx, "대안"] = _label
            else:
                _out = pd.concat([_out, pd.DataFrame([{
                    "대안": _label,
                    "수리부속": _nm if _nm else _pid,
                    "Part_ID": _pid,
                    "총수량": _add,
                    "단가(원)": _unit,
                    "재고금액(원)": _unit * _add,
                    "리드타임(일)": _lead_h / 24.0,
                    "선발주": "O" if bool(_m.get("prebuy_flag", False)) else "",
                    "보호재고": "O" if bool(_m.get("protection_flag", False)) else "",
                }])], ignore_index=True)
        if not _out.empty:
            for _c in ["총수량"]:
                if _c in _out.columns:
                    _out[_c] = pd.to_numeric(_out[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0).astype(int)
            for _c in ["단가(원)","재고금액(원)","리드타임(일)"]:
                if _c in _out.columns:
                    _out[_c] = pd.to_numeric(_out[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
            _out = _out.sort_values(["재고금액(원)", "총수량", "수리부속"], ascending=[False, False, True]).reset_index(drop=True)
        return _out

    def _xai_inventory_xlsx_b64(_df: pd.DataFrame, _sheet: str) -> str:
        if not isinstance(_df, pd.DataFrame) or _df.empty:
            return ""
        _buf = io.BytesIO()
        with pd.ExcelWriter(_buf, engine="openpyxl") as _writer:
            _strip_stage_columns_for_user(_df).to_excel(_writer, sheet_name=str(_sheet)[:31], index=False)
            _ws = _writer.book[str(_sheet)[:31]]
            _ws.freeze_panes = "A2"
            _ws.auto_filter.ref = _ws.dimensions
            _widths = {1:10,2:28,3:20,4:10,5:15,6:17,7:14,8:10,9:10}
            for _i,_w in _widths.items():
                _ws.column_dimensions[chr(64+_i)].width = _w
        return base64.b64encode(_buf.getvalue()).decode("ascii")

    inv_ap = _xai_apply_whatif_inventory(inv_a, "A", "A+")
    inv_bp = _xai_apply_whatif_inventory(inv_b, "B", "B+")

    # V32 · A/B CORE full spare-parts inventory scroll panels
    def _xai_core_inventory_table(_df: pd.DataFrame, _side: str) -> str:
        _side = str(_side).upper()
        if not isinstance(_df, pd.DataFrame) or _df.empty:
            return f'<div class="core-inv-empty">{_side} 수리부속 목록이 없습니다.</div>'
        _rows = []
        for _, _r in _df.iterrows():
            _pid = html.escape(str(_r.get("Part_ID", "") or ""), quote=True)
            _nm = html.escape(str(_r.get("수리부속", "") or ""), quote=True)
            _qty = int(pd.to_numeric(_r.get("총수량", 0), errors="coerce") or 0)
            _unit = float(pd.to_numeric(_r.get("단가(원)", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
            _value = float(pd.to_numeric(_r.get("재고금액(원)", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
            _lead = float(pd.to_numeric(_r.get("리드타임(일)", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
            _rows.append(
                '<tr>'
                f'<td class="mono">{_pid}</td><td class="name">{_nm}</td><td class="qty">{_qty}</td>'
                f'<td class="money">{_xai_compact_money(_unit)}</td><td class="money">{_xai_compact_money(_value)}</td><td>{_lead:.0f}일</td>'
                '</tr>'
            )
        _total_value = float(pd.to_numeric(_df.get("재고금액(원)", pd.Series(dtype=float)), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).sum())
        _total_qty = int(pd.to_numeric(_df.get("총수량", pd.Series(dtype=float)), errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum())
        _title_cls = 'a' if _side == 'A' else 'b'
        return (
            f'<div class="core-inv-card {_title_cls}">'
            f'<div class="core-inv-head"><div><span>{_side} SPARE LIST</span><b>{len(_df):,}종 · {_total_qty:,}개</b></div>'
            f'<small>총 {_xai_compact_money(_total_value)}</small></div>'
            '<div class="core-inv-scroll"><table><thead><tr>'
            '<th>Part ID</th><th>수리부속</th><th>총수량</th><th>단가</th><th>재고금액</th><th>리드타임</th>'
            '</tr></thead><tbody>' + ''.join(_rows) + '</tbody></table></div></div>'
        )

    _core_inventory_html = (
        '<div class="core-inventory"><div class="core-inventory-title"><div><span>SPARE PARTS INVENTORY</span><b>A / B 전체 수리부속 목록</b></div>'
        '<small>스크롤하여 전체 품목 확인</small></div><div class="core-inventory-grid">'
        + _xai_core_inventory_table(inv_a, "A") + _xai_core_inventory_table(inv_b, "B") + '</div></div>'
    )

    cost_a = float(_xai_num(A.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
    cost_b = float(_xai_num(B.get("stock_cost", 0.0)))
    ao_a = _xai_pct(A.get("mean_Ao", A.get("nominal_Ao", 0.0)))
    ao_b = _xai_pct(B.get("mean_Ao", B.get("nominal_Ao", 0.0)))
    risk_a = float(_xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_events", A.get("mean_shortage_units", 0.0))))
    risk_b = float(_xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_events", B.get("mean_shortage_units", 0.0))))
    rate_a = float(_xai_num(A.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0)))
    rate_b = float(_xai_num(B.get("mean_shortage_rate", 0.0)))
    wait_a = float(_xai_num(A.get("mean_DT_wait_h", 0.0)))
    wait_b = float(_xai_num(B.get("mean_DT_wait_h", 0.0)))
    items_a = int(len(inv_a)); items_b = int(len(inv_b))
    units_a = int(pd.to_numeric(inv_a.get("총수량", pd.Series(dtype=float)), errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum()) if not inv_a.empty else 0
    units_b = int(pd.to_numeric(inv_b.get("총수량", pd.Series(dtype=float)), errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum()) if not inv_b.empty else 0

    # V15 · A/B CORE spare risk/cost distribution (display only).
    # The optimization/Monte Carlo logic is untouched. This layer only explains
    # what kind of spare-parts each A/B policy actually holds.
    core_risk_rows = []
    try:
        _meta_all, _vec_all = _build_frontend_policy_payload(result)
        _va = _vec_all.get(a_key, {}) if isinstance(_vec_all, dict) else {}
        _vb = _vec_all.get(b_key, {}) if isinstance(_vec_all, dict) else {}
        _aO, _aI, _aD = list(_va.get("sO", []) or []), list(_va.get("sI", []) or []), list(_va.get("sD", []) or [])
        _bO, _bI, _bD = list(_vb.get("sO", []) or []), list(_vb.get("sI", []) or []), list(_vb.get("sD", []) or [])
        _analysis_years = max(float(_xai_num((result.get("_engine_context", {}) or {}).get("analysis_years", 1.0))), 1e-9)
        for _i, _m in enumerate(_meta_all if isinstance(_meta_all, list) else []):
            def _q(_arr):
                try: return int(_arr[_i]) if _i < len(_arr) else 0
                except Exception: return 0
            _aq = _q(_aO) + _q(_aI) + _q(_aD)
            _bq = _q(_bO) + _q(_bI) + _q(_bD)
            if _aq <= 0 and _bq <= 0:
                continue
            _unit = float(pd.to_numeric(_m.get("proc_cost", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)
            _fr = max(0.0, float(pd.to_numeric(_m.get("failure_rate", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0))
            _cm = max(0.0, float(pd.to_numeric(_m.get("cm_cost", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0))
            _maint_expected = _fr * _cm * _analysis_years
            _lead_d = max(0.0, float(pd.to_numeric(_m.get("lead_time", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0) / 24.0)
            _name = str(_m.get("item_name", _m.get("category", _m.get("part_id", "수리부속"))) or "수리부속").strip()
            core_risk_rows.append({
                "name": _name,
                "cand_idx": _i,
                "part_id": str(_m.get("part_id", "") or ""),
                "fr": _fr,
                "lead": _lead_d,
                "unit": _unit,
                "Aq": _aq,
                "Bq": _bq,
                "Aval": _unit * _aq,
                "Bval": _unit * _bq,
                "Amaint": _maint_expected if _aq > 0 else 0.0,
                "Bmaint": _maint_expected if _bq > 0 else 0.0,
                "owner": "AB" if (_aq > 0 and _bq > 0) else ("A" if _aq > 0 else "B"),
            })
    except Exception:
        core_risk_rows = []

    _crdf = pd.DataFrame(core_risk_rows)
    if not _crdf.empty:
        for _c in ["fr","lead","unit","Aq","Bq","Aval","Bval","Amaint","Bmaint"]:
            _crdf[_c] = pd.to_numeric(_crdf[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        _fr_pos = _crdf.loc[_crdf["fr"] > 0, "fr"]
        _ld_pos = _crdf.loc[_crdf["lead"] > 0, "lead"]
        _fr75 = float(_fr_pos.quantile(.75)) if len(_fr_pos) else 0.0
        _ld75 = float(_ld_pos.quantile(.75)) if len(_ld_pos) else 0.0
        _fr_min, _fr_max = float(_crdf["fr"].min()), float(_crdf["fr"].max())
        _ld_min, _ld_max = float(_crdf["lead"].min()), float(_crdf["lead"].max())
        _fx = np.log1p(_crdf["fr"].clip(lower=0.0))
        _ly = np.log1p(_crdf["lead"].clip(lower=0.0))
        _fx0, _fx1 = float(_fx.min()), float(_fx.max())
        _ly0, _ly1 = float(_ly.min()), float(_ly.max())
        _v = np.log1p((_crdf[["Aval","Bval"]].max(axis=1)).clip(lower=0.0))
        _v0, _v1 = float(_v.min()), float(_v.max())
        _crdf["x"] = 82.0 + ( _fx - _fx0 ) / max(_fx1 - _fx0, 1e-12) * 816.0
        _crdf["y"] = 292.0 - ( _ly - _ly0 ) / max(_ly1 - _ly0, 1e-12) * 224.0
        _crdf["r"] = 5.0 + ( _v - _v0 ) / max(_v1 - _v0, 1e-12) * 7.5
        _crdf["high"] = (_crdf["fr"] >= _fr75) & (_crdf["lead"] >= _ld75)
        _crdf["risk_score"] = (
            (_fx - _fx0) / max(_fx1 - _fx0, 1e-12) * .52
            + (_ly - _ly0) / max(_ly1 - _ly0, 1e-12) * .38
            + (_v - _v0) / max(_v1 - _v0, 1e-12) * .10
        )
        _crdf = _crdf.sort_values(["risk_score","fr","lead"], ascending=[False,False,False]).reset_index(drop=True)
        _fr75x = 82.0 + (np.log1p(max(_fr75,0.0)) - _fx0) / max(_fx1 - _fx0, 1e-12) * 816.0
        _ld75y = 292.0 - (np.log1p(max(_ld75,0.0)) - _ly0) / max(_ly1 - _ly0, 1e-12) * 224.0
    else:
        _fr75 = _ld75 = _fr_min = _fr_max = _ld_min = _ld_max = 0.0
        _fr75x, _ld75y = 694.0, 124.0

    def _cr_num(v: float) -> str:
        v = float(v or 0.0)
        if abs(v) >= 1000: return f"{v:,.0f}"
        if abs(v) >= 10: return f"{v:.1f}"
        if abs(v) >= 1: return f"{v:.2f}"
        if abs(v) >= .01: return f"{v:.3f}"
        return f"{v:.4f}"

    _cr_nodes = []
    _cr_counts = {"A":0,"AB":0,"B":0,"HA":0,"HAB":0,"HB":0}
    if not _crdf.empty:
        for _j, _r in _crdf.iterrows():
            _own = str(_r.get("owner","AB"))
            _high = bool(_r.get("high", False))
            _cr_counts[_own] = _cr_counts.get(_own,0) + 1
            if _high: _cr_counts["H"+_own] = _cr_counts.get("H"+_own,0) + 1
            _nm = html.escape(str(_r.get("name","수리부속")), quote=True)
            _pid = html.escape(str(_r.get("part_id","")), quote=True)
            _x, _y, _rr = float(_r["x"]), float(_r["y"]), float(_r["r"])
            _cls = "a" if _own == "A" else ("b" if _own == "B" else "ab")
            _halo = f'<circle class="cr-halo" cx="{_x:.1f}" cy="{_y:.1f}" r="{_rr+6:.1f}" />' if _high else ''
            if _own == "AB":
                _body = (f'<circle class="cr-shadow" cx="{_x+2:.1f}" cy="{_y+3:.1f}" r="{_rr:.1f}" />'
                         f'<circle class="cr-dot cr-a" cx="{_x:.1f}" cy="{_y:.1f}" r="{_rr:.1f}" />'
                         f'<circle class="cr-ring-b" cx="{_x:.1f}" cy="{_y:.1f}" r="{_rr+2.4:.1f}" />')
            else:
                _body = (f'<circle class="cr-shadow" cx="{_x+2:.1f}" cy="{_y+3:.1f}" r="{_rr:.1f}" />'
                         f'<circle class="cr-dot cr-{_cls}" cx="{_x:.1f}" cy="{_y:.1f}" r="{_rr:.1f}" />')
            _cr_nodes.append(
                f'<g class="cr-point {_cls}{" high" if _high else ""}" data-name="{_nm}" data-part="{_pid}" '
                f'data-fr="{float(_r["fr"]):.8g}" data-lead="{float(_r["lead"]):.6g}" '
                f'data-a="{int(_r["Aq"])}" data-b="{int(_r["Bq"])}" data-aval="{float(_r["Aval"]):.0f}" data-bval="{float(_r["Bval"]):.0f}" '
                f'tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="{_nm}">{_halo}{_body}</g>'
            )
    core_risk_nodes_html = ''.join(_cr_nodes) if _cr_nodes else '<text x="490" y="180" text-anchor="middle" fill="#66899a" font-size="11">수리부속 데이터 없음</text>'
    core_risk_summary = f'A만 {_cr_counts.get("A",0)} · 공통 {_cr_counts.get("AB",0)} · B만 {_cr_counts.get("B",0)}'
    core_risk_high_summary = f'A {_cr_counts.get("HA",0)} · 공통 {_cr_counts.get("HAB",0)} · B {_cr_counts.get("HB",0)}'
    cost_max = max(cost_a, cost_b, 1.0)
    cost_pa = max(8.0, min(100.0, 100.0 * cost_a / cost_max))
    cost_pb = max(8.0, min(100.0, 100.0 * cost_b / cost_max))
    ao_pa = max(0.0, min(100.0, ao_a)); ao_pb = max(0.0, min(100.0, ao_b))
    defend_a = max(0.0, min(100.0, (1.0 - rate_a) * 100.0))
    defend_b = max(0.0, min(100.0, (1.0 - rate_b) * 100.0))
    nominal_ao_a = _xai_pct(A.get("nominal_Ao", A.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)))
    nominal_ao_b = _xai_pct(B.get("nominal_Ao", B.get("mean_Ao", 0.0)))

    sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario or [])
    shield_stages = []
    if not sdf.empty:
        for c in ["total_failures","A_Ao","B_Ao","A_short","B_short","A_short_rate","B_short_rate","A_DT","B_DT"]:
            if c not in sdf.columns: sdf[c] = 0.0
            sdf[c] = pd.to_numeric(sdf[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        sdf = sdf.sort_values("total_failures").reset_index(drop=True)
        qs = [0.10,0.25,0.40,0.55,0.70,0.82,0.90,0.95,0.98,0.995]
        labels = ["여유","여유","보통","보통","주의","주의","불리","불리","매우 불리","매우 불리"]
        for q, lab in zip(qs, labels):
            ix = min(len(sdf)-1, max(0, int(round(q*(len(sdf)-1)))))
            r = sdf.iloc[ix]
            aa = _xai_pct(r.get("A_Ao", ao_a)); bb = _xai_pct(r.get("B_Ao", ao_b))
            ar = float(r.get("A_short_rate", rate_a)); br = float(r.get("B_short_rate", rate_b))
            shield_stages.append({"label":lab,"failures":float(r.get("total_failures",0.0)),"Aao":aa,"Bao":bb,"Adef":max(0.0,min(100.0,(1.0-ar)*100.0)),"Bdef":max(0.0,min(100.0,(1.0-br)*100.0)),"Ashort":float(r.get("A_short",0.0)),"Bshort":float(r.get("B_short",0.0)),"Adt":float(r.get("A_DT",0.0)),"Bdt":float(r.get("B_DT",0.0))})
    if not shield_stages:
        shield_stages = [{"label":"현재","failures":0.0,"Aao":ao_a,"Bao":ao_b,"Adef":defend_a,"Bdef":defend_b,"Ashort":risk_a,"Bshort":risk_b,"Adt":wait_a,"Bdt":wait_b}]

    nodes=[]
    work = idf.copy() if isinstance(idf, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
    if not work.empty:
        for c in ["risk_diff_events","demand_events","stock_diff","unit_cost","A_qty","B_qty","A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events"]:
            if c not in work.columns: work[c]=0.0
            work[c]=pd.to_numeric(work[c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        work["_att"] = work["risk_diff_events"].abs()
        work = work.sort_values(["_att","demand_events"], ascending=[False,False]).head(18).reset_index(drop=True)
        mx_att=max(float(work["_att"].max()),1.0); mx_dem=max(float(work["demand_events"].max()),1.0)
        for i,r in work.iterrows():
            angle = i * 2.399963229728653
            rad = 10.0 + 3.35 * i
            x = 50.0 + min(40.0,rad) * math.cos(angle)
            y = 50.0 + min(38.0,rad*0.82) * math.sin(angle)
            diff=float(r.get("risk_diff_events",0.0)); dem=float(r.get("demand_events",0.0)); stock=float(r.get("stock_diff",0.0))
            size=10.0+18.0*abs(diff)/mx_att
            bright=0.35+0.65*dem/mx_dem
            owner="A" if diff>0 else ("B" if diff<0 else "=")
            nodes.append({"name":str(r.get("display_name","수리부속")),"x":x,"y":y,"size":size,"bright":bright,"owner":owner,"diff":diff,"stock":stock,"demand":dem})

    node_html=[]
    for n in nodes:
        cls = 'A' if n['owner']=='A' else ('B' if n['owner']=='B' else 'eq')
        halo = ' halo' if abs(n['stock']) > 0.1 else ''
        node_html.append(f'<button class="node {cls}{halo}" style="left:{n["x"]:.2f}%;top:{n["y"]:.2f}%;width:{n["size"]:.1f}px;height:{n["size"]:.1f}px;opacity:{n["bright"]:.2f};border:0" data-name="{html.escape(n["name"], quote=True)}" data-demand="{n["demand"]:.0f}" data-diff="{abs(n["diff"]):.0f}" data-stock="{abs(n["stock"]):.0f}" title="{html.escape(n["name"], quote=True)}"></button>')
    if not node_html:
        node_html=['<div style="position:absolute;inset:0;display:grid;place-items:center;color:#688696;font-size:11px">품목별 Monte Carlo 근거가 연결되면 Attention Map이 표시됩니다.</div>']

    def _vuln_html(side: str) -> str:
        if work.empty:
            return '<span class="vuln-empty">품목별 부족근거 대기</span>'
        col = "A_shortage_events" if side == "A" else "B_shortage_events"
        t = work.sort_values([col, "demand_events"], ascending=[False, False]).head(4)
        rows=[]
        for _, rr in t.iterrows():
            ev=float(rr.get(col,0.0))
            if ev <= 0: continue
            nm=html.escape(str(rr.get("display_name","수리부속")))
            rows.append(f'<span class="vuln-chip">{nm}<b>{ev:.0f}</b></span>')
        return ''.join(rows) if rows else '<span class="vuln-empty">뚜렷한 부족 노출 없음</span>'
    def _vuln_items(side: str, limit: int) -> list[dict]:
        if work.empty:
            return []
        col = "A_shortage_events" if side == "A" else "B_shortage_events"
        t = work.sort_values([col, "demand_events"], ascending=[False, False]).head(max(1, int(limit)))
        rows=[]
        for _, rr in t.iterrows():
            ev=float(rr.get(col,0.0))
            if ev <= 0: continue
            rows.append({"name": str(rr.get("display_name","수리부속")), "value": round(ev,1)})
        return rows

    def _vuln_html(side: str) -> str:
        items = _vuln_items(side, 4)
        if not items:
            return '<span class="vuln-empty">뚜렷한 부족 노출 없음</span>'
        return ''.join([f'<span class="vuln-chip">{html.escape(x["name"])}<b>{x["value"]:.0f}</b></span>' for x in items])
    vuln_a_html = _vuln_html("A")
    vuln_b_html = _vuln_html("B")

    if shield_stages:
        _den = max(len(shield_stages)-1, 1)
        for _i, _st in enumerate(shield_stages):
            _cnt = min(8, 2 + int(round((_i / _den) * 6)))
            _st["Aitems"] = _vuln_items("A", _cnt)
            _st["Bitems"] = _vuln_items("B", _cnt)
    else:
        shield_stages = [{"label":"현재","failures":0.0,"Aao":ao_a,"Bao":ao_b,"Adef":defend_a,"Bdef":defend_b,"Ashort":risk_a,"Bshort":risk_b,"Adt":wait_a,"Bdt":wait_b,"Aitems":_vuln_items("A",4),"Bitems":_vuln_items("B",4)}]

    short_max=max(units_a,units_b,1.0)
    short_pa=max(4.0,min(100.0,100.0*units_a/short_max))
    short_pb=max(4.0,min(100.0,100.0*units_b/short_max))

    def half_pair(a,b):
        m=max(float(a),float(b),1e-9); return 48.0*a/m,48.0*b/m
    cl,cr=half_pair(cost_a,cost_b); al,ar=half_pair(ao_a,ao_b); sl,sr=half_pair(units_a,units_b)

    cost_gap_eok_abs = abs(cost_b - cost_a) / 1.0e8
    cost_delta_text = (f"B가 {cost_gap_eok_abs:.2f}억원 더 큼" if cost_b > cost_a else (f"A가 {cost_gap_eok_abs:.2f}억원 더 큼" if cost_a > cost_b else "비용 차이 거의 없음"))
    ao_gap_abs = abs(ao_b - ao_a)
    ao_delta_text = (f"B가 {ao_gap_abs:.2f}%p 더 높음" if ao_b > ao_a else (f"A가 {ao_gap_abs:.2f}%p 더 높음" if ao_a > ao_b else "Ao 차이 거의 없음"))
    unit_gap_abs = abs(units_b - units_a)
    short_delta_text = (f"B가 {unit_gap_abs:,}개 더 많음" if units_b > units_a else (f"A가 {unit_gap_abs:,}개 더 많음" if units_a > units_b else "수리부속 개수 동일"))


    # ---- STAGE 2 · COST INFORM (display only; no engine logic changes) ----
    # Direction is always from the lower-cost policy to the higher-cost policy.
    if cost_a <= cost_b:
        low_label, high_label = "A", "B"
        low_cost, high_cost = cost_a, cost_b
        low_units, high_units = units_a, units_b
        low_risk, high_risk = risk_a, risk_b
        low_wait, high_wait = wait_a, wait_b
        low_ao, high_ao = ao_a, ao_b
    else:
        low_label, high_label = "B", "A"
        low_cost, high_cost = cost_b, cost_a
        low_units, high_units = units_b, units_a
        low_risk, high_risk = risk_b, risk_a
        low_wait, high_wait = wait_b, wait_a
        low_ao, high_ao = ao_b, ao_a

    cost_gap = max(0.0, high_cost - low_cost)
    cost_gap_eok = cost_gap / 1.0e8 if cost_gap > 0 else 0.0
    stock_delta = int(high_units - low_units)
    risk_reduction = float(low_risk - high_risk)  # positive = higher-cost policy reduces shortage
    wait_reduction = float(low_wait - high_wait)  # positive = higher-cost policy reduces waiting
    ao_gain = float(high_ao - low_ao)              # positive = higher-cost policy raises Ao

    # Visual energy intensity uses only observed A/B deltas, normalized within each metric to A/B scale.
    risk_base = max(abs(low_risk), abs(high_risk), 1.0)
    wait_base = max(abs(low_wait), abs(high_wait), 1.0)
    ao_base = max(abs(low_ao), abs(high_ao), 1.0)
    flow_risk = min(100.0, abs(risk_reduction) / risk_base * 100.0)
    flow_wait = min(100.0, abs(wait_reduction) / wait_base * 100.0)
    flow_ao = min(100.0, abs(ao_gain) / ao_base * 100.0)
    flow_stock = min(100.0, abs(stock_delta) / max(high_units, low_units, 1) * 100.0)

    # What-if links COST INTELLIGENCE to the existing verified 10,000-run re-simulation.
    wr_now = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_result")
    wf_active = isinstance(wr_now, dict)
    wf_base = str(wr_now.get("base_label", "A")).upper() if wf_active else "A"
    wf_add_cost = float(_xai_num(wr_now.get("additional_cost", 0.0))) if wf_active else 0.0
    wf_before_short = float(_xai_num((wr_now.get("before", {}) or {}).get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0))) if wf_active else 0.0
    wf_after_short = float(_xai_num((wr_now.get("after", {}) or {}).get("mean_shortage_events", 0.0))) if wf_active else 0.0
    wf_reinf_cost = float(_xai_num(wr_now.get("reinforced_stock_cost", 0.0))) if wf_active else 0.0
    if wf_active and wf_reinf_cost <= 0:
        wf_reinf_cost = (cost_a if wf_base == "A" else cost_b) + wf_add_cost
    wf_other_cost = cost_b if wf_base == "A" else cost_a
    wf_other_risk = risk_b if wf_base == "A" else risk_a
    wf_base_cost = cost_a if wf_base == "A" else cost_b
    wf_base_risk = risk_a if wf_base == "A" else risk_b

    full_cost_gap = abs(wf_other_cost - wf_base_cost)
    full_risk_gap = wf_base_risk - wf_other_risk  # positive when the other policy has lower shortage
    wf_cost_used_pct = (wf_add_cost / full_cost_gap * 100.0) if wf_active and full_cost_gap > 1e-9 else 0.0
    wf_risk_recovery_pct = ((wf_base_risk - wf_after_short) / full_risk_gap * 100.0) if wf_active and full_risk_gap > 1e-9 else 0.0
    wf_cost_used_pct = max(0.0, min(160.0, wf_cost_used_pct))
    wf_risk_recovery_pct = max(-60.0, min(160.0, wf_risk_recovery_pct))
    wf_saving = (wf_other_cost - wf_reinf_cost) if wf_active else 0.0

    # Verified-history inefficiency scanner: find the largest observed drop in marginal
    # risk-recovery per added cost. No fixed score/threshold is introduced.
    hist = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_history", [])
    hist = hist if isinstance(hist, list) else []
    hist_pts = []
    for h in hist:
        if not isinstance(h, dict):
            continue
        try:
            hb = str(h.get("base_label", "A")).upper()
            hbase_cost = cost_a if hb == "A" else cost_b
            hbase_risk = risk_a if hb == "A" else risk_b
            hother_risk = risk_b if hb == "A" else risk_a
            hc = float(_xai_num(h.get("additional_cost", 0.0)))
