            hr = float(_xai_num(h.get("after_shortage", hbase_risk)))
            gap = hbase_risk - hother_risk
            rec = ((hbase_risk - hr) / gap * 100.0) if gap > 1e-9 else 0.0
            if hc >= 0:
                hist_pts.append({"cost": hc, "recovery": rec, "label": str(h.get("short_label", "보강안"))})
        except Exception:
            pass
    hist_pts = sorted(hist_pts, key=lambda z: z["cost"])
    ineff_label = "검증점 추가 필요"
    ineff_cost = None
    if len(hist_pts) >= 3:
        slopes=[]
        for i in range(1, len(hist_pts)):
            dc = hist_pts[i]["cost"] - hist_pts[i-1]["cost"]
            dr = hist_pts[i]["recovery"] - hist_pts[i-1]["recovery"]
            slopes.append((dr/dc if dc > 1e-9 else float('inf'), i))
        drops=[]
        for j in range(1, len(slopes)):
            if slopes[j-1][0] != float('inf') and slopes[j][0] != float('inf'):
                drops.append((slopes[j-1][0]-slopes[j][0], slopes[j][1]))
        if drops:
            _, di = max(drops, key=lambda x: x[0])
            ineff_cost = hist_pts[di]["cost"]
            ineff_label = "효율 둔화 지점"

    # Compact labels for the visual layer.
    flow_cost_label = _xai_compact_money(cost_gap)
    wf_saving_label = _xai_compact_money(abs(wf_saving)) if wf_active else "-"
    ineff_cost_label = _xai_compact_money(ineff_cost) if ineff_cost is not None else "-"
    # 02 RISK FLOW에 실제 What-if 결과를 연결함.
    whatif_flow = []
    flow_base_label = ""
    _wr_flow = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_result")
    if isinstance(_wr_flow, dict) and isinstance(_wr_flow.get("scenario_df"), pd.DataFrame) and not sdf.empty:
        try:
            _wdf = _wr_flow.get("scenario_df").copy()
            _wdf["simulation_no"] = pd.to_numeric(_wdf.get("simulation_no"), errors="coerce")
            _wdf["After_Ao"] = pd.to_numeric(_wdf.get("After_Ao"), errors="coerce")
            _wdf["After_shortage_events"] = pd.to_numeric(_wdf.get("After_shortage_events"), errors="coerce")
            _base = sdf.copy()
            if "n" in _base.columns:
                _base["n"] = pd.to_numeric(_base["n"], errors="coerce")
                _m = _base.merge(_wdf[["simulation_no","After_Ao","After_shortage_events"]], left_on="n", right_on="simulation_no", how="inner")
                if not _m.empty:
                    _m = _m.sort_values("total_failures").reset_index(drop=True)
                    for _q in [0.10,0.25,0.40,0.55,0.70,0.82,0.90,0.95,0.98,0.995]:
                        _ix=min(len(_m)-1,max(0,int(round(_q*(len(_m)-1)))))
                        _r=_m.iloc[_ix]
                        _ao=float(_r.get("After_Ao",0.0))
                        if abs(_ao)<=1.5: _ao*=100.0
                        whatif_flow.append({"ao":_ao,"short":float(_r.get("After_shortage_events",0.0))})
                    flow_base_label=str(_wr_flow.get("base_label","A") or "A").upper()
        except Exception:
            whatif_flow=[]
            flow_base_label=""

    # 품목별 시나리오 이력은 현재 엔진에 없으므로 정확한 '부족 시점'을 만들지 않음.
    # 대신 A/B에서 관측된 미래수요와 부족차이를 이용한 AI 예상 점검구간만 표시함.
    flow_candidates=[]
    if not work.empty and shield_stages:
        _mx_dem=max(float(work["demand_events"].max()),1.0)
        for _,_rr in work.sort_values(["_att","demand_events"],ascending=[False,False]).head(5).iterrows():
            _dem=float(_rr.get("demand_events",0.0)); _imp=abs(float(_rr.get("risk_diff_events",0.0)))
            _stage=int(round((_dem/_mx_dem)*(len(shield_stages)-1)))
            flow_candidates.append({"name":str(_rr.get("display_name","수리부속")),"stage":max(0,min(len(shield_stages)-1,_stage)),"demand":_dem,"diff":_imp})

    # ---- V8 · SPARE DIFFERENCE ----
    diff_df = idf.copy() if isinstance(idf, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
    if not diff_df.empty:
        for _c in ["A_qty","B_qty","unit_cost","lead_time_h","demand_events"]:
            if _c not in diff_df.columns:
                diff_df[_c] = 0.0
            diff_df[_c] = pd.to_numeric(diff_df[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        if "display_name" not in diff_df.columns:
            diff_df["display_name"] = diff_df.get("part_id", pd.Series(["수리부속"]*len(diff_df))).astype(str)
        diff_df = diff_df[(diff_df["A_qty"] > 0) | (diff_df["B_qty"] > 0)].copy()
        diff_df["delta_qty"] = diff_df["A_qty"] - diff_df["B_qty"]
        diff_df["abs_delta_qty"] = diff_df["delta_qty"].abs()
        diff_df["group"] = np.select([
            (diff_df["A_qty"] > 0) & (diff_df["B_qty"] <= 0),
            (diff_df["A_qty"] <= 0) & (diff_df["B_qty"] > 0),
            (diff_df["A_qty"] > 0) & (diff_df["B_qty"] > 0) & (diff_df["A_qty"] != diff_df["B_qty"]),
        ], ["A_ONLY","B_ONLY","QTY_DIFF"], default="SAME")
    else:
        diff_df = pd.DataFrame(columns=["display_name","A_qty","B_qty","unit_cost","lead_time_h","demand_events","delta_qty","abs_delta_qty","group"])

    a_only_df = diff_df[diff_df["group"] == "A_ONLY"].sort_values(["abs_delta_qty","unit_cost"], ascending=False)
    b_only_df = diff_df[diff_df["group"] == "B_ONLY"].sort_values(["abs_delta_qty","unit_cost"], ascending=False)
    qty_diff_df = diff_df[diff_df["group"] == "QTY_DIFF"].sort_values(["abs_delta_qty","unit_cost"], ascending=False)

    def _spare_node_html(row, group, max_delta):
        nm = html.escape(str(row.get("display_name","수리부속")), quote=True)
        aq = int(round(float(row.get("A_qty",0))))
        bq = int(round(float(row.get("B_qty",0))))
        dq = aq - bq
        uc = float(row.get("unit_cost",0.0) or 0.0)
        ld = float(row.get("lead_time_h",0.0) or 0.0) / 24.0
        glow = 0.16 + 0.84 * min(1.0, abs(dq)/max(max_delta,1.0))
        a_pct = 100.0 * aq / max(aq,bq,1)
        b_pct = 100.0 * bq / max(aq,bq,1)
        sign = f"+{dq}" if dq > 0 else str(dq)
        return f'<button type="button" class="sd-node {group.lower()}" style="--glow:{glow:.2f}" data-name="{nm}" data-a="{aq}" data-b="{bq}" data-delta="{dq}" data-cost="{uc:.0f}" data-lead="{ld:.1f}"><span class="sd-name">{nm}</span><span class="sd-pair"><b class="sd-a">A {aq}</b><i>{sign}</i><b class="sd-b">B {bq}</b></span><span class="sd-bars"><em class="ba" style="width:{a_pct:.1f}%"></em><em class="bb" style="width:{b_pct:.1f}%"></em></span></button>'

    _mx = max(float(diff_df["abs_delta_qty"].max()) if not diff_df.empty else 1.0, 1.0)
    a_only_nodes = [_spare_node_html(r,"A_ONLY",_mx) for _,r in a_only_df.head(10).iterrows()] or ['<div class="sd-empty">없음</div>']
    qty_diff_nodes = [_spare_node_html(r,"QTY_DIFF",_mx) for _,r in qty_diff_df.head(14).iterrows()] or ['<div class="sd-empty">수량 차이 없음</div>']
    b_only_nodes = [_spare_node_html(r,"B_ONLY",_mx) for _,r in b_only_df.head(10).iterrows()] or ['<div class="sd-empty">없음</div>']
    a_more=max(0,len(a_only_df)-10); q_more=max(0,len(qty_diff_df)-14); b_more=max(0,len(b_only_df)-10)


    # ---- V10 · RISK COVERAGE GRAPH ----
    # Graph logic: future demand increase -> A/B shortage -> A/B Ao.
    # No artificial coverage score is used.
    rc_sdf = pd.DataFrame(scenario or [])
    if not rc_sdf.empty:
        _aliases = {"A_short":"A_shortage_events","B_short":"B_shortage_events","A_DT":"A_DT_wait_h","B_DT":"B_DT_wait_h"}
        for _dst,_src in _aliases.items():
            if _dst not in rc_sdf.columns and _src in rc_sdf.columns:
                rc_sdf[_dst] = rc_sdf[_src]
        for _c in ["total_failures","A_Ao","B_Ao","A_short","B_short"]:
            if _c not in rc_sdf.columns:
                rc_sdf[_c] = 0.0
            rc_sdf[_c] = pd.to_numeric(rc_sdf[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        if float(rc_sdf["A_Ao"].max()) <= 1.5: rc_sdf["A_Ao"] *= 100.0
        if float(rc_sdf["B_Ao"].max()) <= 1.5: rc_sdf["B_Ao"] *= 100.0
        _base_fail = max(float(rc_sdf["total_failures"].median()), 1e-9)
        rc_sdf["shock_pct"] = (rc_sdf["total_failures"] / _base_fail - 1.0) * 100.0
        rc_sdf = rc_sdf.sort_values(["total_failures"]).reset_index(drop=True)
        _bin_count = min(45, max(12, len(rc_sdf)//120 if len(rc_sdf) > 0 else 12))
        _rows=[]
        for _ch in np.array_split(rc_sdf, _bin_count):
            if _ch.empty: continue
            _m = _ch[["shock_pct","A_short","B_short","A_Ao","B_Ao","total_failures"]].median(numeric_only=True)
            _rows.append({
                "x":float(_m["shock_pct"]),
                "Ashort":float(_m["A_short"]),"Bshort":float(_m["B_short"]),
                "Aao":float(_m["A_Ao"]),"Bao":float(_m["B_Ao"]),
                "fail":float(_m["total_failures"]),
            })
        rc_points = _rows
    else:
        rc_points = []

    if not rc_points:
        rc_points = [{"x":0.0,"Ashort":risk_a,"Bshort":risk_b,"Aao":ao_a,"Bao":ao_b,"fail":0.0}]

    # The observed point where the A/B shortage gap expands fastest.
    _xs=np.asarray([p["x"] for p in rc_points],float)
    _gaps=np.asarray([abs(p["Ashort"]-p["Bshort"]) for p in rc_points],float)
    if len(rc_points) >= 3 and np.ptp(_xs) > 1e-9:
        _sl=np.gradient(_gaps,_xs)
        _bp_i=int(np.nanargmax(_sl))
    else:
        _bp_i=int(np.nanargmax(_gaps)) if len(_gaps) else 0
    _bp=rc_points[_bp_i]
    rc_break_x=float(_bp["x"])
    rc_break_gap=float(abs(_bp["Ashort"]-_bp["Bshort"]))

    # Item contribution to the observed A/B shortage-event difference.
    rc_items = idf.copy() if isinstance(idf,pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
    contrib_rows=[]
    if not rc_items.empty:
        for _c in ["risk_diff_events","A_qty","B_qty","lead_time_h","unit_cost","demand_events"]:
            if _c not in rc_items.columns: rc_items[_c]=0.0
            rc_items[_c]=pd.to_numeric(rc_items[_c],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        if "display_name" not in rc_items.columns:
            rc_items["display_name"] = rc_items.get("part_id",pd.Series(["수리부속"]*len(rc_items))).astype(str)
        rc_items["abs_gap"] = rc_items["risk_diff_events"].abs()
        _total_gap=max(float(rc_items["abs_gap"].sum()),1e-9)
        _tmp=rc_items[rc_items["abs_gap"]>0].sort_values(["abs_gap","demand_events"],ascending=False).head(7)
        for _,_r in _tmp.iterrows():
            _rd=float(_r.get("risk_diff_events",0.0))
            contrib_rows.append({
                "name":str(_r.get("display_name","수리부속")),
                "share":float(_r.get("abs_gap",0.0))/_total_gap*100.0,
                "gap":abs(_rd),
                "better":"A" if _rd>0 else ("B" if _rd<0 else "="),
                "Aq":int(round(float(_r.get("A_qty",0.0)))),
                "Bq":int(round(float(_r.get("B_qty",0.0)))),
                "lead":float(_r.get("lead_time_h",0.0))/24.0,
            })
    if not contrib_rows:
        contrib_rows=[{"name":"품목별 차이 없음","share":0.0,"gap":0.0,"better":"=","Aq":0,"Bq":0,"lead":0.0}]

    _max_share=max([r["share"] for r in contrib_rows]+[1.0])
    contrib_html=[]
    for _r in contrib_rows:
        _nm=html.escape(_r["name"],quote=True)
        _w=max(2.0,100.0*_r["share"]/_max_share)
        _cls="ca" if _r["better"]=="A" else ("cb" if _r["better"]=="B" else "ceq")
        contrib_html.append(f'<button class="rci {_cls}" type="button" data-name="{_nm}" data-share="{_r["share"]:.1f}" data-gap="{_r["gap"]:.0f}" data-better="{_r["better"]}" data-aq="{_r["Aq"]}" data-bq="{_r["Bq"]}" data-lead="{_r["lead"]:.1f}"><span class="rci-name">{_nm}</span><span class="rci-track"><i style="width:{_w:.1f}%"></i></span><b>{_r["share"]:.1f}%</b></button>')

    rc_points_json=json.dumps(rc_points,ensure_ascii=False)

    # ---- V13 · SPARE DISTRIBUTION COST AXIS / PSEUDO-3D ----
    _sd_dots=[]
    _sd_cost_max=max(float(pd.to_numeric(diff_df.get("unit_cost",0),errors="coerce").fillna(0).max()) if not diff_df.empty else 0.0,1.0)
    _sd_log_max=math.log1p(_sd_cost_max)
    def _sd_dot_rows(_df, _zone, _limit=90):
        _rows=[]
        if not isinstance(_df,pd.DataFrame) or _df.empty:
            return _rows
        _d=_df.copy().head(_limit)
        _base_y={"A":62.0,"O":116.0,"B":170.0}.get(_zone,116.0)
        for _i,(_, _r) in enumerate(_d.iterrows()):
            _nm=html.escape(str(_r.get("display_name","수리부속")),quote=True)
            _aq=int(round(float(_r.get("A_qty",0.0)))); _bq=int(round(float(_r.get("B_qty",0.0))))
            _uc=max(0.0,float(_r.get("unit_cost",0.0) or 0.0)); _ld=float(_r.get("lead_time_h",0.0) or 0.0)/24.0
            _qty=max(_aq,_bq,1); _dq=abs(_aq-_bq)
            _x=112.0 + 770.0*(math.log1p(_uc)/_sd_log_max if _sd_log_max>0 else 0.0)
            _z=min(20.0, 3.2*math.sqrt(_qty)+1.2*min(_dq,5))
            _j=((_i%5)-2)*2.4
            _cy=_base_y-_z+_j
            if _zone=="A": _fill="#59caff"; _stroke="#bceeff"; _cls="sd3d-a"
            elif _zone=="B": _fill="#ff9b61"; _stroke="#ffd0ad"; _cls="sd3d-b"
            else: _fill="#b9eaff" if _dq==0 else "#83d9ff"; _stroke="#eefbff"; _cls="sd3d-o"
            _r0=3.5+min(6.5,1.35*math.sqrt(_qty)+.35*min(_dq,5))
            _rows.append(f'<g class="sd3d-point {_cls}" data-name="{_nm}" data-a="{_aq}" data-b="{_bq}" data-cost="{_uc:.0f}" data-lead="{_ld:.1f}" tabindex="0"><line x1="{_x:.1f}" y1="{_base_y+9:.1f}" x2="{_x:.1f}" y2="{_cy:.1f}" stroke="{_fill}" stroke-opacity=".20"/><ellipse cx="{_x+5:.1f}" cy="{_base_y+8:.1f}" rx="{_r0*1.35:.1f}" ry="{max(2.0,_r0*.42):.1f}" fill="rgba(0,0,0,.28)"/><circle cx="{_x:.1f}" cy="{_cy:.1f}" r="{_r0:.1f}" fill="{_fill}" stroke="{_stroke}" stroke-width="1.1"><title>{_nm} · 단가 {int(_uc):,}원 · A {_aq} / B {_bq}</title></circle></g>')
        return _rows
    _both_df=diff_df[(diff_df["A_qty"]>0)&(diff_df["B_qty"]>0)].copy() if not diff_df.empty else pd.DataFrame()
    _sd_dots += _sd_dot_rows(a_only_df,"A",90)
    _sd_dots += _sd_dot_rows(_both_df.sort_values(["unit_cost","abs_delta_qty"],ascending=False),"O",110)
    _sd_dots += _sd_dot_rows(b_only_df,"B",90)
    sd_distribution_html="\n".join(_sd_dots)
    sd_overlap_count=int(len(_both_df))
    sd_cost_ticks=[0.0, math.expm1(_sd_log_max*.25), math.expm1(_sd_log_max*.50), math.expm1(_sd_log_max*.75), _sd_cost_max]

    # ---- V13 · DYNAMIC RISK ITEM POOL: A/B difference + common missed risk ----
    rc_pool=[]
    if not rc_items.empty:
        _pool=rc_items.copy()
        for _c in ["risk_diff_events","A_qty","B_qty","lead_time_h","demand_events","unit_cost","A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events"]:
            if _c not in _pool.columns: _pool[_c]=0.0
            _pool[_c]=pd.to_numeric(_pool[_c],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        _pool["abs_gap"]=_pool["risk_diff_events"].abs()
        _pool["common_short"]=_pool[["A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events"]].min(axis=1)
        _pool=_pool[(_pool["abs_gap"]>0)|(_pool["A_qty"]!=_pool["B_qty"])|(_pool["common_short"]>0)].copy()
        _max_dem=max(float(_pool["demand_events"].max()) if not _pool.empty else 0.0,1.0)
        _max_gap=max(float(_pool["abs_gap"].max()) if not _pool.empty else 0.0,1.0)
        _max_common=max(float(_pool["common_short"].max()) if not _pool.empty else 0.0,1.0)
        _npt=max(len(rc_points),2)
        _pool["priority"]=(.42*(_pool["abs_gap"]/_max_gap)+.33*(_pool["common_short"]/_max_common)+.25*(_pool["demand_events"]/_max_dem))
        _pool=_pool.sort_values(["priority","demand_events"],ascending=False).head(48)
        for _,_r in _pool.iterrows():
            _dem=float(_r.get("demand_events",0.0)); _gap=float(_r.get("abs_gap",0.0)); _rd=float(_r.get("risk_diff_events",0.0))
            _ash=float(_r.get("A_shortage_events",0.0)); _bsh=float(_r.get("B_shortage_events",0.0)); _common=min(_ash,_bsh)
            _aq=int(round(float(_r.get("A_qty",0.0)))); _bq=int(round(float(_r.get("B_qty",0.0))))
            _strength=.40*(_dem/_max_dem)+.34*(_gap/_max_gap)+.26*(_common/_max_common)
            _threshold=int(round((1.0-min(max(_strength,0.0),1.0))*(_npt-1)*.78))
            # A/B verified difference candidates retain the relative-stock + observed-shortage evidence.
            _candA=bool((_bq>_aq) and (_ash>_bsh))
            _candB=bool((_aq>_bq) and (_bsh>_ash))
            # Common candidate catches repeated shortage shared by both alternatives, even when A/B are similar.
            _candCommon=bool(_common>0)
            rc_pool.append({
                "name":str(_r.get("display_name","수리부속")),"demand":_dem,"gap":_gap,"riskDiff":_rd,
                "Ashort":_ash,"Bshort":_bsh,"commonRisk":_common,
                "Aq":_aq,"Bq":_bq,"lead":float(_r.get("lead_time_h",0.0))/24.0,
                "threshold":_threshold,"candA":_candA,"candB":_candB,"candCommon":_candCommon,
            })
    rc_item_pool_json=json.dumps(rc_pool,ensure_ascii=False)

    # V35 · 10,000개 미래 전체의 누적 부족 이벤트 요약.
    # 그래프의 A/B 부족은 고장수요 구간별 대표(중앙) 부족값이고, 아래 누적값은
    # item-level Monte Carlo shortage event를 10,000개 미래 전체에 걸쳐 합산한 값이다.
    if not rc_items.empty:
        _rc_a_cum=float(pd.to_numeric(rc_items.get("A_shortage_events",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).sum())
        _rc_b_cum=float(pd.to_numeric(rc_items.get("B_shortage_events",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).sum())
        _rc_common_cum=float(pd.concat([
            pd.to_numeric(rc_items.get("A_shortage_events",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0),
            pd.to_numeric(rc_items.get("B_shortage_events",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0),
        ],axis=1).min(axis=1).sum())
    else:
        _rc_a_cum=_rc_b_cum=_rc_common_cum=0.0

    # Existing verified Monte Carlo What-if result, if present, is shown inside RISK COVERAGE only as a result summary.
    _wf_verify = st.session_state.get("xai_whatif_result")
    if isinstance(_wf_verify, dict):
        _wf_before=_wf_verify.get("before",{}) if isinstance(_wf_verify.get("before"),dict) else {}
        _wf_after=_wf_verify.get("after",{}) if isinstance(_wf_verify.get("after"),dict) else {}
        _wf_base=str(_wf_verify.get("base_label","A") or "A").upper()
        _wf_extra=float(_xai_num(_wf_verify.get("additional_cost",0.0)))
        _wf_short_before=float(_xai_num(_wf_before.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))
        _wf_short_after=float(_xai_num(_wf_after.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))
        _wf_ao_before=_xai_pct(_wf_before.get("mean_Ao",0.0)); _wf_ao_after=_xai_pct(_wf_after.get("mean_Ao",0.0))
        wf_verify_payload={"active":True,"base":_wf_base,"cost":_wf_extra,"shortDelta":_wf_short_after-_wf_short_before,"aoDelta":_wf_ao_after-_wf_ao_before}
    else:
        wf_verify_payload={"active":False}

    # ---- V16 · A/B CORE local tab 01: cost difference by meaningful spare-risk views ----
    core_cost_rows=[]
    if not _crdf.empty:
        _cc=_crdf.copy()
        _cc["Avalue"]=pd.to_numeric(_cc.get("Aval",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        _cc["Bvalue"]=pd.to_numeric(_cc.get("Bval",0.0),errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        # V24: use only plain, directly understandable stock-cost views.
        # Avoid abstract labels such as core-risk/top-risk cost in A/B CORE.
        # V25: long-lead cost follows the dashboard's active "장기 조달기간 기준".
        # _crdf["lead"] is stored in days, while the dashboard threshold is hours.
        _active_long_lead_h = float(globals().get("long_lead_threshold_h", 8760.0) or 8760.0)
        _long_lead_mask = (pd.to_numeric(_cc.get("lead", 0.0), errors="coerce").fillna(0.0) * 24.0) >= _active_long_lead_h
        # Failure-rate TOP10 is selected from the full candidate universe, then
        # A/B stock value is summed only where those TOP10 items are actually held.
        _top10_idx = set()
        try:
            _rank = sorted(
                [(int(i), float(pd.to_numeric(m.get("failure_rate", 0.0), errors="coerce") or 0.0)) for i,m in enumerate(_meta_all if isinstance(_meta_all,list) else [])],
                key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
            )[:10]
            _top10_idx = {i for i,v in _rank if v > 0}
        except Exception:
            _top10_idx = set()
        _top10_mask = _cc.get("cand_idx", pd.Series(index=_cc.index,dtype=float)).isin(_top10_idx)

        # V37: cost views are stock-investment only.
        # Exclusive holding cost = items stocked by only one of A/B.
        _a_only_mask = (_cc["Avalue"] > 0.0) & (_cc["Bvalue"] <= 0.0)
        _b_only_mask = (_cc["Bvalue"] > 0.0) & (_cc["Avalue"] <= 0.0)

        _metrics=[
            ("총 재고비용", pd.Series(True,index=_cc.index), "stock"),
            ("고장률 TOP10 품목 비용", _top10_mask, "stock"),
            ("장납기 품목 비용", _long_lead_mask, "stock"),
        ]
        for _label,_mask,_kind in _metrics:
            _mask=pd.Series(_mask,index=_cc.index).fillna(False).astype(bool)
            _av=float(_cc.loc[_mask,"Avalue"].sum()); _bv=float(_cc.loc[_mask,"Bvalue"].sum())
            core_cost_rows.append({"label":_label,"A":_av,"B":_bv,"delta":_bv-_av})

        # For exclusive holdings, each side is intentionally evaluated with its own mask.
        _a_exclusive=float(_cc.loc[_a_only_mask,"Avalue"].sum())
        _b_exclusive=float(_cc.loc[_b_only_mask,"Bvalue"].sum())
        core_cost_rows.append({"label":"단독 보유 품목 비용","A":_a_exclusive,"B":_b_exclusive,"delta":_b_exclusive-_a_exclusive})

    if not core_cost_rows:
        core_cost_rows=[{"label":"총 재고비용","A":cost_a,"B":cost_b,"delta":cost_b-cost_a}]
    _ccmax=max([max(abs(x["A"]),abs(x["B"])) for x in core_cost_rows]+[1.0])
    core_cost_html=[]
    for _i,_r in enumerate(core_cost_rows):
        _rowmax=max(abs(_r["A"]),abs(_r["B"]),1.0)
        _ap=100.0*abs(_r["A"])/_rowmax; _bp=100.0*abs(_r["B"])/_rowmax
        core_cost_html.append(
            f'<button type="button" class="cc-row{" active" if _i==0 else ""}" data-label="{html.escape(_r["label"],quote=True)}" data-a="{_r["A"]:.0f}" data-b="{_r["B"]:.0f}">'
            f'<span class="cc-name">{html.escape(_r["label"])}</span>'
            f'<span class="cc-side a"><b>A</b><i style="width:{_ap:.1f}%"></i><em>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(_r["A"]))}</em></span>'
            f'<span class="cc-side b"><b>B</b><i style="width:{_bp:.1f}%"></i><em>{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(_r["B"]))}</em></span>'
            '</button>'
        )
    core_cost_html=''.join(core_cost_html)
    _cc_default = core_cost_rows[0] if core_cost_rows else {"label":"총 재고비용","A":cost_a,"B":cost_b}
    core_cost_default_label = str(_cc_default.get("label","총 재고비용"))
    core_cost_default_a = float(_cc_default.get("A",0.0) or 0.0)
    core_cost_default_b = float(_cc_default.get("B",0.0) or 0.0)

    # ---- V16 · WHAT-IF visual recommendation ----
    _wf_work=rc_items.copy() if isinstance(rc_items,pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
    wf_base_rec = "A" if risk_a >= risk_b else "B"
    wf_other_rec = "B" if wf_base_rec=="A" else "A"
    wf_rec_items=[]
    wf_cause_share=0.0
    if not _wf_work.empty:
        for _c in ["A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events","A_qty","B_qty","unit_cost","demand_events","risk_diff_events"]:
            if _c not in _wf_work.columns: _wf_work[_c]=0.0
            _wf_work[_c]=pd.to_numeric(_wf_work[_c],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        if "display_name" not in _wf_work.columns:
            _wf_work["display_name"]=_wf_work.get("part_id",pd.Series(["수리부속"]*len(_wf_work))).astype(str)
        if wf_base_rec=="A":
            _wf_work["base_gap"] = (_wf_work["A_shortage_events"]-_wf_work["B_shortage_events"]).clip(lower=0)
            _wf_work["stock_gap"] = (_wf_work["B_qty"]-_wf_work["A_qty"]).clip(lower=0)
        else:
            _wf_work["base_gap"] = (_wf_work["B_shortage_events"]-_wf_work["A_shortage_events"]).clip(lower=0)
            _wf_work["stock_gap"] = (_wf_work["A_qty"]-_wf_work["B_qty"]).clip(lower=0)
        _wf_work["common_short"]=_wf_work[["A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events"]].min(axis=1)
        _mx_bg=max(float(_wf_work["base_gap"].max()),1.0); _mx_cs=max(float(_wf_work["common_short"].max()),1.0); _mx_dem=max(float(_wf_work["demand_events"].max()),1.0)
        _wf_work["rec_score"]=.55*(_wf_work["base_gap"]/_mx_bg)+.27*(_wf_work["common_short"]/_mx_cs)+.18*(_wf_work["demand_events"]/_mx_dem)
        _wf_rank=_wf_work[(_wf_work["base_gap"]>0)|(_wf_work["common_short"]>0)].sort_values(["rec_score","base_gap","common_short"],ascending=False).head(3)
        _total_cause=max(float(_wf_work["base_gap"].sum()),1e-9)
        _picked_cause=float(_wf_rank["base_gap"].sum()) if not _wf_rank.empty else 0.0
        wf_cause_share=max(0.0,min(100.0,100.0*_picked_cause/_total_cause)) if _total_cause>1e-9 else 0.0
        for _,_r in _wf_rank.iterrows():
            _add=int(max(1,min(2,round(float(_r.get("stock_gap",0.0))) if float(_r.get("stock_gap",0.0))>0 else 1)))
            wf_rec_items.append({"name":str(_r.get("display_name","수리부속")),"add":_add,"unit":float(_r.get("unit_cost",0.0)),"gap":float(_r.get("base_gap",0.0)),"common":float(_r.get("common_short",0.0))})
    wf_rec_cost=sum(x["unit"]*x["add"] for x in wf_rec_items)
    _wf_items_html=''.join(f'<div class="wf-move"><span>{html.escape(x["name"])}</span><i>+{x["add"]}</i></div>' for x in wf_rec_items) or '<div class="wf-empty">보강 후보 없음</div>'
    _wf_names=', '.join(x["name"] for x in wf_rec_items) if wf_rec_items else '보강 후보'
    _wf_result_sentence='10,000회 재시뮬레이션 전'
    _wf_reinforced_label=f'{wf_base_rec}+보강'
    if isinstance(_wf_verify,dict):
        _base=str(_wf_verify.get("base_label",wf_base_rec)).upper()
        _wf_reinforced_label=f'{_base}+보강'
        _before=_wf_verify.get("before",{}) if isinstance(_wf_verify.get("before"),dict) else {}
        _after=_wf_verify.get("after",{}) if isinstance(_wf_verify.get("after"),dict) else {}
        _before_s=float(_xai_num(_before.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))
        _after_s=float(_xai_num(_after.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))
        _before_ao=_xai_pct(_before.get("mean_Ao",0.0)); _after_ao=_xai_pct(_after.get("mean_Ao",0.0))
        _actual_cost=float(_xai_num(_wf_verify.get("additional_cost",0.0)))
        _risk_red=(100.0*(_before_s-_after_s)/_before_s) if _before_s>1e-9 else 0.0
        _wf_result_sentence=f'추가비용 {_xai_compact_money(_actual_cost)} · 평균 Ao {_after_ao-_before_ao:+.2f}%p · 부족위험 {_risk_red:+.1f}%'


    # ---- V24 · WHAT-IF visual: A/B independent reinforcement + manual add + animated risk/effect HUD ----
    def _wf_visual_side(_side: str):
        _w = rc_items.copy() if isinstance(rc_items, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
        if _w.empty:
            return [], 0.0, 0, 0.0
        for _c in ["A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events","A_qty","B_qty","unit_cost","demand_events","lead_time_h"]:
            if _c not in _w.columns:
                _w[_c] = 0.0
            _w[_c] = pd.to_numeric(_w[_c], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        if "display_name" not in _w.columns:
            _w["display_name"] = _w.get("part_id", pd.Series(["수리부속"]*len(_w))).astype(str)
        _own = _w[f"{_side}_shortage_events"]
        _other = _w["B_shortage_events" if _side == "A" else "A_shortage_events"]
        _w["own_short"] = _own
        _w["relative_gap"] = (_own - _other).clip(lower=0.0)
        _w["common_short"] = _w[["A_shortage_events","B_shortage_events"]].min(axis=1)
        _mx1=max(float(_w["own_short"].max()),1.0)
        _mx2=max(float(_w["relative_gap"].max()),1.0)
        _mx3=max(float(_w["common_short"].max()),1.0)
        _mx4=max(float(_w["demand_events"].max()),1.0)
        _mx5=max(float(_w["lead_time_h"].max()),1.0)
        _w["score"]=(.44*(_w["own_short"]/_mx1)+.22*(_w["relative_gap"]/_mx2)+.16*(_w["common_short"]/_mx3)+.11*(_w["demand_events"]/_mx4)+.07*(_w["lead_time_h"]/_mx5))
        _w=_w[(_w["own_short"]>0)|(_w["common_short"]>0)].sort_values(["score","own_short","demand_events"],ascending=False).head(3)
        _rows=[]; _extra=0.0
        for _j,(_, _r) in enumerate(_w.iterrows()):
            _add=2 if _j==0 else 1
            _unit=float(_r.get("unit_cost",0.0))
            _extra += _unit*_add
            _rows.append({
                "name":str(_r.get("display_name","수리부속")),
                "add":_add,
                "short":float(_r.get("own_short",0.0)),
                "lead_d":float(_r.get("lead_time_h",0.0))/24.0,
            })
        _all_short = max(float(_w["own_short"].sum()), 0.0)
        _sel_short = float(sum(float(x.get("short",0.0)) for x in _rows))
        _focus = 100.0 * _sel_short / _all_short if _all_short > 1e-9 else 0.0
        _add_qty = int(sum(int(x.get("add",0)) for x in _rows))
        return _rows,_extra,_add_qty,_focus

    _wfA_rows,_wfA_extra,_wfA_addqty,_wfA_focus=_wf_visual_side("A")
    _wfB_rows,_wfB_extra,_wfB_addqty,_wfB_focus=_wf_visual_side("B")

    def _wf_cards(_rows,_side):
        if not _rows:
            return '<div class="wf20-empty">후보 없음</div>'
        _html=[]
        for _rank,x in enumerate(_rows,start=1):
            _html.append(
                f'<div class="wf20-part" data-side="{_side}">'
                f'<span class="wf20-rank">{_rank}</span><span class="wf20-name">{html.escape(x["name"])}</span>'
                f'<b>+{int(x["add"])}</b><small>부족 {x["short"]:.0f} · {x["lead_d"]:.0f}일</small></div>'
            )
        return ''.join(_html)
    _wfA_html=_wf_cards(_wfA_rows,"A")
    _wfB_html=_wf_cards(_wfB_rows,"B")
    _wfA_names=" · ".join(html.escape(x["name"]) for x in _wfA_rows) if _wfA_rows else "-"
    _wfB_names=" · ".join(html.escape(x["name"]) for x in _wfB_rows) if _wfB_rows else "-"
    def _wf_short_value(_d: dict) -> float:
        # Keep the visual card numeric even when an old result contains None/text.
        _v = _xai_num(_d.get("mean_shortage_events", _d.get("mean_shortage_units", 0.0))) if isinstance(_d,dict) else 0.0
        try:
            _v = float(_v)
        except Exception:
            _v = 0.0
        return max(0.0, _v) if math.isfinite(_v) else 0.0
    _wfA_short_mean=_wf_short_value(A)
    _wfB_short_mean=_wf_short_value(B)

    def _wf_mini_risk(side: str) -> str:
        """V27 mini replay: actual RISK COVERAGE before/after reinforcement."""
        _side = str(side).upper()
        _base = sdf.copy() if isinstance(sdf, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
        _short_col = f"{_side}_shortage_events"
        if _base.empty or _short_col not in _base.columns:
            return '<div class="wfmini-empty">데이터 없음</div>'
        if "total_failures" not in _base.columns:
            _base["total_failures"] = np.arange(1, len(_base)+1, dtype=float)
        _base["total_failures"] = pd.to_numeric(_base["total_failures"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        _base[_short_col] = pd.to_numeric(_base[_short_col], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
        _den = _base["total_failures"].clip(lower=1.0)
        _base["BaseCoverage"] = (1.0 - _base[_short_col] / _den).clip(0.0, 1.0) * 100.0

        _wr = st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{_side}")
        _after = None
        if isinstance(_wr, dict) and isinstance(_wr.get("scenario_df"), pd.DataFrame):
            _wd = _wr["scenario_df"].copy()
            if "After_Risk_Coverage" not in _wd.columns and {"After_shortage_events","total_failures"}.issubset(_wd.columns):
                _d = pd.to_numeric(_wd["total_failures"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0).clip(lower=1.0)
                _s = pd.to_numeric(_wd["After_shortage_events"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
                _wd["After_Risk_Coverage"] = (1.0 - _s/_d).clip(0.0,1.0)
            if "After_Risk_Coverage" in _wd.columns:
                _wd["AfterCoverage"] = pd.to_numeric(_wd["After_Risk_Coverage"], errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
                if float(_wd["AfterCoverage"].max()) <= 1.5:
                    _wd["AfterCoverage"] *= 100.0
                if "simulation_no" in _wd.columns and "n" in _base.columns:
                    _bb = _base.copy(); _bb["n"] = pd.to_numeric(_bb["n"], errors="coerce")
                    _wd["simulation_no"] = pd.to_numeric(_wd["simulation_no"], errors="coerce")
                    _m = _bb.merge(_wd[["simulation_no","AfterCoverage"]], left_on="n", right_on="simulation_no", how="inner")
                else:
                    _m = _base.iloc[:min(len(_base),len(_wd))].copy()
                    _m["AfterCoverage"] = _wd["AfterCoverage"].iloc[:len(_m)].to_numpy()
                if not _m.empty:
                    _after = _m.sort_values("total_failures").reset_index(drop=True)

        _src = _after if isinstance(_after,pd.DataFrame) and not _after.empty else _base.sort_values("total_failures").reset_index(drop=True)
        _bins = min(22, max(8, len(_src)//250 if len(_src)>0 else 8))
        _ba=[]; _af=[]
        for _ch in np.array_split(_src, _bins):
            if _ch.empty: continue
            _ba.append(float(pd.to_numeric(_ch["BaseCoverage"],errors="coerce").median()))
            if "AfterCoverage" in _ch.columns:
                _af.append(float(pd.to_numeric(_ch["AfterCoverage"],errors="coerce").median()))
        _vals = _ba + (_af if _af else [])
        if not _vals:
            return '<div class="wfmini-empty">데이터 없음</div>'
        _lo=min(_vals); _hi=max(_vals)
        # Keep small improvements visually readable while retaining the numeric scale.
        if _hi-_lo < 0.40:
            _mid=(_hi+_lo)/2.0; _lo=max(0.0,_mid-.20); _hi=min(100.0,_mid+.20)
            if _hi-_lo < .20: _lo=max(0.0,_hi-.40)
        def _path(_v):
            _n=max(len(_v)-1,1); _pts=[]
            for _i,_y in enumerate(_v):
                _x=24+232*_i/_n; _yy=82-56*(_y-_lo)/max(_hi-_lo,1e-9)
                _pts.append(f"{_x:.1f},{_yy:.1f}")
            return "M "+" L ".join(_pts)
        _base_path=_path(_ba); _after_path=_path(_af) if _af else ""
        _accent="#59c9ff" if _side=="A" else "#ff9a60"
        _base_mean=float(np.mean(_ba)) if _ba else 0.0
        _after_mean=float(np.mean(_af)) if _af else _base_mean
        _delta=_after_mean-_base_mean
        _after_svg=(f'<path class="wfmini-after" d="{_after_path}" stroke="{_accent}" />' if _after_path else '')
        _delta_svg=(f'<g class="wfmini-delta"><rect x="166" y="89" width="90" height="17" rx="8"/><text x="211" y="101" text-anchor="middle">+{_delta:.2f}%p</text></g>' if _after_path else '')
        _status='' if _after_path else '<text x="140" y="103" text-anchor="middle" class="wfmini-wait">보강 실행 후 실제 Risk Coverage 재생</text>'
        _legend=(f'<g class="wfmini-legend"><line x1="145" y1="13" x2="163" y2="13" class="wfmini-base"/><text x="167" y="16">기존</text>'
                 + (f'<line x1="207" y1="13" x2="225" y2="13" class="wfmini-after" stroke="{_accent}"/><text x="229" y="16">보강</text>' if _after_path else '') + '</g>')
        return (f'<svg viewBox="0 0 280 112" class="wfmini-svg"><line x1="24" y1="88" x2="256" y2="88" class="wfmini-axis"/>'
                f'<line x1="24" y1="18" x2="24" y2="88" class="wfmini-axis"/>'
                f'<text x="28" y="16" class="wfmini-y">{_hi:.2f}%</text><text x="28" y="98" class="wfmini-y">{_lo:.2f}%</text>'
                f'{_legend}<path d="{_base_path}" class="wfmini-base"/>{_after_svg}{_delta_svg}{_status}</svg>')
    _wfA_mini=_wf_mini_risk("A")
    _wfB_mini=_wf_mini_risk("B")

    def _wf_effect(side: str) -> dict:
        _side=str(side).upper()
        _wr=st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{_side}")
        if not isinstance(_wr,dict):
            return {"active":False,"ao":"실행 전","risk":"실행 전","cost":"-","eff":"-"}
        _bef=_wr.get("before",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("before"),dict) else {}
        _aft=_wr.get("after",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("after"),dict) else {}
        _b_ao=_xai_pct(_bef.get("mean_Ao",0.0)); _a_ao=_xai_pct(_aft.get("mean_Ao",0.0))
        _b_r=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_bef.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))); _a_r=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_aft.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0))))
        _extra=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_wr.get("additional_cost",0.0))))
        _red=(100.0*(_b_r-_a_r)/_b_r) if _b_r>1e-9 else 0.0
        _eff=(_red/(_extra/1e8)) if _extra>1e-9 else 0.0
        return {"active":True,"ao":f"{_a_ao-_b_ao:+.2f}%p","risk":f"{_red:+.1f}%","cost":_xai_compact_money(_extra),"eff":f"{_eff:.1f}%/억원"}
    _wfEA=_wf_effect("A"); _wfEB=_wf_effect("B")
    def _wf_effect_html(_side: str, _e: dict) -> str:
        _cls='a' if _side=='A' else 'b'
        return (f'<div class="wfjarvis {_cls}"><div class="wfjarvis-title">{_side}+ 보강 효과</div>'
                f'<div><small>Ao 변화</small><b>{html.escape(str(_e["ao"]))}</b></div>'
                f'<div><small>부족위험 변화</small><b>{html.escape(str(_e["risk"]))}</b></div>'
                f'<div><small>추가비용</small><b>{html.escape(str(_e["cost"]))}</b></div>'
                f'<div><small>투자효율</small><b>{html.escape(str(_e["eff"]))}</b></div></div>')
    _wfEA_html=_wf_effect_html("A",_wfEA); _wfEB_html=_wf_effect_html("B",_wfEB)

    # ---- V26 · INVESTMENT VALUE ----
    # 실제 A/B 및 검증 완료된 A+/B+만 사용한다. 임의의 저투자/균형/과투자 구간은 만들지 않는다.
    def _inv_point(_label: str, _cost: float, _risk: float, _ao: float, _kind: str = "base") -> dict:
        return {"label":_label,"cost":max(0.0,float(_cost)),"risk":max(0.0,float(_risk)),"ao":float(_ao),"kind":_kind}

    inv_points=[_inv_point("A",cost_a,risk_a,ao_a,"A"),_inv_point("B",cost_b,risk_b,ao_b,"B")]
    for _side in ["A","B"]:
        _wr=st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{_side}")
        if not isinstance(_wr,dict):
            continue
        _aft=_wr.get("after",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("after"),dict) else {}
        _base_cost=cost_a if _side=="A" else cost_b
        _total=float(_xai_num(_wr.get("reinforced_stock_cost",0.0)))
        if _total<=0:
            _total=_base_cost+max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_wr.get("additional_cost",0.0))))
        inv_points.append(_inv_point(_side+"+",_total,float(_xai_num(_aft.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0))),_xai_pct(_aft.get("mean_Ao",0.0)),_side+"+"))

    _inv_costs=[p["cost"] for p in inv_points]; _inv_risks=[p["risk"] for p in inv_points]; _inv_aos=[p["ao"] for p in inv_points]
    _cmin,_cmax=min(_inv_costs),max(_inv_costs); _rmin,_rmax=min(_inv_risks),max(_inv_risks)
    _cr=max(_cmax-_cmin,1.0); _rr=max(_rmax-_rmin,1e-6)
    for _p in inv_points:
        _p["x"]=112.0+730.0*(_p["cost"]-_cmin)/_cr
        # shortage가 작을수록 화면 위쪽(좋은 방향)에 위치
        _p["y"]=292.0-190.0*(_p["risk"]-_rmin)/_rr
        _p["cost_eok"]=_p["cost"]/1e8

    # INVESTMENT FIELD 축은 최소 3개 수치만 표시
    _iv_x_ticks=[]
    for _frac in (0.0,0.5,1.0):
        _x=112.0+730.0*_frac
        _v=_cmin+(_cmax-_cmin)*_frac
        _iv_x_ticks.append(f'<text x="{_x:.1f}" y="329" text-anchor="middle" class="iv-axis">{html.escape(_xai_compact_money(_v))}</text>')
    _iv_y_ticks=[]
    for _frac in (0.0,0.5,1.0):
        _y=102.0+190.0*_frac
        _v=_rmax-(_rmax-_rmin)*_frac
        _iv_y_ticks.append(f'<text x="55" y="{_y+3:.1f}" text-anchor="end" class="iv-axis">{_v:.2f}회</text>')

    def _inv_color(_lab):
        return "#58ccff" if _lab.startswith("A") else "#ff9c63"
    _inv_nodes=[]
    for _p in inv_points:
        _col=_inv_color(_p["label"]); _plus=_p["label"].endswith("+")
        _rad=13 if _plus else 10
        _inv_nodes.append(f'<g class="iv-node" data-lab="{html.escape(_p["label"])}"><line x1="{_p["x"]:.1f}" y1="310" x2="{_p["x"]:.1f}" y2="{_p["y"]:.1f}" stroke="{_col}" stroke-opacity=".15"/><ellipse cx="{_p["x"]+5:.1f}" cy="312" rx="{_rad*1.4}" ry="4.5" fill="rgba(0,0,0,.28)"/><circle cx="{_p["x"]:.1f}" cy="{_p["y"]:.1f}" r="{_rad}" fill="{_col}" fill-opacity="{0.95 if _plus else 0.68}" stroke="#e7f8ff" stroke-width="{2.2 if _plus else 1.2}"/><text x="{_p["x"]:.1f}" y="{_p["y"]-18:.1f}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#edfaff" font-size="12" font-weight="900">{html.escape(_p["label"])}</text><title>{html.escape(_p["label"])} · {_xai_compact_money(_p["cost"])} · 부족 {_p["risk"]:.2f}회 · Ao {_p["ao"]:.2f}%</title></g>')
    _inv_paths=[]
    _pmap={p["label"]:p for p in inv_points}
    for _b,_a in [("A","A+"),("B","B+")]:
        if _b in _pmap and _a in _pmap:
            _p0,_p1=_pmap[_b],_pmap[_a]; _col=_inv_color(_b)
            _inv_paths.append(f'<path d="M{_p0["x"]:.1f},{_p0["y"]:.1f} C{(_p0["x"]+_p1["x"])/2:.1f},{_p0["y"]-28:.1f} {(_p0["x"]+_p1["x"])/2:.1f},{_p1["y"]+28:.1f} {_p1["x"]:.1f},{_p1["y"]:.1f}" fill="none" stroke="{_col}" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="5 5" opacity=".65"/>')

    # A/B의 실제 비용차이와 그에 동반된 위험/Ao 변화만 설명한다.
    if abs(cost_b-cost_a)<1e-9:
        _iv_summary="A와 B의 재고비용은 거의 같습니다. 부족발생과 Ao 차이를 함께 확인하십시오."
        _iv_from,_iv_to="A","B"
    else:
        _iv_from,_iv_to=("A","B") if cost_a<cost_b else ("B","A")
        _lo=_pmap[_iv_from]; _hi=_pmap[_iv_to]
        _dc=_hi["cost"]-_lo["cost"]; _dr=_hi["risk"]-_lo["risk"]; _da=_hi["ao"]-_lo["ao"]
        _risk_phrase=(f"평균 부족이 {abs(_dr):.2f}회 감소" if _dr<0 else f"평균 부족이 {abs(_dr):.2f}회 증가")
        _ao_phrase=(f"Ao가 {abs(_da):.2f}%p 상승" if _da>0 else f"Ao가 {abs(_da):.2f}%p 하락")
        _iv_summary=f"{_iv_to}는 {_iv_from}보다 {_xai_compact_money(_dc)} 추가 투자이며, {_risk_phrase}, {_ao_phrase}했습니다."

    _iv_max_cost=max(_inv_costs+[1.0]); _iv_max_risk=max(_inv_risks+[1e-9]); _iv_min_ao=min(_inv_aos); _iv_max_ao=max(_inv_aos); _iv_ao_span=max(_iv_max_ao-_iv_min_ao,0.01)
    _iv_rows=[]
    for _p in inv_points:
        _cw=max(4.0,100.0*_p["cost"]/_iv_max_cost)
        _rw=max(4.0,100.0*_p["risk"]/_iv_max_risk)
        _aw=max(4.0,100.0*(_p["ao"]-_iv_min_ao+0.08*_iv_ao_span)/(1.08*_iv_ao_span))
        _iv_rows.append(f'<div class="iv-metric-row"><b>{html.escape(_p["label"])}</b><div class="iv-mini"><span>비용</span><i><em class="cost" style="width:{_cw:.1f}%"></em></i><strong>{_xai_compact_money(_p["cost"])}</strong></div><div class="iv-mini"><span>부족</span><i><em class="risk" style="width:{_rw:.1f}%"></em></i><strong>{_p["risk"]:.2f}회</strong></div><div class="iv-mini"><span>Ao</span><i><em class="ao" style="width:{_aw:.1f}%"></em></i><strong>{_p["ao"]:.2f}%</strong></div></div>')

    def _iv_reinforce_flow(_side):
        _wr=st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{_side}")
        if not isinstance(_wr,dict):
            return f'<div class="iv-flow-empty">{_side}+는 WHAT-IF 보강 실행 후 실제 결과가 연결됩니다.</div>'
        _bef=_wr.get("before",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("before"),dict) else {}; _aft=_wr.get("after",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("after"),dict) else {}
        _extra=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_wr.get("additional_cost",0.0))))
        _br=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_bef.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))); _ar=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_aft.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0))))
        _ba=_xai_pct(_bef.get("mean_Ao",0.0)); _aa=_xai_pct(_aft.get("mean_Ao",0.0))
        _red=_br-_ar; _again=_aa-_ba
        _rw=min(100.0,abs(_red)/max(_br,1e-9)*100.0) if _br>0 else 0.0
        _aw=min(100.0,max(0.0,abs(_again))*18.0)
        return f'<div class="iv-flow"><div class="iv-flow-title">{_side} → {_side}+ 실제 보강</div><div class="iv-flow-step"><span>추가 투자</span><div class="iv-flow-bar"><i style="width:100%"></i></div><b>+{_xai_compact_money(_extra)}</b></div><div class="iv-arrow">→</div><div class="iv-flow-step"><span>평균 부족 변화</span><div class="iv-flow-bar risk"><i style="width:{max(3.0,_rw):.1f}%"></i></div><b>{_red:+.2f}회</b></div><div class="iv-arrow">→</div><div class="iv-flow-step"><span>Ao 변화</span><div class="iv-flow-bar ao"><i style="width:{max(3.0,_aw):.1f}%"></i></div><b>{_again:+.2f}%p</b></div></div>'
    _iv_flow_a=_iv_reinforce_flow("A"); _iv_flow_b=_iv_reinforce_flow("B")

    # ---- V34 · INVESTMENT VALUE · Ao 상승 확대뷰 (A→A+, B→B+) ----
    def _iv_ao_shift_card(_side: str) -> str:
        _wr=st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{_side}")
        _cls='a' if _side=='A' else 'b'
        if not isinstance(_wr,dict):
            return (f'<div class="iv-ao-card {_cls} wait"><div class="iv-ao-head"><div><span>{_side} → {_side}+</span>'
                    f'<b>운용가용도 Ao · 10,000회 시뮬레이션</b></div><small>WHAT-IF 검증 후 표시</small></div>'
                    f'<div class="iv-ao-empty">{_side}+ 보강 검증을 실행하면 기존 대비 Ao 분포 상승을 표시합니다.</div></div>')
        _sdf=_wr.get("scenario_df")
        if not isinstance(_sdf,pd.DataFrame) or _sdf.empty or "Before_Ao" not in _sdf.columns or "After_Ao" not in _sdf.columns:
            return (f'<div class="iv-ao-card {_cls} wait"><div class="iv-ao-head"><div><span>{_side} → {_side}+</span>'
                    f'<b>운용가용도 Ao · 10,000회 시뮬레이션</b></div><small>시나리오 없음</small></div>'
                    f'<div class="iv-ao-empty">Ao 시나리오 데이터가 없어 그래프를 표시할 수 없습니다.</div></div>')
        _before=pd.to_numeric(_sdf["Before_Ao"],errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float)*100.0
        _after=pd.to_numeric(_sdf["After_Ao"],errors="coerce").dropna().to_numpy(dtype=float)*100.0
        if len(_before)==0 or len(_after)==0:
            return f'<div class="iv-ao-card {_cls} wait"><div class="iv-ao-empty">Ao 시나리오 데이터가 없습니다.</div></div>'
        # 5~95% 구간 확대: 극단 꼬리 때문에 상승폭이 눌리지 않도록 함
        _qs=np.linspace(0.05,0.95,61)
        _bq=np.quantile(_before,_qs); _aq=np.quantile(_after,_qs)
        _ymin=float(min(np.min(_bq),np.min(_aq))); _ymax=float(max(np.max(_bq),np.max(_aq)))
        _span=max(_ymax-_ymin,0.02)
        _ypad=max(_span*0.055,0.012)
        _lo=max(0.0,_ymin-_ypad); _hi=min(100.0,_ymax+_ypad)
        if _hi-_lo<0.08:
            _mid=(_hi+_lo)/2.0; _lo=max(0.0,_mid-0.04); _hi=min(100.0,_mid+0.04)
        _x0,_x1,_y0,_y1=70.0,590.0,48.0,252.0
        def _pt(_i,_v):
            _x=_x0+(_x1-_x0)*_i/max(len(_qs)-1,1)
            _y=_y1-(_y1-_y0)*((_v-_lo)/max(_hi-_lo,1e-9))
            return _x,_y
        def _path(_vals):
            return ' '.join((('M' if i==0 else 'L')+f'{_pt(i,float(v))[0]:.1f},{_pt(i,float(v))[1]:.1f}') for i,v in enumerate(_vals))
        _bp=_path(_bq); _ap=_path(_aq)
        _area_pts=[_pt(i,float(v)) for i,v in enumerate(_aq)] + [_pt(i,float(v)) for i,v in reversed(list(enumerate(_bq)))]
        _area=' '.join((('M' if i==0 else 'L')+f'{x:.1f},{y:.1f}') for i,(x,y) in enumerate(_area_pts))+' Z'
        _bm=float(np.mean(_before)); _am=float(np.mean(_after)); _gain=_am-_bm
        _p05b=float(np.quantile(_before,.05)); _p05a=float(np.quantile(_after,.05)); _p05gain=_p05a-_p05b
        _ticks=[]
        for _j in range(5):
            _v=_hi-(_hi-_lo)*_j/4.0; _yy=_y0+(_y1-_y0)*_j/4.0
            _ticks.append(f'<line x1="{_x0:.0f}" y1="{_yy:.1f}" x2="{_x1:.0f}" y2="{_yy:.1f}" stroke="rgba(120,174,198,.11)"/><text x="58" y="{_yy+4:.1f}" text-anchor="end">{_v:.2f}%</text>')
        _col='#59c9ff' if _side=='A' else '#ff9a60'
        return f'''<div class="iv-ao-card {_cls}"><div class="iv-ao-head"><div><span>{_side} → {_side}+</span><b>운용가용도 Ao · 보강 전/후 10,000회 시뮬레이션</b></div><small>확대뷰 · 5~95% 시나리오</small></div>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 660 292" aria-label="{_side} 보강 전후 운용가용도 분포"><g class="iv-ao-grid">{''.join(_ticks)}</g><line x1="{_x0}" y1="{_y1}" x2="{_x1}" y2="{_y1}" stroke="#456a7b"/>
        <path d="{_area}" fill="{_col}" fill-opacity=".095" stroke="none"/>
        <path d="{_bp}" fill="none" stroke="#8da5b0" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-opacity=".44" stroke-dasharray="5 5"/>
        <path d="{_ap}" fill="none" stroke="{_col}" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
        <text x="{_x0}" y="276" fill="#779dad" font-size="9">불리한 미래</text><text x="{_x1}" y="276" fill="#779dad" font-size="9" text-anchor="end">유리한 미래</text>
        <g font-size="9"><line x1="414" y1="22" x2="434" y2="22" stroke="#8da5b0" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="5 4"/><text x="440" y="25" fill="#8da5b0">기존 {_side}</text><line x1="505" y1="22" x2="525" y2="22" stroke="{_col}" stroke-width="5"/><text x="531" y="25" fill="{_col}">보강 {_side}+</text></g></svg>
        <div class="iv-ao-hud"><div><small>평균 Ao 상승</small><b>{_gain:+.2f}%p</b></div><div><small>하위 5% Ao 상승</small><b>{_p05gain:+.2f}%p</b></div><div><small>시뮬레이션</small><b>{min(len(_before),len(_after)):,}회</b></div></div></div>'''
    _iv_ao_shift=f'<div class="iv-ao-gridwrap iv-ao-only">{_iv_ao_shift_card("A")}{_iv_ao_shift_card("B")}</div>'

    # ---- V27 · INVESTMENT VALUE intelligence layer ----
    def _iv_shortage_free_from_df(_side: str) -> float:
        _col=f"{_side}_shortage_events"
        if isinstance(sdf,pd.DataFrame) and _col in sdf.columns and len(sdf):
            _v=pd.to_numeric(sdf[_col],errors="coerce").fillna(0.0)
            return 100.0*float((_v<=0).mean())
        return 0.0
    _iv_sfA=_iv_shortage_free_from_df("A"); _iv_sfB=_iv_shortage_free_from_df("B")

    def _iv_after_stats(_side: str):
        _wr=st.session_state.get(f"xai_whatif_result_{_side}")
        if not isinstance(_wr,dict): return None
        _bef=_wr.get("before",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("before"),dict) else {}
        _aft=_wr.get("after",{}) if isinstance(_wr.get("after"),dict) else {}
        _extra=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_wr.get("additional_cost",0.0))))
        _br=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_bef.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0)))); _ar=max(0.0,float(_xai_num(_aft.get("mean_shortage_events",0.0))))
        _bc=100.0*float(_xai_num(_bef.get("mean_risk_coverage",0.0))); _ac=100.0*float(_xai_num(_aft.get("mean_risk_coverage",0.0)))
        _bsf=100.0*float(_xai_num(_bef.get("shortage_free_rate",0.0))); _asf=100.0*float(_xai_num(_aft.get("shortage_free_rate",0.0)))
        _bao=_xai_pct(_bef.get("mean_Ao",0.0)); _aao=_xai_pct(_aft.get("mean_Ao",0.0))
        _de=max(0.0,_br-_ar); _eok=_extra/1e8
        return {"extra":_extra,"br":_br,"ar":_ar,"bc":_bc,"ac":_ac,"bsf":_bsf,"asf":_asf,"bao":_bao,"aao":_aao,"de":_de,"eff":(_de/_eok if _eok>1e-9 else 0.0)}
    _ivWA=_iv_after_stats("A"); _ivWB=_iv_after_stats("B")

    _iv_compare_max=max(cost_a,cost_b,1.0)
    _iv_costA_w=100.0*cost_a/_iv_compare_max; _iv_costB_w=100.0*cost_b/_iv_compare_max
    _iv_rmax=max(risk_a,risk_b,1e-9); _iv_rA_w=100.0*risk_a/_iv_rmax; _iv_rB_w=100.0*risk_b/_iv_rmax
    _iv_amin=min(ao_a,ao_b); _iv_amax=max(ao_a,ao_b); _iv_asp=max(_iv_amax-_iv_amin,.01)
    _iv_aoA_w=max(8.0,100.0*(ao_a-_iv_amin+.08*_iv_asp)/(1.08*_iv_asp)); _iv_aoB_w=max(8.0,100.0*(ao_b-_iv_amin+.08*_iv_asp)/(1.08*_iv_asp))
    _iv_scan = f'''<div class="iv-scan"><div class="iv-scan-title"><span>AI VALUE SCAN</span><b>A/B 실제 결과 동시 해석</b></div>
    <div class="iv-scan-row"><span>재고 투자비용</span><div class="iv-dual"><i class="a" style="width:{_iv_costA_w:.1f}%"></i><i class="b" style="width:{_iv_costB_w:.1f}%"></i></div><em>A {_xai_compact_money(cost_a)} · B {_xai_compact_money(cost_b)}</em></div>
    <div class="iv-scan-row"><span>평균 부족</span><div class="iv-dual"><i class="a" style="width:{_iv_rA_w:.1f}%"></i><i class="b" style="width:{_iv_rB_w:.1f}%"></i></div><em>A {risk_a:.2f}회 · B {risk_b:.2f}회</em></div>
    <div class="iv-scan-row"><span>평균 Ao</span><div class="iv-dual"><i class="a" style="width:{_iv_aoA_w:.1f}%"></i><i class="b" style="width:{_iv_aoB_w:.1f}%"></i></div><em>A {ao_a:.2f}% · B {ao_b:.2f}%</em></div></div>'''

    _iv_sfmax=max(_iv_sfA,_iv_sfB,1.0)
    _iv_scenario = f'''<div class="iv-scenario"><div class="iv-sub">10,000 SCENARIO SIGNAL · 부족 없는 미래</div>
    <div class="iv-sf-row"><b>A</b><div><i class="a" style="width:{_iv_sfA:.2f}%"></i></div><span>{_iv_sfA:.2f}%</span></div>
    <div class="iv-sf-row"><b>B</b><div><i class="b" style="width:{_iv_sfB:.2f}%"></i></div><span>{_iv_sfB:.2f}%</span></div></div>'''

    def _iv_eff_block(_side,_st):
        if not isinstance(_st,dict):
            return f'<div class="iv-eff wait"><b>{_side}+</b><span>WHAT-IF 실행 후 투자효율 자동 계산</span></div>'
        _mx=max((_ivWA or {}).get("eff",0.0),(_ivWB or {}).get("eff",0.0),1e-9)
        _w=100.0*_st["eff"]/_mx
        _cov=max(0.0,_st["ac"]-_st["bc"])
        _sf=max(0.0,_st["asf"]-_st["bsf"])
        return (f'<div class="iv-eff"><div class="iv-eff-head"><b>{_side} → {_side}+</b><span>+{_xai_compact_money(_st["extra"])}</span></div>'
                f'<div class="iv-eff-bar"><i class="{_side.lower()}" style="width:{max(3.0,_w):.1f}%"></i></div>'
                f'<div class="iv-eff-grid"><span>1억원당 평균 부족 감소 <b>{_st["eff"]:.2f}회</b></span><span>Risk Coverage <b>+{_cov:.2f}%p</b></span><span>부족 없는 미래 <b>+{_sf:.2f}%p</b></span></div></div>')
    _iv_eff_html=_iv_eff_block("A",_ivWA)+_iv_eff_block("B",_ivWB)

    if isinstance(_ivWA,dict) or isinstance(_ivWB,dict):
        _cand=[]
        if isinstance(_ivWA,dict): _cand.append(("A+",_ivWA["eff"],_ivWA))
        if isinstance(_ivWB,dict): _cand.append(("B+",_ivWB["eff"],_ivWB))
        _best=max(_cand,key=lambda x:x[1]) if _cand else None
        if _best and _best[1]>0:
            _iv_ai_sentence=(f'{_best[0]} 보강은 현재 실행 결과 중 투자 1억원당 평균 부족을 {_best[1]:.2f}회 줄여 가장 큰 보강효율을 보였습니다. '
                             f'이 판단은 동일 미래 10,000회 재시뮬레이션 결과입니다.')
        else:
            _iv_ai_sentence='보강은 실행됐지만 현재 조건에서는 추가 투자 대비 평균 부족 감소가 크지 않습니다.'
    else:
        _iv_ai_sentence='WHAT-IF에서 A 또는 B를 보강 실행하면 추가 투자금이 실제 Risk Coverage와 부족 없는 미래를 얼마나 바꾸는지 자동 계산합니다.'
    _iv_ai_html=f'<div class="iv-ai"><span>AI INTERPRETATION</span><p>{html.escape(_iv_ai_sentence)}</p><div class="iv-ai-pulse"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></div></div>'
    def _iv_download_btn(_label: str, _df: pd.DataFrame, _sheet: str, _filename: str, _cls: str = "") -> str:
        _b64 = _xai_inventory_xlsx_b64(_df, _sheet)
        if not _b64:
            return f'<span class="iv-dl disabled {_cls}"><b>{html.escape(_label)}</b><small>WHAT-IF 실행 후</small></span>'
        return (f'<a class="iv-dl {_cls}" href="data:application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet;base64,{_b64}" '
                f'download="{html.escape(_filename)}"><b>{html.escape(_label)}</b><small>{len(_df):,}종 · XLSX</small></a>')

    _iv_downloads = (
        '<div class="iv-downloads"><div class="iv-dl-head"><div><span>SPARE PARTS DATA</span><b>A / A+ / B / B+ 수리부속 목록</b></div><small>통합결과 상세보기 수준 · XLSX</small></div><div class="iv-dl-grid">'
        + _iv_download_btn("A", inv_a, "A_Inventory", "ExplainableAI_A_수리부속목록.xlsx", "a")
        + _iv_download_btn("A+", inv_ap, "Aplus_Inventory", "ExplainableAI_Aplus_수리부속목록.xlsx", "a plus")
        + _iv_download_btn("B", inv_b, "B_Inventory", "ExplainableAI_B_수리부속목록.xlsx", "b")
        + _iv_download_btn("B+", inv_bp, "Bplus_Inventory", "ExplainableAI_Bplus_수리부속목록.xlsx", "b plus")
        + '</div></div>'
    )

a+(it?Number(it.unit_cost||0)*(map[ci]||0):0)},0),tot=Math.max(1,items.reduce((a,x)=>a+own(x,side),0)),cover=Math.min(100,100*rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+own(x,side),0)/tot);if($('wf29Count'))$('wf29Count').textContent=cis.length+'종';if($('wf29Units'))$('wf29Units').textContent='+'+units+'개';if($('wf29Cost'))$('wf29Cost').textContent=money(cost);if($('wf29Cover'))$('wf29Cover').textContent=cover.toFixed(0)+'%';if($('wf29CoverBar'))$('wf29CoverBar').style.width=cover+'%';}function adjustments(s){return Object.entries(selected[s]).map(([ci,q])=>({candidate_index:Number(ci),add_units:Number(q)})).filter(x=>x.add_units>0);}function savedState(){return{side,A:adjustments('A'),B:adjustments('B')}}function emit(v){window.parent.postMessage({type:'xai:setPanel',panel:'whatif'},'*');if($('wf29Status'))$('wf29Status').textContent='SIMULATING · 10,000';document.querySelectorAll('.wf29-actions button').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);window.parent.postMessage({type:'xai:componentValue',value:Object.assign({nonce:Date.now(),saved:savedState()},v)},'*');}document.querySelectorAll('.wf29-side').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{side=b.dataset.side;renderLists();}));if($('wf29Search'))$('wf29Search').addEventListener('input',renderLists);if($('wf29RunSide'))$('wf29RunSide').addEventListener('click',()=>{let a=adjustments(side);if(a.length)emit({action:'run',sides:[side],adjustments:{[side]:a}});});if($('wf29RunBoth'))$('wf29RunBoth').addEventListener('click',()=>{let A=adjustments('A'),B=adjustments('B');if(A.length||B.length)emit({action:'run',sides:['A','B'],adjustments:{A,B}});});hydrate();renderLists();})();</script>'''.replace('__WFDATA__',_wf_json)
    html_block=html_block.replace('</body>',_interaction_script+'</body>',1)

    # V30 · WHAT-IF 실행 후 rerun되어도 VISUAL INFORM 04 탭을 유지한다.
    _xai_active_panel=str(st.session_state.get('xai_visual_active_panel','core') or 'core').lower()
    if _xai_active_panel=='whatif':
        html_block=html_block.replace('class="mode active" data-p="core"','class="mode" data-p="core"',1)
        html_block=html_block.replace('class="mode" data-p="whatif"','class="mode active" data-p="whatif"',1)
        html_block=html_block.replace('<section id="core" class="panel active">','<section id="core" class="panel">',1)
        html_block=html_block.replace('<section id="whatif" class="panel">','<section id="whatif" class="panel active">',1)

    _component=_xai_visual_component_callable()
    _cmd=_component(html=html_block,height=1870,key='xai_visual_inform_v33',default=None)
    if isinstance(_cmd,dict) and _cmd.get('nonce') is not None:
        _nonce=str(_cmd.get('nonce'))
        if _nonce != str(st.session_state.get('xai_visual_last_nonce','')):
            st.session_state['xai_visual_last_nonce']=_nonce
            st.session_state['xai_visual_active_panel']='whatif'
            if isinstance(_cmd.get('saved'),dict): st.session_state['xai_wf_component_saved']=_cmd.get('saved')
            _mc=st.session_state.get('risk_simulation_result') if isinstance(st.session_state.get('risk_simulation_result'),dict) else {}
            _cfgd=_mc.get('config',{}) if isinstance(_mc.get('config'),dict) else {}
            _cfg=MonteCarloConfig(iterations=int(_cfgd.get('iterations',10000)),random_seed=int(_cfgd.get('random_seed',42)),stress_factor=float(_cfgd.get('stress_factor',1.10)),failure_rate_lognormal_sigma=float(_cfgd.get('failure_rate_lognormal_sigma',0.0)))
            _sig=_xai_policy_signature_from_mc(_mc);_sides=[str(x).upper() for x in (_cmd.get('sides') or []) if str(x).upper() in {'A','B'}];_adjmap=_cmd.get('adjustments',{}) if isinstance(_cmd.get('adjustments'),dict) else {};_ok=[];_errs=[]
            st.session_state['xai_suppress_risk_request_until']=time.time()+8.0
            for _side in _sides:
                _adjs=_adjmap.get(_side,[]) if isinstance(_adjmap.get(_side,[]),list) else []
                if not _adjs: continue
                try:
                    _wr=run_multi_item_stock_whatif(result,a_key,b_key,_side,_adjs,_cfg);_base=cost_a if _side=='A' else cost_b;_other=cost_b if _side=='A' else cost_a
                    _wr['_xai_policy_sig']=_sig;_wr['base_stock_cost']=_base;_wr['other_stock_cost']=_other;_wr['reinforced_stock_cost']=_base+float(_wr.get('additional_cost',0.0) or 0.0)
                    st.session_state[f'xai_whatif_result_{_side}']=_wr;st.session_state['xai_whatif_result']=_wr;st.session_state['xai_whatif_policy_sig']=_sig
                    _bef=_wr.get('before',{}) if isinstance(_wr.get('before'),dict) else {};_aft=_wr.get('after',{}) if isinstance(_wr.get('after'),dict) else {};_ok.append(f"{_side}+ Risk Coverage {float(_bef.get('mean_risk_coverage',0))*100:.2f}%→{float(_aft.get('mean_risk_coverage',0))*100:.2f}%")
                except Exception as _exc: _errs.append(f"{_side}: {type(_exc).__name__}: {_exc}")
            st.session_state['xai_whatif_last_message']=' · '.join(_ok);st.session_state['xai_whatif_last_error']=' | '.join(_errs)
            # V33: WHAT-IF 검증 후 전체 앱이 아니라 Explainable AI fragment만 갱신한다.
            # 현재 04 WHAT-IF 탭과 브라우저 스크롤 위치가 첫 화면으로 튀는 현상을 줄인다.
            try:
                st.rerun(scope="fragment")
            except Exception:
                try: st.rerun()
                except Exception: pass



def _render_xai_body(result: dict | None, df_input: pd.DataFrame | None):
    st.markdown("### Explainable AI")
    if st.button("최신 시뮬레이션 결과 불러오기", key="xai_manual_refresh", help="자동 새로고침 없이 이 탭만 최신 완료 결과로 갱신합니다."):
        pass
    if not result:
        st.info("먼저 NSGA 분석을 실행해야 합니다.")
        return
    payload, payload_error = _xai_native_payload(result)
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        st.info("리스크 분석 시뮬레이션을 완료한 뒤 최신 결과를 불러오면 Explainable AI가 표시됩니다.")
        return
    A=dict(payload.get("A",{}));B=dict(payload.get("B",{}));scenario=payload.get("scenario",[]) if isinstance(payload.get("scenario"),list) else [];items=payload.get("item_points",[]) if isinstance(payload.get("item_points"),list) else []
    idf=_xai_prepare_item_df(items,df_input)
    active_mc = st.session_state.get("risk_simulation_result")
    whatif_was_reset = _xai_reset_whatif_if_policy_changed(active_mc)
    if whatif_was_reset:
        st.caption("새로운 A/B 대안으로 시뮬레이션한 결과입니다. 이전 What-if 보강선은 자동으로 초기화했습니다.")
    # V29: WHAT-IF controls are fully inside the bidirectional VISUAL INFORM component.
    # No native Streamlit input widget is exposed to the user.
    _xai_render_mission_briefing(result, A, B, payload, scenario, idf)
    if payload_error:
        st.caption("품목 상세 연결 일부를 보완하여 표시했습니다. A/B 및 시뮬레이션 결과는 정상적으로 반영되어 있습니다.")
if _fragment_decorator is not None:
    render_xai = _fragment_decorator(_render_xai_body)
else:
    render_xai = _render_xai_body


def render_prescriptive(result: dict | None, df_input: pd.DataFrame | None):
    st.markdown("### Prescriptive AI")
    if not result:
        st.info("아직 실행 결과가 없습니다. 먼저 NSGA 분석을 실행해야 합니다.")
        return

    result = _selected_solution_result_for_ai(result)
    st.caption(_selected_solution_ai_caption(result))
    result["xai"] = build_explainability_tables_v4(result, df_input)
    prescriptive = build_prescriptive_package(result, df_input)
    action_df = prescriptive.get("action_df", pd.DataFrame())
    summary = prescriptive.get("summary", {})
    narrative = prescriptive.get("narrative", "")

    c1, c2, c3, c4, c5, c6 = st.columns(6)
    with c1: show_metric_card("총 추천 액션 수", f"{_safe_int(summary.get('total_actions', 0)):,}")
    with c2: show_metric_card("즉시 실행 권고 수", f"{_safe_int(summary.get('immediate_actions', 0)):,}")
    with c3: show_metric_card("추가 검토 수", f"{_safe_int(summary.get('review_actions', 0)):,}")
    with c4: show_metric_card("유지/모니터링 수", f"{_safe_int(summary.get('monitor_actions', 0)):,}")
    with c5: show_metric_card("Watchlist 승격 후보 수", f"{_safe_int(summary.get('watchlist_candidates', 0)):,}")
    with c6: show_metric_card("보류 후보 수", f"{_safe_int(summary.get('deferred_actions', 0)):,}")

    st.markdown(f"<div class='section-box'>{clean_chart_text(narrative)}</div>", unsafe_allow_html=True)

    st.markdown("#### 정책 추천 카드")
    cards = summary.get("policy_cards", [])
    for i in range(0, len(cards), 3):
        row_cards = cards[i:i + 3]
        cols = st.columns(len(row_cards))
        for col, card in zip(cols, row_cards):
            with col:
                st.markdown(
                    f"""<div class="section-box">
                    <div style="font-size:1.02rem;font-weight:800;color:#1b4e2a;">{_safe_text(card.get('title'))}</div>
                    <div style="font-size:1.55rem;font-weight:800;color:#1e4f2b;margin:8px 0;">{_safe_int(card.get('count')):,}건</div>
                    <div style="color:#3d5f45;line-height:1.75;"><b>대표 이유</b><br>{_safe_text(card.get('reason'))}<br><br>
                    <b>예상 효과</b><br>{_safe_text(card.get('effect'))}</div></div>""",
                    unsafe_allow_html=True,
                )

    st.markdown("#### Prescriptive Action Table")
    st.dataframe(action_df, use_container_width=True, height=520) if not action_df.empty else st.info("표시할 Prescriptive action_df 없음")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Engine runner
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def call_engine(df: pd.DataFrame, config: NSGAConfig, progress_callback=None) -> dict:
    sig = inspect.signature(run_nsga2)
    kwargs = {}
    if "progress_callback" in sig.parameters:
        kwargs["progress_callback"] = progress_callback
    if "config" in sig.parameters:
        result = run_nsga2(df, config=config, **kwargs)
    else:
        kwargs2 = {key: value for key, value in config.__dict__.items() if key in sig.parameters}
        kwargs2.update(kwargs)
        result = run_nsga2(df, **kwargs2)
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        raise ValueError("엔진이 dict 형태 결과를 반환하지 않았습니다.")
    return result
